# Engine feature backlog

The detail behind the engine items in `BACKLOG.md` (the repo-root list of
everything still to do). This file ranks engine features by how many real
cards each would unblock, from two measured populations:

1. **The commander gap** (current priority): `top-commanders.txt` and the
   per-commander triage `top-commanders-gaps.json`, ranked by
   `npm run cmdrs:gaps -w engine`.
2. **The card backlog**: `top-commander-cards.txt` (the top 5000 Commander
   cards by EDHREC rank, refetched 2026-09-26, cross-referenced against `cards/pool/` — see
   `engine/scripts/top-commander-cards.mjs`).
   **Refresh the `[x]` marks with `npm run cards:mark -w engine`** after
   authoring; that re-marks in place and leaves the ranking snapshot alone.

Finished passes are indexed at the end, compactly, because source comments
cite them ("needed-cards P16", "Tier-1 feature"). Their narrative is in
`git log -- engine/src/cards/neededCards-features.md`. Read `cards/AUTHORING.md`
before authoring any card; this file is a priority list, not a how-to.

---

## The commander gap

(Card authoring's priority is now the Tarkir: Dragonstorm precons' missing cards, then the
top-5000 list — `BACKLOG.md`, "Card backlog". The commander numbers below date from 2026-09-25.)

**256 of the 500 most-played commanders are implemented** (2026-09-25).
Almost any real decklist someone imports still has its commander substituted,
which replaces the one card the deck is built around. `BACKLOG.md` carries
the current top of the build order; `cmdrs:gaps` has the live numbers.


### Why the pool missed them

Both existing backlogs rank by **how often a card is played across Commander
decks** — Scryfall's `order=edhrec`. That is the wrong measure for a
commander, and not slightly: a commander is a singleton in its own deck and
is played almost nowhere else, so its *card* rank stays low however popular
the deck is. Measured rather than assumed:

> Of EDHREC's 100 most-played commanders, **only 47 appear anywhere in the
> top 500 commander-legal cards by card rank.** Edgar Markov, The Ur-Dragon,
> Krenko, Kaalia, Yuriko and ~50 others are absent outright.

So no amount of working down `top-commander-cards.txt` would have fixed this.
It is a different population, and it needs its own list.

### The list

**`top-commanders.txt`** — the top 500 commanders by EDHREC `num_decks`: how
many decks run that card **as their commander**. Generated by
`npm run cmdrs:top -w engine`; re-mark after authoring with
`npm run cmdrs:mark -w engine` (no network, leaves the ranking snapshot put,
exactly like `cards:mark`).

Source caveat, recorded because it was a real decision: the ranking comes
from `json.edhrec.com`, which is **not a documented API** — the same
objection that kept the card replacer off Scryfall Tagger's GraphQL. It is
used here because nothing else publishes commander-specific deck counts, and
because this is a *build-time* dependency producing a checked-in file: if the
endpoint changes, the list still works and only a re-fetch breaks. Names,
costs and type lines still come from Scryfall. Partner pairs, which EDHREC
files under a joined "A // B" name that looks exactly like a double-faced
card, are told apart by asking Scryfall (a DFC resolves, a pair doesn't) and
credited to each half.

### What it takes — measured, not screened

The first version of this section read a keyword screen as "authoring, not
engine work". **That was wrong.** On 2026-09-22 every unimplemented entry was
triaged individually. One agent per eight commanders read each card's Oracle
text and rulings against the engine, and named clause by clause what it
would need. The ad-hoc gap names were then normalized onto one vocabulary of
245 engine features. The result is checked in as
**`top-commanders-gaps.json`** (commander → features, feature → size / needs
client UI / what to build). **`npm run cmdrs:gaps -w engine`** re-ranks it,
leaving out commanders already marked implemented and features listed in its
`built` array. So record a feature there when it lands, and the numbers stay
current.

Of the 469 still missing when the snapshot was taken:

| features still needed | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5+ |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| commanders | 29 | 85 | 124 | 112 | 71 | 48 |

- **~94% need engine work, but it's a long tail of small primitives, not a
  few big mechanics.** No feature blocks more than 22 of them, and even the
  most common rarely block one alone. The blockers are unglamorous: a static
  scope that takes a filter, last-known information, "another target",
  dynamic comparisons in filters, per-turn counters. This is the Tier 1b
  lesson again, at the scale of the whole commander list.
- **Greedy engine-only order.** Repeatedly build the feature that fully
  unblocks the most commanders per unit of effort (small 1, medium 3,
  large 10), leaving out anything that needs client UI. That unblocks about
  **75 commanders with 40 features**, most of them small. `cmdrs:gaps` prints
  the current order.

The live per-feature ranking (needed by, sole blocker, size, needs client
UI) and the greedy order are `cmdrs:gaps`' output; each feature's "what to
build" — including what is already built of a partly shipped one — is its
`description` in the JSON. The commanders authored and then dropped by their
reviews carry a `note` there, and are listed in `BACKLOG.md`.

### Suggested order

1. **Build down `cmdrs:gaps`' engine-only order**, authoring each
   commander a feature unblocks in the same commit, each with an
   adversarial rules review.
2. **Then the UI-bound features.** Ward payment (choosing new targets for a
   copy landed 2026-09-30), "may sacrifice — when you do", choosing a permanent, and entering
   attacking with a chosen defender each need a new decision the client
   renders. See also `docs/plans/token-stack-choices.md`.

## Open: the card backlog

Ranked from a keyword screen of the top 2000 taken on 2026-09-12. The screen
itself is retired: the card sweeps' per-card triage replaced it ("Card sweep
1" below, and `engine/data/sweep-2/`). Tier 1 and the Tier 1b primitives are
done (see the index at the end). Measure before picking the next one: `node
scripts/top-commander-cards.mjs --count 2000 --out <throwaway> --cache-json
<path>` gives current Oracle text for the whole list without touching the
checked-in snapshot.

### Tier 2 — solid value, smaller or more speculative

- ~~**Spree**~~ *(built — five Spree cards in the pool; Return the Favor and Great Train Heist wait on other features, BACKLOG's engine rules gaps)*.
- **Class enchantments** — **built 2026-10-10** (rule 716: `classLevel(n, cost)`, `atLevel(n, ability)`, the `class-level-gained` trigger; batch 45, 13 cards — 22 were waiting). The 9 left each need a clause of their own (`B45.json`).

### Tier 3 — real but niche, or a big lift for a small current payoff

- **Station** (7 cards, mostly newest-set/low-rank: Exploration Broodship, Evendo, Uthros) — a whole new subsystem (a permanent sub-type + counter-threshold-gated text tiers, keyed off what's tapped to fund it). Large build, currently low return — revisit once more Station cards enter the format.
- **Discover** (4), **Reconfigure** (3) — each a distinct, self-contained alt-cast/alt-ability shape; neither shares much with the other or with Tier 1/2. Cherry-pick opportunistically. (**Evoke** is built for mana costs, 2026-09-29; the Incarnations' exile-a-card evoke cost is BACKLOG's.)
- **Phasing** (8 cards, headlined by **Teferi's Protection** at #109) — explicitly out of scope per this file's parent (CLAUDE.md's "Not modeled" list). High-profile but a genuinely large state-machine addition (a whole not-really-a-zone permanent status); revisit only as a deliberate scope change, not opportunistically.
- **Vehicles/crew** — **built 2026-10-09** (`crew(n)`, rule 702.122; batch 38, 24 cards — it led the triage records with 38 cards waiting). Left: "becomes crewed" triggers, "crews as though its power were greater" and "crewed it this turn".
- **Casting during resolution** — **mostly built 2026-10-09** (`cast-now`'s `copies`, rule 707.12/704.5e; any number from among exiled cards; a top X amount; batch 41, 14 cards). Left, each its own key (`B41.json`): search to exile then cast (4 cards), an alternative cost, another player casting, the command zone, total mana value, any mana type, the discarded card.
- **Ninjutsu** — **built 2026-10-09** (`ninjutsu(cost)`, `commanderNinjutsu(cost)`, rule 702.49; batch 43, 15 cards — 23 were waiting). Left: a static granting ninjutsu to cards in a hand and "whenever you activate a ninjutsu ability" (Satoru Umezawa), "enters from your hand" (Thousand-Faced Shadow).
- **Rooms** — **built 2026-10-09** (rule 709.5: `roomCard`, `unlockThisDoor`, the `unlock-door` special action and effect; batch 44, 13 cards — 20 were waiting). The 7 left each need a clause of their own (`B44.json`).
- **Dungeons/Initiative** (3), **split/aftermath layout** (4), **Battle cards** (2) — each explicitly out of scope already; low card counts in the top 2000 confirm they're not worth a scope change yet.
- **Backgrounds** (3) are no longer blocked as a mechanic: the pairing is built (`pairing: { kind: "choose-a-background" }`) and one Background, Raised by Giants, is authored. What's left of the three comes down to each card's own text.

### Still open inside finished features

- **Overload**: Mizzix's Mastery, Eldritch Immunity, Winds of Abandon (a
  different effect shape each).
- **Conditional free-cast**: Deflecting Swat (targeting a stack *ability* and
  a change-the-target effect); Etali, Primal Storm (multi-card impulse +
  cast-any-number).
- **Damage doubling** is built (`would-deal-damage`): Twinflame Tyrant, City
  on Fire and Fiery Emancipation were authored 2026-09-29. Solphim still
  waits on "Discard two cards" as a cost.
- **Convoke**: Clever Concealment (phasing).
- **Additional costs**: an optional, repeatable cost — Plumb the Forbidden
  and Dargo ("sacrifice one or more creatures") are built
  (`copyForEachSacrificed`, `sacrificeReduces`). Redirect Lightning needs a
  change-the-target effect.
- **Global land-type statics**: Urborg, Tomb of Yawgmoth and Yavimaya, Cradle
  of Growth — `effectiveSubtypes` is deliberately given no `GameState`, so a
  board-dependent land subtype touches check lands and every `subtype`
  clause.
- **Remaining `StaticCondition`s**: Coven and Raid (no card whose other text
  is expressible yet).
- **Protection from a filter** (19 cards — Mother of Runes, Giver of Runes,
  the Sword cycle): `protection: {colors, types}` widening to a `CardFilter`.
  The One Ring and Teferi's Protection give protection to a *player* instead.
- **Cards E3 skipped** for want of a primitive and still unauthored:
  Ponder (put cards back in any order), The One Ring (protection for a
  player), Urza's Saga. (Chaos Warp's `shuffle-into-library` was built
  2026-10-02.)

### Modal triggers with targeted modes — built 2026-09-26

**Built** as `ModeOption.targets` on an `announced` modal effect: the chosen
modes bring their targets as the ability goes on the stack, a mode needing a
target it can't have isn't offered (rule 603.3c), and each mode reads its own
slice of the targets as it resolves (`withTargetSlice`), so a mode whose
target has gone still does the rest while an all-illegal ability does nothing
(608.2b). "Choose one that hasn't been chosen this turn" now works announced
too, recorded as each copy goes on the stack. Measured before: 32 missing
top-5000 cards. Shipped: Hullbreaker Horror, Junji, Aether Channeler, Retreat
to Coralhelm, Charming Prince, Silverback Elder, Retreat to Kazandu, Dread
Presence, Pip-Boy 3000, Voracious Hydra (`double-counters` gained a
`counter` kind), Retreat to Hagra, Shambling Ghast, Tiller Engine, Oltec
Matterweaver, Kykar, Glorious Sunrise, and the commanders Ayula, Queen Among
Bears, Jin Sakai, Ghost of Tsushima and Galadriel, Light of Valinor, with
Breeches and Parapet Thrasher. Still blocked among the 32: shield counters
(Titan of Industry), countering an ability (Ertai Resurrected), "remove up to
three counters" of the player's choice (Glissa Sunslayer), a mode never chosen
before, not just this turn (Silent Hallcreeper), mana spent as though any
colour (Grenzo), a "life they lost this turn" read for a *target* player
(Astarion — `turnStat` has no target scope), modes that must target
different players (Shadrix), Role tokens (Charming Scoundrel), a
nonartifact-spell mana restriction (The Mightstone and Weakstone), a card
shuffled in from the graveyard (Kogla and Yidaro), and Caesar's attacking
tokens. Modal *activated* abilities (Breya, Koma, Umezawa's Jitte) are
`effect:modal-activated-targeted-modes` in the gaps file.

### Host triggers — built 2026-09-26

**Built** as the `"attached"` `TriggerWho`: "whenever **equipped** creature
…", "whenever **enchanted** creature / land …" — the Equipment's or Aura's
host as the event happened, whoever controls it, with the trigger still the
attachment's and its controller's. A host that died is matched by the link
the attachment still has until the next state-based check (704.5n); an
attachment that left alongside it remembers its host
(`LastKnownInfo.attachedTo`, rule 603.10a). Measured before it was built: 75
missing top-5000 cards. It shipped with Skullclamp, Wild Growth (a triggered
mana ability, so the auto-payer counts it), Sword of the Animist, Sword of
Feast and Famine, Sword of Fire and Ice, Sword of Light and Shadow,
Curiosity, Ophidian Eye, Aqueous Form, Mask of Memory, Goldvein Pick,
Beamtown Beatstick, Argentum Armor, Explorer's Scope and Sticky Fingers.

A second batch followed the same day: Winged Boots, Bloodforged Battle-Axe,
Super State, Sword of Truth and Justice (its "a creature you control" is a
resolution-time `choose-permanents`), Kaya's Ghostform, Keen Sense,
Overgrowth, Wolfwillow Haven, Rogue's Gloves, Zephyr Boots, Sword of Body and
Mind, Sword of Sinew and Steel, Sword of War and Peace, Quietus Spike, Ultima
Weapon, Staff of Titania, Wand of Orcus, Adaptive Omnitool, Bilbo's Ring, The
Spear of Leonidas, Fiendlash, Sigil of Sleep, Resurrection Orb and Mage Slayer
(an attack trigger's `toTriggerRecipient` is now the player or planeswalker
attacked). 28 of the 75 are still blocked (34 then; Pip-Boy 3000, Shiny Impetus, Sword of Forge and
Frontier, Sword of Wealth and Power, Fertile Ground and Vorpal Sword have since
been authored), each by something outside host triggers, most of it shared
with other cards:

- **A static "is goaded"** (the Impetus Auras: Parasitic, Martial,
  Ghoulish): goad as a continuous effect of an attachment, not a one-shot.
- **"Return this card" after its host died** (Angelic Destiny, Endless Evil,
  Gift of Immortality, Ghoulish Impetus): the Aura reaches the graveyard by the
  state-based check after its trigger, so the ability has to follow that one
  move, the way a leaves-the-battlefield trigger follows its own object.
- **"Deals damage" once per event** (Spirit Link, Umezawa's Jitte, Lost
  Jitte): with no recipient named, damage to several things at once is one
  trigger (rule 603.2c), where `deals-damage` fires once per recipient.
- **Free casts during resolution** (Buster Sword, Sword of Once and Future, The
  Key to the Vault), **tokens that enter tapped and attacking** (Andúril, Flame of
  the West), **For Mirrodin!** (Hexplate Wallbreaker; living weapon and
  "the first combat phase of the turn" are built — Genji Glove and
  Nettlecyst are authored), **ascend** (Andúril, Narsil Reforged), **stun
  counters** (Mjölnir), a **dynamic "pay X life"** (Mask of Griselbrand), an
  **attached host condition** (Combat Research's "as long as enchanted
  creature is legendary"), and one-offs (Sword of Hearth and Home, Songbirds'
  Blessing, Infiltration Lens, Thran Power Suit, Pain for All,
  Ordeal of Nylea, The Aetherspark).

Sword of Fire and Ice turned up a real bug: a combat damage trigger's first
target slot was auto-filled with the damaged player whenever it could hold
one, so Mindscour Dragon's "target player mills four" could only ever pick
the damaged opponent. "That player" is now the `"trigger-player"` scope
(Xyris, Captain N'ghathrod moved to it) and every slot is a real choice.

### A token copy that enters with counters — built 2026-11-11

By EDHREC rank: Ochre Jelly (6738), Littjara Mirrorlake (7463).
`create-token-copy`'s `withCounters: { kind, amount }`: the copies enter with
those counters beside their own (122.6), folded into the one entry where the
tokens are minted (`mintFreshTokenObject`, through the entering replacement's
counters, so a counter replacement sees one placement) and put on before the
entry is announced where they wait on an "as this enters" choice
(`enterTokenCopies`). Not copiable (707.9e). The scaffolder reads "a copy of
[target], except it enters with N additional +1/+1 counters on it"; Ochre
Jelly's Split line recurs nowhere and has no template. Key:
effect:token-copy-enters-with-counters.

### Damage dealt to you as though its source had infect — built 2026-11-11

By EDHREC rank: Phyrexian Unlife (6722), the only card. `damageAsThoughSourceHad`
takes `to: "you"`: only damage dealt to the static's controller (120.3b,
702.90b), under the static's own `condition` ("as long as you have 0 or less
life"). Damage dealt at once is one event (510.2), so a damage batch reads
the answer once, before any of it: the damage that takes you to 0 or less is
all loss of life. The scaffolder reads both of the card's lines ("You don't
lose the game for having 0 or less life" too). Key:
static:damage-to-you-as-though-infect.

### A static P/T bonus per thing that happened this turn — built 2026-11-11

By EDHREC rank: Kinbinding (6510). `grantPtPerCount.turnHistory: { what,
filter? }` reads one of the controller's this-turn journal lists
(`journalCount`), each entry as it was then, so a creature that entered and
has since died still counts. The scaffolder reads "+X/+X, where X is the
number of [type]s that entered the battlefield under your control this turn".
Key: static:pt-per-turn-history.

### A floor on a spell's total cost — built 2026-11-11

The key `cost:minimum-total-cost` (the rank goal). `costModification`'s
`minimumMana`: an effect that directly affects the total cost, applied once
the total is determined (rule 601.2f) — after every increase and reduction,
on whatever cost the spell is cast for (an alternative cost, "without paying
its mana cost"), with an announced X counted, and before delve, convoke and
the like pay for it. `Game.castingCostOf` adds the difference as generic mana
when the total's mana (`cheapestManaAmount`: a hybrid or Phyrexian symbol is
one) comes to less. Trinisphere (rank 5360), under the existing "as long as
this is untapped" condition. A cost with one or two twobrid symbols and
nothing else would need the floor read per way of paying them; no printed card
has fewer than three.

### "Those players" a batch of attackers attacked — built 2026-11-11

The key `effect:attacked-players-scope` (the rank goal). Two `PlayerScope`
words, `"attacked-players"` and `"you-and-attacked-players"`: each player one
of the attackers that counted for an attack trigger (its `batch`) was declared
attacking (rules 506.2, 508.1b), read off the declaration the trigger keeps
(`LastKnownRefs.attackers`), so a creature removed from combat since still
names its player and one attacking a planeswalker names nobody. The trigger
itself needed nothing: `attacks-batch` with `attackingPlayer` in its filter.
Zurzoth, Chaos Rider (rank 5337); its draw trigger is `draws` with
`nthEachTurn: 1` and the drawing player's "not their turn" as the intervening
if.

### A ward cost read as it resolves — built 2026-11-11

The key `decision:ward-dynamic-x` (the rank goal). `WardCost.payGeneric` and
`payLifeAmount`, an `EffectAmount` each with the printed `text`: the ward
ability's "unless that player pays [cost]" is followed as it resolves (rules
702.21a, 608.2h), so the amount is read then, from the ward ability's
controller's side, the source as it last existed if it has left, and fixed
into the cost's `mana` / `payLife` before the usual pay-or-be-countered
question. No new decision. Minthara, Merciless Soul (rank 5300: ward {X}, X
her controller's experience counters; {0} is still asked) and Raubahn, Bull of
Ala Mhigo (6925: pay life equal to his power).

### "Is destroyed" — built 2026-11-10

The commander gap's `trigger:destroyed`. A **`destroyed`** trigger (`who`,
`filter?`, `otherOnly?`): a permanent destroyed by a "destroy" effect or by the
state-based actions for lethal damage and deathtouch damage (rules 701.8b,
704.5g-h). The `permanent-destroyed` event is shared with the legend rule, 0
loyalty and an Aura with nothing to enchant, which put a permanent into a
graveyard without destroying it, so the trigger reads the event's reason. It
looks back in time as a sacrifice trigger does (603.10a). Jaws, Relentless
Predator (commander 461) is the only card in the Oracle snapshot with the
wording: its "sacrificed or destroyed" is a `sacrifice` trigger and a `destroyed`
one with the same effect. Karmic Justice's "a spell or ability an opponent
controls destroys" (`trigger:destroyed-by-opponent-trigger`) is not this: it
needs the destroying effect's controller on the event.

### A search, then something about the card it found — no engine code, 2026-11-10

The key `effect:search-then-act-on-found`, asked for by name (the strong bot
decks' stand-ins). Its three records predate what the search already had:
`search-library`'s `then` runs with the finds as its targets, so slot 0 is "that
card", and `{ thisWay: "put-onto-battlefield" }` is "it" once it has entered.
Scion of the Ur-Dragon is a `then` of `become-copy` (`target: "source"`, `of: 0`,
`ofCard`, until end of turn) under a `conditional` on the find being in the
graveyard — its "If you do": a find Rest in Peace exiled isn't copied. Savage
Order is the search and a `grant-keyword-all` over what was put onto the
battlefield this way, until your next turn; its "sacrifice a creature with power
4 or greater" is `additionalCost.sacrifice` with a `power` clause. Instrument of
the Bards, the key's last card, is a `conditional` on slot 0 being legendary.
Tests: `search-then-act-on-found.test.ts`.

### An emblem that gives others abilities — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys family `effect:emblem-triggered-abilities`' top group. An emblem's
`grantsActivated` and `grantsTriggered` reach a class of permanents (Koth of the
Hammer's Mountains, Mu Yanling, Sky Dancer's Islands), each its own ref since no
definition holds it, and every `grantsToSpells` reader asks the emblems too (Ral,
Crackling Wit's storm). A source with a narrow and a richer mana option (a Swamp's
{B} and Liliana's {B}{B}{B}{B}) is planned with the richer only when the narrow
fails to pay. An emblem's own activated ability (Ob Nixilis of the Black Oath) is
not built: nothing offers an ability of an object in the command zone that isn't
its card's. Keys: effect:emblem-grants-activated, effect:emblem-grants-to-spells.

### "You may cast the discarded card", madness's exile too — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Malcolm, Alluring Scoundrel (1572). `cast-now`'s
`from: "discarded-this-way"` is the cards this resolution had discarded, found
where the discard put them if that's a public zone (rule 400.7j): the graveyard,
or the exile madness put one in instead (702.35a), cast from there before
madness's own trigger, which then finds nothing (declined, madness still offers
it). Not a card Library of Leng put on top of the library, a hidden zone. The
scaffolder reads the line (not after a cost's discard, whose "discarded card" is
the cost's) and "if there are N or more X counters on ~, …" mid-ability as a
`self-counters` conditional. Proving card: Malcolm, Alluring Scoundrel
(`cast-discarded-card.test.ts`). Key: effect:cast-discarded-card-exiled-by-madness.

### Playing linked exiled cards for life, and "a card you didn't control" — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Valgavoth, Terror Eater (1299). A `playFromExile` static's
`payLifeManaValue` is the cast's alternative cost of life equal to its mana
value (rule 118.9), the only one allowed unless another permission reaches the
card (`Game.exileStaticLifeCast`). "A card you didn't control" needed no engine
code: `controlledBy: "opponent"` on the graveyard replacement, since a card off
the battlefield and the stack is its owner's to control (rule 108.4a), with the
replacement's `linked`. The scaffolder reads both lines and Rest in Peace's and
Leyline of the Void's. Proving card: Valgavoth, Terror Eater
(`exile-play-pay-life.test.ts`). Keys: zone:linked-exile-play-pay-life-alternative,
replacement:exile-instead-cards-you-didnt-control.

### A would-die exile linked to its permanent — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: The Darkness Crystal (1159). A graveyard-exile replacement's
`linked` exiles the card **with** its permanent (rule 607.2b,
`GameObject.exiledWith`, in the stint it has as the card arrives — or last had,
gone in the same event), so "exiled with ~" finds it; given up, as `then` and a
void counter are, when the moving object's controller picks another exile that
could apply (616.1 — Rest in Peace). The scaffolder sets it on a card that says
"exiled with" anywhere. Proving cards: The Darkness Crystal, Gisa, Glorious
Resurrector (`exile-instead-linked.test.ts`). Key: replacement:exile-instead-linked.
### A draw you may replace — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Abundance (1960). `would-draw`'s `instead: { effect }` takes
`may: true` and the option's `text`: each draw, as it would happen, asks the
drawing player "Draw a card" or the replacement (rule 616.1), a draw of several
one at a time (614.11a), from an empty library too (614.11 — the rest of a draw
from it is no longer dropped while one could apply). Beside a mandatory effect
replacement or a "next time you would draw" the choice is between those.
Proving cards: Abundance, Pavel Chekov, First Posting, Obstinate Familiar ("you
may skip that draw instead", an empty effect). Keys:
replacement:draw-replacement-optional-skip. Still open in
`replacement:draw-replacement-reveal`: Enduring Renewal, Zur's Weirding and
Breathstealer's Crypt, which draw within the replacement (rule 614.5).

### "You may return another creature you control … If you do" — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Temur Sabertooth (1962). No engine code: a `choose-permanents`
(min 0) returning to hand, then a `returned-to-hand` this-way conditional
(Biblioplex Kraken's shape). The scaffolder now reads the pair whole, for any
permanent kind ("a land", "another permanent"). Key: effect:may-return-if-you-do.

### "Discard two cards unless you discard an artifact card" — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Thirst for Knowledge (1953). No engine code: `discard`'s
`unlessOne` (Compulsive Research) already ran it. The scaffolder now reads "then
discard N cards unless you discard a [kind] card" whole. Key:
effect:discard-unless-land.

### Doubling X as a spell is cast, and copying what has {X} — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Unbound Flourishing (2595). A `double-x` effect doubles the X of
the spell whose casting fired its trigger, while it's still that spell on the stack
(rules 107.3a, 400.7): its mana value there and its permanent's X (107.3m) follow.
The `activates-ability` trigger takes `xInCost` ("if that ability's activation cost
contains {X}", the mana symbol only); `cast-spell`'s filter already had
`xInManaCost`, and `copy-spell` / `copy-ability` did the copying. Proving card:
Unbound Flourishing (with Endless One, Walking Ballista, Blaze, Helix Pinnacle).
Key: effect:double-x-on-cast.

### "An Equipment that was attached to it" — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Zack Fair (2591). A leaving permanent's snapshot records what was
attached to it (`LastKnownInfo.attachments`, each in its stint), and an effect's
filter `wasAttachedTo: "source" | "trigger-object"` matches those still on the
battlefield as the same objects (rules 608.2h, 400.7) — or what's attached now
while it's still there. `attach`'s `choose` takes `min` ("attach **an** Equipment",
mandatory). Proving cards: Zack Fair (sacrificed for the cost, his Equipment fallen
off by 704.5n), Rhuk, Hexgold Nabber (all or none, from an attacking or dying
creature). Key: effect:attach-equipment-that-was-attached. Left: Cass, Hand of
Vengeance also returns the Aura cards that were attached from the graveyard,
attached to a target — a graveyard card's stint differs from its battlefield one.

### "Unless you waterbend {2}" — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Waterbending Lesson (4367). An `unless` option's `pay` takes
`waterbend: true` (rule 701.67a): offered when mana and taps together can pay it,
the `choose-modes` decision then carries `costWaterbend`, its offer a
`WaterbendOffer` (`waterbend`) and its answer the taps (`waterbend`), the engine
tapping the fewest that pay when a driver names none. The client asks which tap
after "Pay", through the cast's waterbend picker. The scaffolder reads "then
discard N cards unless you waterbend {N}" whole. Key: effect:unless-waterbend.

### One chosen for each color, then all at once — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Call the Spirit Dragons (4521). The `choose-for-each-slot` effect:
the controller chooses one permanent for each slot in turn (`choose-one-of-each`'s
slots — colors, basic land types, card types) on the `choose-permanents` decision's
keep choice, a permanent free to stand for several and a stack's token singled out
as it's chosen; then `each` applies to every one chosen at once (rule 608.2f), `"x"`
the slots it was chosen for, so a two-color Dragon's two counters are one placement.
"Five Dragons this way" is `this-way` `"counters-put"` (distinct permanents). No new
decision. Key: effect:per-color-counter-choice-and-win.

### "Damage isn't removed from this creature during cleanup steps" — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Ancient Adamantoise (4567). A `keepsDamageInCleanup` static: the
cleanup step's damage removal (rule 514.2) passes the permanent by while it's phased
in (the ruling; rule 702.26b), regeneration still clearing it. Its redirection was
Palisade Giant's. The scaffolder reads the line. Key:
static:damage-not-removed-in-cleanup.

### Plotting costs less — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Doc Aurlock, Grizzled Genius (2543). A static's `plotCostReduction`
takes generic mana (rule 118.7a) off the plot cost its controller pays to plot a card
from hand (702.170a, a special action, 116.2k): `Game.plotCostNow`, read by the offer
(its `cost` shows the reduced one), its check and its payment. Doc's other line,
"spells you cast from your graveyard or from exile cost {2} less", is a
`costModification` with `castFrom: ["graveyard", "exile"]`, which already ran. The
scaffolder reads both lines. Key: cost:plot-cost-reduction.

### Each owner of a spell cast this way losing life — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Kefka, Dancing Mad (3869, flagged ASAP). The `thisWay` amount's `byOwner`
reads each object by its owner rather than the player the kind names (a `"cast"` spell's caster),
so a `lose-life` to `"each-player"` of `{ thisWay: "cast", who: "each", byOwner: true, sumOf:
"mana-value" }` is "each player who owns a spell you cast this way loses life equal to its mana
value", read on the stack (a free spell's X is 0, rule 107.3b). The random exile from each
opponent's graveyard (`choose-random-card` in a `for-each-player`) and the free casts
(`cast-now` from `exiled-this-way`) already ran. The scaffolder reads the sentence. Proving card:
Kefka, Dancing Mad. Key: effect:cast-this-way-owner-loses-life. Left: The Ruinous Powers ("when
you cast a spell this way, its owner loses life" — a cast trigger on an impulse permission, a
different shape).

### Only land creatures attacking in the added combat — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Bumi, Unleashed (3529, flagged ASAP). `additional-combat`'s `onlyAttackers`
(with `afterThisPhase`) rides on the queued phase (`AddedPhase.onlyAttackers`); as that phase
begins, `Game.bindForcedAttackers` pushes a `PlayerEffect.onlyAttackers` that ends at its end of
combat, and `whyCannotAttack` refuses a creature not matching it (rule 508.1c) — that combat
alone (the ruling). The scaffolder reads "After this phase, there is an additional combat phase"
(Port Razer's, untaught till now) and the lands-only sentence after it. Proving card: Bumi,
Unleashed. Key: effect:extra-combat-attack-restriction. Left: Last Night Together ("only the
chosen creatures", objects, and after a main phase), Intimidation Bolt ("other creatures can't
attack this turn"), Boros Battleshaper and Forgestoker Dragon (a target that can't attack or
block this combat) — each a different shape on the same key.

### More Than Meets the Eye, living metal and convert — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Slicer, Hired Muscle (3512, flagged ASAP). `CardDefinition.moreThanMeetsTheEye`
(rule 702.162a) is an alternative cost, `via: "more-than-meets-the-eye"` with `face: 1`, offered
from the hand or the command zone like impending's: the card is cast converted, its back face up
on the stack (712.11a), its mana value its front's (712.8c), and enters converted. A transforming
card's back face is now refused as a cast but by disturb, this, or an effect casting it
transformed (712.11). Living metal (702.161a) is a `your-turn` static adding the creature type;
convert is `transform` (701.28a). The scaffolder reads both keywords. Proving cards: Slicer, Hired
Muscle // Slicer, High-Speed Antagonist. Key: mechanic:more-than-meets-the-eye. Left: Starscream
(also `trigger:becomes-monarch`), Ratchet, Field Medic, for `author-freed`; like blitz's and
web-slinging's, the offer comes from the hand and command zone only, not every zone a card may
be cast from (`docs/engine-gaps.md`).
### A mana ability's effect before its mana — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Empowered Autogenerator (3982). `add-mana`'s `first` is what
the ability does before it adds the mana, still one mana ability (rules 605.1a,
605.3b), its `amount` read after it — so the charge counter it puts counts, doubled
under Doubling Season. `Game.liveManaAmount` reads a `first` of a fixed number of
counters on the source as already done (`counterTotal`, Solemnity), so the
auto-payer, the by-hand offers and "could produce" size it right; any other
`first` is activated by hand. The scaffolder reads "[sentence]. Add …" in an
activated ability this way, and "Add X mana of any one color, where X is the
number of [kind] counters on ~" whole. Key: effect:mana-ability-effect-before-mana.

### A token copy that becomes a creature if it isn't one — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Ultron, Artificial Malevolence (4121). No engine code: the
sentence after the copy is no copy exception (no "except"), so it's `animate-all`
over the token made this way that isn't a creature (`thisWay: "created"`,
`notTypes`), with no duration — Mirrorworks' trigger otherwise. The scaffolder reads
"You may pay {N}. If you do, create a token that's a copy of …" with that sentence
after it. Key: effect:token-copy-if-noncreature-animate.

### "If the total mana value of the cards exiled this way is 13 or less" — built 2026-11-07

Flagged Build ASAP: Dance with Calamity (4151). No engine code: the `amounts`
condition compares the `thisWay` sum (`{ thisWay: "exiled", sumOf: "mana-value" }`)
with 13, read once the `repeat` (a `may` around one with `mayAgain`, "as many times as
you choose") is done, over every pass; `cast-now` with `repeat` and `free` casts from
among them. The scaffolder reads the whole card, "Shuffle your library." included.
Key: condition:this-way-total-mana-value.

### Library-top permissions until end of turn, for life too — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Gwenom, Remorseless (2650), The Belligerent (7006). A

### Doubling the equipped creature's triggers — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Wizard's Staff (2832). `doubleTriggersOf`'s `host` doubles the
triggers of the permanent the static's own permanent is attached to, whoever
controls it (the line says nothing of "you control"), granted ones too, and a
dies trigger of the host looks back (603.10a) to the Equipment still on it.
The scaffolder reads the line, "Equipped/Enchanted creature has prowess" and
"Equip [creature type] {N}". Key: static:double-triggers-of-attached.

### All damage dealt as though its source had wither — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Everlasting Torment (5228), the only card with the line. A

has every source's damage dealt as one with that keyword deals it (120.3d,

By EDHREC rank: Terra, Magical Adept (5171), the only card. No engine code:

### A spell put onto the battlefield transformed — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Esper Origins (5103), the only card with the line. No engine
code: `exile` of `"source"` takes the resolving spell off the stack, and

### "Target player adds" mana — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: The Warring Triad (3110), Radiant Lotus (5668). `add-mana`'s

graveyard, ~ isn't a creature". Key: effect:add-mana-target-player.

### The next spell has improvise — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Archway of Innovation (926). `grant-spells-this-turn`'s
`improvise` gives the next matching spell improvise (702.126a) as it's cast,

creatures convoke and artifacts improvise in one payment. Key:
effect:next-spell-has-improvise.

### An ability copied X times, and what can't be copied — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Gogo, Master of Mimicry. `copy-ability`'s `count` copies the
ability that many times ("X times"), as `copy-spell`'s does, each copy asked
about new targets in turn (rule 707.10c). "This ability can't be copied" is
`ActivatedAbility.cantBeCopied`, "This spell can't be copied"
`CardDefinition.cantBeCopied` (a rare field): `copyStackAbility` and
`copySpellFrom`, where every copy of an ability or a spell is made (rule
707.10), make nothing for one; it's still a legal target. The scaffolder reads
"This spell can't be copied."; Gogo's ability line is its own. Proving cards:
Gogo, Master of Mimicry, See Double. Keys: effect:copy-ability-count,
static:spell-cant-be-copied.

### "You may cast that card … Do this only once each turn" — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Planetarium of Wan Shi Tong (3240). A `cast-now` with
`marksAbilityDone` records the ability done this turn
(`GameState.abilitiesDoneThisTurn`, Carpet of Flowers' record) when a spell is
cast off its offer, and the trigger's `not this-ability-did-this-turn`
condition (an intervening if, rule 603.4) keeps it from triggering again that
turn — the card's ruling; declining, or nothing castable on top, spends
nothing. No scaffolder template: the line is the Planetarium's alone. Proving
card: Planetarium of Wan Shi Tong. Key:
effect:cast-now-once-each-turn-if-cast.

### Escalate that taps or discards — built 2026-11-07

By EDHREC rank: Collective Effort (3204). `castModal.alsoPerExtraMode` is
escalate's cost when it isn't mana (rule 702.120a, paid as additional costs
are, 601.2f–h): a kicker's `also`, once for each mode beyond the first, beside
`costPerExtraMode`'s mana. A tap is picked from the cast's `tapCost` offer,
which says `perExtraMode` (`tapCountForModes` counts it once the modes are
in: the client, the fuzzer and the engine's own pick); a discard is chosen as
it's paid. The offer's `maxModes` is capped at what the whole cost can pay
for. No sacrifice (`spellSacrificeParts` plans before the modes are known); no
scaffolder template: the parser reads no modal spells. Proving cards:
Collective Effort, Collective Brutality. Key: cost:escalate-nonmana.

### Spells off the top of the library for life — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Bolas's Citadel (274). A `castFromLibraryTop` permission's
`payLifeManaValue` is "if you cast a spell this way, pay life equal to its mana
value rather than pay its mana cost": the alternative cost (118.9) that
permission allows and no other, X 0 (107.3b), additional costs and increases
still paid; beside a permission without it (Future Sight) the card may be cast
either way. An impulse's or the Citadel's forced pay-life cast of a card with
an alternative cost of its own (Gush) no longer pays that one too. Key:
zone:cast-from-library-top-pay-life-mana-value.
### "If you haven't done it with this ability this turn" — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Carpet of Flowers (1325). The `this-ability-did-this-turn`
condition reads `GameState.abilitiesDoneThisTurn`, which an `add-mana` with
`marksAbilityDone` adds to only when it added at least one mana (rule 107.1b:
X = 0 adds none), by the ability's per-turn key, which the intervening-if checks (rule 603.4) and a
resolving `conditional` now pass (`ConditionOptions.abilityKey`): asked as it
triggers, so once it's added mana the ability doesn't trigger again that turn,
and again as it resolves. "Each of your main phases" is two entries joined by
`sameAbilityAs`. No scaffolder template: the line is Carpet's alone. Proving
card: Carpet of Flowers. Key: condition:this-ability-did-this-turn.

### The next creature spell enters with a counter — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Summon: Fenrir (1937). No engine code: "When you next cast a
creature spell this turn, that creature enters with an additional +1/+1
counter on it" was already `delayed-trigger` `at: { nextSpell: { type:

Faces Kakkazan; rule 603.7, 614.1c), and Fenrir's III Triumph of Ferocity's

### An attack tax in Phyrexian mana — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Norn's Annex (1923). The `attackTax` static's `phyrexian: "W"`
is one {W/P} a creature attacking what it covers (rule 508.1h), two Annexes two
pips: each paid with {W} or 2 life (rule 107.4f), the attacker choosing for
each (its ruling) through `declare-attackers`'s new `phyrexianLife` (how many
in life), which the client asks with the spells' Phyrexian prompt; omitted,
mana pays what it can and life the rest. The offer's
`attackTax.phyrexianPerCreature` names the pips, and `withinAttackTax` counts
each as 2 life against the life budget. The scaffolder reads "pays {C/P} for
each …" in `ATTACK_TAX_LINE`. Proving card: Norn's Annex. Key:
cost:attack-tax-phyrexian-mana. A Phyrexian tax for a while (a
`player-effect`) and one on a creature (`combatTax`) aren't built.

### A flicker that returns at its owner's next end step — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: The Eternal Wanderer (1806). `flicker`'s `returnOnOwnersTurn` with a
`"your-…"` `returnAt` waits for each exiled card's owner's turn, not the
controller's: "return that card to the battlefield under its owner's control
at the beginning of that player's next end step" is `returnAt:
"your-next-end-step", returnOnOwnersTurn: true` — one delayed return per owner
(a link each, `DelayedTrigger.turnOf`), still the controller's (rule 603.7e);
this turn's end step when the card is the controller's own. The rest of the
Wanderer was there: `attackLimit` on itself, and the −4 a `vote` of `choose`
`about: "each-player"` `keep` then `sacrifice-all` of `notThisWay: "chosen"`.
No scaffolder template: the scaffolder reads no delayed-return flicker yet, and
the owner's-turn line is the Wanderer's alone. Proving card: The Eternal
Wanderer. Key: effect:flicker-return-at-owners-next-end-step.

### A search's exiled finds castable later this turn — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Ugin, Eye of the Storms (1710). `search-library`'s `playable`
takes any `ExilePermission` beside `"while-exiled"`, granted on every exiled
find as `exile`'s `mayCast` grants it (`exilePermissionGrant`): "until end of
turn, you may cast those cards without paying their mana costs" is `{
duration: "end-of-turn", free: true }` (free only, rule 118.9; each find cast
when its own timing allows, the ruling), Chandra, Heart of Fire's "you may
cast them this turn" `{ duration: "end-of-turn" }`. The scaffolder reads both
lines (`searchExileCastLaterAt`). Proving cards: Ugin, Eye of the Storms;
Chandra, Heart of Fire. Key: effect:search-finds-castable-this-turn.

### A granted static that replaces or prevents — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Caduceus, Staff of Hermes (1438). `Game.staticsOfSource` now
lists a permanent's granted statics (`grantedStatics`) after its own, so every
scan through it — damage replacements and prevention (`damageSteps`), cost
modifications, play permissions, prohibitions — reads a granted static as the
object's own, working from it (rule 113.10). `damageSteps` also reads the
statics granted a permanent after it lost its abilities (613.7). Proving card:
Caduceus, Staff of Hermes. Key: static:granted-static-replacement. Acolyte of
Bahamut (`static:granted-static-cost-modification`) likely runs now through
the same path but wasn't tested.

### A divided total read from X beyond plain X — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Shatterskull Smashing (1413). A spell's `divided.total` that
reads X other than as plain X — Meteor Shower's "X plus 1" (`{ sum: ["x", 1]
}`), Shatterskull Smashing's "twice X if X is 6 or more" (an `ifCondition` on
`{ kind: "x" }`; its ruling: twice X is what's divided) — was always read at
the X cast, but the offer named the X = 0 total and capped the group by it.
Now the cast offer carries the total at every X (`divide.totalByX`,
`Game.dividedTotalsByX`), which the client's division step reads at the X
chosen, and `xGroupOffer` bounds the number of targets by it as it does "up to
X" (`xCost.minXByTargetCount`: at least 1 each, rule 601.2d) and by the
group's own `max`. The scaffolder reads "X plus N … among any number of", "X
… among up to two/three target …" (a total of `"x"`) and the "If X is N or
more, ~ deals twice X damage … instead." sentence. Proving cards: Shatterskull
Smashing, Meteor Shower. Key: effect:divided-total-reading-x. An activated
ability's total read from X is still offered at one X only. An X group's
offer (this one, and "up to X"/"X target" groups) now also carries
`maxXUnreduced`/`reducingTargets` when a target takes something off (Spellwild
Ouphe): without it, an X only that target pays for was offered and refused.

### A copy of the spell for each creature sacrificed to cast it — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Plumb the Forbidden (1148). "You may sacrifice one or more
creatures" is the sacrifice of X (`sacrificeCount: "x"`, Vicious Betrayal's);
`additionalCost.copyForEachSacrificed` adds "when you do, copy this spell for
each creature sacrificed this way": one reflexive trigger as the cast finishes
(rule 603.12), only when X is 1 or more, making X copies with the original's
targets — replicate's record (`queuePaidCostCopy`, now with `newTargets`
off). The scaffolder reads the line, and "you draw a card and lose N life".
Proving card: Plumb the Forbidden. Key: cost:sacrifice-any-number-copy-for-each.

### Reveal until a card sharing a card type with a permanent — already built, 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Wild Magic Surge (1993). No engine code: `reveal-until`'s
filter takes `sharesCardTypeWith` of the target slot, read as the permanent
last existed once it has been destroyed or sacrificed (rule 608.2h), and
`whose: { controllerOfTarget }` is its controller then; "a permanent card" is
`typesAnyOf` the permanent types. Reweave's "if the player does" is a
`this-way` `sacrificed` conditional, and its splice gives it its own target
slice. The scaffolder now reads Wild Magic Surge whole, and "target permanent
an opponent controls". Proving cards: Wild Magic Surge, Reweave
(`reveal-until-shares-type.test.ts`). Key: effect:reveal-until-shares-type
(Chaotic Transformation, its third card, left for `author-freed`).

### Tempting offer — already built, 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Tempt with Bunnies (1979). No engine code: the pool's six
tempting offers (Tempt with Vengeance, Glory, Discovery, Immortality,
Reflections, Mayhem) are `each-player-may` of each opponent in turn order, the
offer theirs to do, and `ifDid` yours once per taker; Bunnies' offer is a draw
and a Rabbit. The scaffolder now reads a tempting offer whose offer can't be
taken and not done (draws, tokens, counters, life; no targets) whole —
Vengeance, Glory and Bunnies. Proving card: Tempt with Bunnies
(`tempting-offer.test.ts`). Key: effect:tempting-offer.

### Prevention over the permanents matching as it resolves — already built, 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Mutational Advantage (1973). No engine code: "prevent all
damage that would be dealt to those permanents this turn" names the set the
spell's grants reached, fixed as it resolves (the ruling), so it is a
`for-each-permanent` putting a `prevent-damage` `"all"` shield on each, not a
`damage-rule` asked as the damage would be dealt. The scaffolder now reads
"permanents you control [with counters on them] gain … and … until end of
turn" and the "those permanents" prevention after such a grant. Proving card:
Mutational Advantage (`prevent-damage-each-matching.test.ts`). Key:
effect:prevent-damage-each-matching-at-resolution.

### "For each creature exiled this way, its controller searches" — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Winds of Abandon (1940). `for-each-player`'s `whoseAmount`
keeps only the players for whom an amount read for them is 1 or more (a
`thisWay` of `who: "each"`: the exiled creatures' controllers as they left),
and a `search-library`'s `min`/`max` are read for its searcher, so each such
player searches once for that many basic lands, the finds held and entering
together (`choicesHeld`), then shuffles; a player who lost nothing neither
searches nor shuffles. Overload's "each" half is `exile-all` before the same
step. Proving card: Winds of Abandon (`per-controller-search.test.ts`). Key:
keyword:overload-per-controller-search.

### "Until end of turn, you don't lose unspent red mana" — built 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: The Last Agni Kai (1942). `player-effect`'s `keepsUnspentMana`
is the static's keep for a while: `Game.keptManaTypes` reads its owner's
player effects beside the statics, so every {R} in that pool stays through the
turn's steps, whenever it was made, until the effect ends at cleanup and the
pool empties as that step ends (rule 500.5). The fight's "add that much {R}" is
`add-mana` of Rhino's Rampage's `excessDamageThisWay` with `to: 1`. Proving
card: The Last Agni Kai (`keep-unspent-mana-this-turn.test.ts`). Key:
replacement:keep-unspent-mana-until-end-of-turn.

### "Whenever you discard one or more cards" — already built, 2026-11-06

By EDHREC rank: Inti, Seneschal of the Sun (1949). No engine code: the
`discards` trigger already fires once a batch (Rielle, the Everwise), the
"you may discard a card. When you do, …" is Tether Technician's reflexive
trigger, and the exile is `impulse-exile` until your next end step. The
scaffolder now reads "you may play that card until your next end step" (and
"until your next end step, you may …"). Proving card: Inti, Seneschal of the
Sun (`discards-batch-impulse.test.ts`). Key: trigger:discards-batch-trigger.

### An Aura on a graveyard card, and an enchant ability an effect gives — built 2026-11-05

By EDHREC rank: Animate Dead (223) and Necromancy (1030). An Aura whose target
slot is `card-in-graveyard` (`enchantsGraveyardCard`) enters attached to that
card — cast, or chosen as it's put onto the battlefield (303.4f) — and is
unattached when the card leaves the graveyard; statics don't reach the card
there. The `swap-enchant` effect gives the source an enchant ability
(`GameObject.enchantSwap`): "enchant creature put onto the battlefield with
this" (`"put-this-way"`, that object in that stint) or any spec, and with
`becomeAura` makes it an Aura (layer 4). Proving cards: Animate Dead, Dance of
the Dead, Necromancy, Cloudform, Lightform, Rageform. Keys:
effect:aura-enchants-card-in-graveyard, effect:enchantment-becomes-aura.

### Exile with its own way back — built 2026-11-04

The need-keys family `effect:exile-any-number-until-leaves`' top unit. Glorious
Protector and Bronzebeak Foragers needed no new code (`choose-permanents` with
`exile { untilSourceLeaves }`, one optional slot a seat, `card-in-exile`'s `linked`).
Dimensional Breach: `DelayedWhenever.whileAnyExiled` (used up, untriggered, once none
of its `about` cards is still exiled as the object it was), `look-and-choose`'s
`player: "active-player"` and `exiledThatCanEnter` (its targets still in exile, no Aura
with nothing to enchant, rule 303.4g). Keys: effect:exile-any-number-until-leaves,
effect:exile-until-leaves-per-opponent (Valki waits on its own "revealed this way" and
its copy ability), trigger:linked-exile-each-upkeep-return-one. Left:
effect:exile-until-source-untaps-with-auras (Tawnos's Coffin: its Auras exiled with it,
counters noted and returned, "becomes untapped or leaves", the Auras returning attached).

### Where a card goes instead of a graveyard, and a discard put back — built 2026-11-04

The need-keys family `replacement:new-event-replacements`' top unit, but for its
optional permanent's replacement. `would-be-put-into-graveyard`'s `instead` takes
`"library-top"` with `self` and `from: "battlefield"` (Gravebane Zombie), and
`notCycled` (Abandoned Sarcophagus: the discard paying a cycling cost, a variant's
too, is let through). A new replacement `would-discard`, `causedBy: "effect" |
"opponent"`, `reveal` (Library of Leng, Nephalia Academy): the discard waits on a
`choose-from-zone` of its cards, min 0, in order (`discardToLibrary`, the queue
`GameState.pendingDiscardsToLibrary`), the chosen on top, the rest as a discard goes,
one `cards-discarded` for all, naming Library of Leng's unrevealed cards in `unrevealed`:
their characteristics are undefined (rule 701.9c), so no filter on a discarded card
matches them and no seat learns them. A cost's and cleanup's are never asked. What's left:
Pulmonic Sliver, `replacement:graveyard-to-library-top-optional`, needs a "you may"
on a permanent's every way to a graveyard, a wrath's deaths waiting on one answer
with their order (its ruling), and no death path can wait yet but a commander's
903.9b. Keys: replacement:dies-to-library-top,
replacement:discard-to-library-replacement, replacement:exile-if-not-cycled.

### A countered spell's destination, and a counter replaced — built 2026-11-04

The need-keys family `effect:counter-exile-instead`. `counter`'s `into` takes
`"library-top"` and `"library-bottom"` (Memory Lapse, Lapse of Certainty, Spell
Crumple; Hinder's "your choice" is an `each-player-may` of the two, its controller's
answer), `withTimeCounters` with `into: "exile"` (Delay, rule 702.62b: only when it
was countered, and only a card that reached exile), and a replacement
`would-counter-spell` (Guile: the `counter` effect's controller's permanents, on a
spell that could be countered; exiled, never countered, so no "this way" rider, and
`cast-now` with `free` and `play` offered; a ward no one can pay runs the same
effect). Keys: effect:counter-exile-instead, effect:counter-into-exile-suspend,
effect:counter-into-library-bottom, effect:counter-to-library-top,
effect:counter-to-library-top-or-bottom.

### Spells countered all at once, and spells going to a hand as they resolve — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys families `effect:counter-all-spells` and
`effect:spell-returns-itself-to-hand`. `for-each-on-stack` gains `filter`
("counter all spells with those names": `{ sameNameWith: "exiled-with-source" }`,
Grimoire Thief still waits on its look at the cards); this-way kind `countered`
(an ability object of several copies once a copy; `spell-countered` now names its
controller). Swift Silence, Glen Elendra's Answer, Counterflux (overload), Reverse
the Polarity. `return-to-hand`'s `now`: from the stack at this point of the
instructions, so "return ~, then discard a card at random" can discard it (Hanabi
Blast, its ruling); View from Above and Molten Birth needed only the existing
`from: "stack"`. `grant-spells-this-turn`'s `intoHandAsItResolves` marks the spell
as it's cast, no trigger (`GameObject.intoHandAsItResolves`), read at
`leaveStackAfterResolving` as Feather's mark is, with the same rule 616.1 choice
(Soulfire Grand Master). Keys: effect:counter-all-spells,
effect:counter-all-spells-named-linked-exile, effect:spell-returns-itself-to-hand,
effect:next-spell-returns-to-hand.

### One target slot over several kinds of object, and exiled cards as targets — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys family `effect:target-spec-additions`' next two groups, features
and proving cards. New target spec **`one-of`** (`of: TargetSpec[]`, legal when
any part accepts, candidates each part's, an object two accept once): spell or
creature (Unsubstantiate), abilities and a filtered spell (Tale's End), a
graveyard or exile (Sorceress's Schemes), and Daretti's and Shivan Sand-Mage's
slots. `SpellTargetsFilter`'s `player` with `permanent` now reads as either:
"that targets you or a creature you control" (Siren Stormtamer). `card-in-exile`
gains `whose: "trigger-player"` (Time Reaper) and `thisWay: "exiled"` on a
reflexive ability's slot, fixed to `among` as it triggers, since it chooses
targets after the resolution is done (rule 603.12 — Espers to Magicite).
`shuffle-into-library` takes a card from exile (Riftsweeper). Voidslime and Not
of This World needed only the record corrected. Keys:
effect:target-spell-or-ability, effect:target-spell-or-creature,
effect:target-card-in-exile, effect:target-card-exiled-this-way,
target:card-in-graveyard-or-permanent, target:stack-spell-filter,
target:card-exiled-with-source.

### Hexproof from abilities, and a "can't be the target" that isn't hexproof — built 2026-11-03

`keyword:hexproof-from-abilities`: Volatile Stormdrake's keyword is `hexproofFrom: { abilitiesOnly: true }` (lost with
hexproof, 702.11e); the printed "can't be the target of …" statics take `notHexproof` and keep through a loss of
hexproof (Shanna, Sisay's Legacy against Shadowspear — and Dense Foliage, Fiendslayer Paladin, Mercenary Informer,
Thrun, marked the same). Proving cards: Volatile Stormdrake, Shanna, Sisay's Legacy.

### Activating an exhaust or a power-up ability, as a trigger — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:activates-ability`, its first unit, features only.
`trigger:activates-exhaust` and `trigger:activates-power-up`: `exhaust` and `powerUp` on
`activates-ability`, read off the ability as activated, as `ninjutsu` and `loyalty` are. Proving
cards: Rangers' Refueler, Adrenaline Jockey, Marvel Boy, Noh-Varr. Afterburner Expert triggers from
its graveyard, which this trigger doesn't watch (`trigger:activates-ability-from-graveyard`).

### Counters-put firing counts: per counter, and once for a placement — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:counters-put`, features only. `trigger:counters-put-each-counter`:
`perCounter`. `trigger:counters-put-batch`: `batched`, settled by `withCounterBatch`, with `otherOnly`.
Proving cards: Fathom Mage, Flourishing Defenses, Bloodcrazed Hoplite, Invisible Woman, Sue Storm,
Bioessence Hydra. Sigurd, Jarl of Ravensthorpe still needs `effect:choose-add-or-remove-counter`.

### Damage triggers that read the amount, and excess damage — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:damage-trigger-extensions`, features only.
`trigger:deals-damage-amount-threshold` and `trigger:dealt-damage-amount-threshold`: `atLeast` on
both ends, one simultaneous event and never a total over separate ones. `trigger:excess-damage-trigger`:
`dealt-damage`'s `excess` (rule 120.4a; `triggerValue` is the excess) and `batched` ("one or more"),
with `damagedThisTurnBy.spell`. Proving cards: Fall of Cair Andros, Magmatic Galleon, Aegar, the
Freezing Flame, Deus of Calamity, Pain Magnification, Innocent Bystander, Spinneret and Spiderling.
Toralf ("any target other than that permanent", and his Hammer) and Overclocked Electromancer (an
excess amount on the dealing end) aren't this shape alone.

### A player becoming a target, an ability's targets, the first time by anyone — built 2026-11-03

`trigger:player-becomes-target`, `trigger:becomes-target-by-your-ability`, `trigger:becomes-target-first-time-by-anyone`
(family `trigger:becomes-target-fixes`): the `player-targeted` event (once per player a spell or ability targets),
`becomes-target`'s `player`, `playerOnly`, `abilityOnly` and `firstTimeEachTurnByAnyone`. Proving cards: Amulet of
Safekeeping, Unsettled Mariner, Dormant Gomazoa, Loki, God of Mischief, Angelic Cub. Still needing more: Leyline of
Combustion ("you and/or at least one permanent you control" — once per spell or ability), Rayne's "if enchanted",
Parnesse's copy, Skophos Maze-Warden's land named Labyrinth of Skophos, Kira's granted "counter that spell or
ability"; `trigger:becomes-target-spell-on-stack` (Surrak) and `-of-ability-targeting-only-it` (Agrus Kos) open.

### A land tapped for mana producing another type — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:tap-for-mana-produces-color-instead`: `StaticAbility.landManaInstead` (per land kind, whoever taps or
only you) and the `player-effect` field `landManaInstead` (until end of turn), applied where a land's {T} mana
ability makes mana, in the auto-payer's options and in the offered activations. Proving cards: Deep Water, Reality
Twist, Infernal Darkness.

`replacement:land-mana-type-replacement` (2026-11-03) finished it: an entry's `one` ("and amount" — Contamination),
`colorsOnly` ("any other color" — Hall of Gemstone), `produces: "any-color"` (the tapper's pick — Pulse of Llanowar,
Harvest Mage), and a player effect's own entry (every player's nonbasic lands — Pale Moon). A land its own cost
sacrificed is read as it last was, and "could produce" reads the replacement (rule 106.7).

### A mode picked at random as an ability goes on the stack — built 2026-11-03

`effect:random-mode-announced`: `modal`'s `announced` with `random`, on a triggered or chapter ability (a chapter can
now announce at all), among the modes it could have; `chooseIf` for "you choose one instead". Proving cards: Umaro,
Raging Yeti, Cult of Skaro, Typhoid Mary, Fractured, Summon: Magus Sisters.

### The amassed Army — built 2026-11-03

`effect:amassed-army-power`: an `amassed` event, this-way kind and ref (`AttachRef`, `AmountRef`, `DamageFrom`). A
reflexive ability `about` an object now reads its own compares instead of being bound by the creating resolution.
Proving cards: Foray of Orcs, Grishnákh, Brash Instigator, Goblin Plate Mail, Surrounded by Orcs, Widespread Brutality.

### Two mana of different colors — built 2026-11-03

`effect:add-mana-two-different-colors`: `add-mana`'s `{ oneOf, different }` — an offer per pairing by hand, an option
per pairing to the payer (which now takes the option covering the most of what's still needed), a mode per pairing
on the stack. Proving cards: Firemind Vessel, Interplanar Beacon, Open the Omenpaths.

### Unspent mana as steps and phases end: another type, every player's kept, life lost — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:mana-pool-emptying` (family `replacement:mana-pool-emptying`): `unspentManaBecomes` (Kruphix's
replacement to a colour — Omnath, Locus of All, Ozai), `keepsUnspentManaEachPlayer` (Upwelling) and
`manaLossCausesLifeLoss` (Yurlok of Scorch Thrash), in the rule 500.4 pool emptying. Proving cards: Horizon Stone,
Upwelling, Yurlok of Scorch Thrash. Still open: `replacement:mana-persistence-color` (The Last Agni Kai's one-shot
"until end of turn, you don't lose unspent red mana" beside its excess-fight-damage mana).

### A draw replaced by an effect, and draw-N exemptions — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:draw-replacement-look-and-choose`, `replacement:draw-replacement-except-first`,
`replacement:draw-replacement-first-each-turn` (family `replacement:new-event-replacements`).
`would-draw`'s `instead: { effect }` (rule 614.11, 614.11a: each draw of several replaced and
answered before the next, applied on an empty library), and `exceptFirstInDrawStep` /
`firstEachTurn` on `draws` and `effect` replacements, the turn's draw events kept in
`PlayerState.wouldDrawThisTurn`. Proving cards: Underrealm Lich, Tomorrow, Azami's Familiar,
Sages of the Anima, Teferi's Ageless Insight, Reed Richards, Smartest Man, Bard, King of Dale.
Still open in the group: `replacement:draw-replacement-reveal` (Abundance's optional
land-or-nonland reveal, Breathstealer's Crypt's draw-and-reveal, the play-with-hand-revealed
pair). Scion of Halaster needs its replacement granted to commander creatures; Aladdin's Lamp a
one-shot "the next time you would draw" shield.

### An exile's end as a parameter — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `effect:exile-from-hand-until-source-leaves` +
`effect:exile-until-opponent-becomes-monarch` + `effect:exile-until-other-target-leaves`,
all rule 610.3: `discard-chosen`'s `untilSourceLeaves` (back to the hand it came from,
`exiledUntil.returnTo`), and `exile`'s `until`, `{ targetLeaves: N }` or
`"opponent-becomes-monarch"` (`GameObject.exiledUntilMonarch`, read in `Game.setMonarch`;
`GameState.monarchBecameAt` for 610.3b). Proving cards: Kitesail Freebooter, Brain Maggot,
Deep-Cavern Bat, Palace Jailer, Calix. Left over from the first key: Cloak and Dagger,
Entwined (one choice over a hand and a creature), Intellect Devourer (each opponent's own
choice, and playing them), Hypnox and Wormfang Behemoth (a whole hand exiled at once).
Mesmeric Fiend reads as Tidehollow Sculler's shape, no new code. The family's next unit is
the "until your next turn" durations.

### Held down through a chosen player's next N untap steps — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `effect:doesnt-untap-next-two-untap-steps` +
`effect:doesnt-untap-next-untap-step` + `effect:doesnt-untap-your-next-untap-step`:
`tap`'s `doesntUntapSteps` (and `tap-all`'s) and `untapStepOf: "you"`, on
`GameObject.skipsFurtherUntaps` and a `skipsUntapOf` that holds one `{ player, steps }`
a player. Overlapping holds keep the longer rather than add. Proving cards: Telekinesis,
Reveka, Samite Alchemist, Spectral Bears, Vorinclex, Dreamshackle Geist; the ten Tempest
lands read "your next untap step" exactly now. Left over: `Deep Spawn`'s and `Homarid
Warrior`'s effects are expressible, only the Homarid's "Tap it" wants the parser's help.

### Attacking and blocking alone, or not — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:can-only-attack-alone` (its group's four
keys, and `static:cant-attack-alone` riding along), features only, no new
decision kind. Five `CombatRestriction`s, so an Aura or a `restrict` carries them
as it does any other; `combat/company.ts` says what each asks of the others
declared. The offers leave out a creature with no group it could be in, and carry
`company` / `blockCompany`; `attackCompanyViolations` and
`blockCompanyViolations` go beside the validators' other set-level checks. With
company in play, attack requirements are counted as rule 508.1d says (the most
obeyed, not all), so "another must attack, so this can't" holds, and
`withinAttackCompany` / `withinBlockCompany` fix a bot's or the fuzzer's
declaration without a draw. 5 proving cards: Mogg Flunkies, Craven Hulk, Orcish
Conscripts, Errantry, Bonded Construct. Left open: Okk's and Scarred Puma's "a
creature with greater power / a black or green creature also attacks" (their own
keys), the "no more than N creatures can attack" side of
`static:attack-limit`. The client's Confirm isn't told: an illegal declaration is
refused with the engine's reason, as a toast.

### Limits on the untap step — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:untap-step-limit`, features only, no new
decision kind. **`untapLimit`** (a static: `who`, `count`, `filter?`) and
**`untapOnlyChosenType`** (`who`, `types`) decide which permanents untap in
rule 502.3's step, after "you may choose not to": `Game.continueUntapStep` asks
each Storage Matrix's question as a `choose-modes` (`awaiting.untapTypes`), then
each limit still broken as a `choose-permanents` of exactly `count`
(`awaiting.untapLimit`), and only then untaps. A limit is a maximum on what
would otherwise untap, so the player untaps all it allows (502.3: only an effect
keeps a permanent tapped). A partial stack's tokens split off. Reused the
decisions, so the client needs nothing. 6 proving cards: Winter Orb, Static Orb,
Winter Moon, Smoke, Mungha Wurm, Storage Matrix. An emblem's limit is reached too
(tested on a bare emblem, for Dovin Baan's).

### The owner, not the controller, of a target or trigger object — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 group of `effect:owner-of-target-player-ref` in the
family `effect:target-spec-additions`, features only. `{ ownerOfTarget }`
reaches `gain-life`, `lose-life`, `damage`'s `who`, `mill` and
`exile-graveyard`, beside `draw` and `discard`, which took it already. The new
`"trigger-owner"` scope is "that player" of "returned to a player's hand" and
"put into a player's graveyard" (rule 108.3: an owner never changes, and a
token's is read as it last existed). `damage`'s `who: "attached-owner"` with
`from: "attached"` is "enchanted creature deals 1 damage to its owner". The
scaffolder reads each line and both triggers. Keys:
effect:owner-of-target-player-ref, effect:exile-graveyard-of-target-card-owner,
effect:trigger-object-owner-player-ref. Proving cards: Path of Peace, Recoil,
Geth, Nurgle's Conscription, Enslave, Warped Devotion, Liability.

### A cost keyed to a spell's target: a mana tax, and a reduction on the target's controller — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:target-controller-graveyard-reduction`
with `cost:targeted-by-spells-mana-tax`, features only. Terror of the Peaks'
`targetedBySpellsCost` takes `payGeneric` beside `payLife` ("cost {2} more to
cast"), can be granted (`grantsStatic`: one instance per permanent named), and
with `targeting` reads the spell's targets against a filter ("a Merfolk you
control", "one or more commanders you control"), once per spell. Kopala's other
half is `abilityCostModification`'s `byOpponents` and `targeting`. The total
is fixed once the targets are (rules 601.2c, 601.2f): `Game.castingCostOf`
prices the chosen targets, an increase before any reduction. An offer prices
each target through a probe (`targetTaxOffer`) and leaves out what its caster
can't afford, says the rest in `LegalAction.targetTaxes` and
`xCost.targetTaxes`, and the drivers fit to them (`targetsWithinTaxes`,
`maxXForTargets`). The new filter clause `controllerGraveyardAtLeast` is
Lullmage's Domination's target; a `targetCostReduction` whose filter reads X
is now priced at each X's own targets. The scaffolder reads each line. Keys:
cost:target-controller-graveyard-reduction, cost:targeted-by-spells-mana-tax.
Proving cards: Boreal Elemental, Jubilant Skybonder, Kopala, Lullmage's
Domination.
### A P/T bonus by each affected creature's own count, and other amounts — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:pt-per-amount`, features only. On
`grantPtPerCount`: `relativeToAffected` asks `filter` of each affected permanent
(`attachedTo: { isSource: true }` is "attached to it", `excludeSelf` "other than
it"), `sharesCreatureTypeWithAffected` narrows the filter to its creature types,
and `ofAffected` is its mana value, colors or creature types. Then `countersAmong`,
`playerCountersOf: "opponents"`, `anyCountersOnSource`, `turnStat`, `basicLandTypes`
(Kavu Scout, Wayfaring Giant, Strength of Unity and Exotic Curse moved to it from
five statics each), `opponents`, `fromAffectedController`, `step` and `max`. The
greatest mana value among a set was already `aggregate`. The scaffolder reads the
lines. Keys: static:count-attached-to-affected,
static:pt-per-count-excluding-affected, static:shares-creature-type-count,
static:grant-pt-equal-attached-mana-value, static:grant-pt-per-own-colors,
static:pt-per-affected-creature-types, static:pt-per-counters-among-permanents,
static:pt-per-opponents-player-counters, static:pt-per-turn-stat,
static:grant-pt-per-any-counter-on-source, static:grant-pt-per-domain,
static:pt-bonus-greatest-mana-value,
static:pt-per-count-affected-controller-graveyard, static:pt-per-count-step,
static:pt-per-opponent. Proving cards: Thran Power Suit, Auramancer's Guise,
Champion of the Flame, Sliver Legion, Coat of Arms, Alpha Status, Hedron Matrix,
Civic Saber, Knight of New Alara, Mycosynth Fiend, Gleam of Authority, Knowledge Is
Power, Kyler, Manaforce Mace, Dark Matter Manipulator, Disturbing Conversion. Left:
mechanic:hone-counters, static:pt-per-turn-history (Kinbinding),
static:anthem-per-counter-on-source (Banner of Kinship's enters-with count),
static:per-keyword-pump, static:pt-per-attachments-on-source,
static:pt-per-defending-player-count.

### Protection from players, everything, spells, subtypes and exiled card types — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 group `keyword:protection-from-opponents` and its six
others, features only: protection (rule 702.16) whose quality is any source
quality, read by all four parts — target, enchant and equip, damage, block —
through `qualityMatches`. New: `fromPlayers` on a static, a player's protection
and `grant-protection` (702.16k: "opponents", fixed as a grant resolves, or
"chosen"), `CardFilter.isSpell` ("spells that are one or more colors", "instant
spells"), `exiledCardTypes`, a static's filter read with its own source (Riders
of Gavony's "of the chosen type"), `doesntRemoveSource` by card type (702.16n),
everything needing no object to ask about (702.16j), a source that left read as
it last existed for a filter, and `protectionLabel` in words instead of "a
quality" (`protection-qualities.test.ts`). Keys:
keyword:protection-from-opponents, keyword:protection-from-everything,
keyword:protection-from-colored-spells,
keyword:protection-from-spells-and-cast-permanents,
keyword:protection-from-subtype, static:protection-from-exiled-card-types,
static:protection-except-this-aura; Tier B static:player-protection-from-opponents,
filter:protection-from-spells-only. Proving cards: Cliffside Rescuer, Figure of
Fable, Absolute Virtue, True-Name Nemesis, Vexilus Praetor, Emrakul the Aeons Torn,
Emrakul the World Anew, Devoted Caretaker, Baneslayer Angel, Riders of Gavony,
Mirror Golem, Tattoo Ward. Left: protection from mana values and from a target
player (groups of their own), the bots' `combat-math` reading only colours and
types.

### A source of your choice, to anything, for the turn — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `replacement:prevent-damage-from-chosen-source`,
features only. Most of it was in `prevent-damage-from-chosen-source` already
(`to: "any"`, `amount: "all"`, a redirection, rule 609.7), with no card, test or
parser line reading it that way. New: `to: "your-side-creatures"` ("you and/or
creatures you control") and `gainLifeFrom`, the filter on the source a
`gainLifePrevented` reads as the damage would be dealt
(`chosen-source-recipients.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads each wording, and "you
gain life …" after one. Keys:
replacement:prevent-all-damage-from-chosen-source-this-turn,
replacement:prevent-next-damage-from-chosen-source-any-recipient,
replacement:redirect-chosen-source-any-recipient,
replacement:chosen-source-shield-you-and-your-creatures. Proving cards: Burrenton
Forge-Tender, Prahv, Pilgrim of Virtue, Opal-Eye, Samite Ministration, Shadowbane.
Left: Mourner's Shield needs "shares a color with the exiled card" as a filter.

### Keyword abilities and types for cards outside the battlefield — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:grant-to-cards-outside-battlefield`,
features only. `grantsToGraveyard.keywords` gives cards in your graveyard
unearth, encore, embalm, harmonize, retrace or sneak, each with its cost
(`GraveyardKeywordGrant`: fixed, `"mana-cost"`, or `"mana-value"` for "encore
{X}, where X is its mana value"), read by `Game.graveyardKeywordsOf`; an emblem
holds such a grant too (rule 113.6p). `grant-graveyard-keyword` is the one-shot,
until end of turn, for one target or each card matching a filter
(`GameObject.grantedGraveyardKeywords`). `castWithSneakFromGraveyard` is Ninja
Teen's "using their sneak abilities". `grantsCyclingInHand` (`search`,
`eachPlayer`) and `grantsForetellInHand` (`reducedBy`, the cost kept on the card
as `foretoldCost`) are the hand's, with the `foretells` trigger.
`typesOutsideBattlefield` (`ofType`, `zones`) is layer 4 off the battlefield,
read through `layerFour` by every filter, and `setsChosenCreatureType` is
"are the chosen type" with no "in addition". The scaffolder reads them all.
Keys: effect:grant-embalm, effect:grant-harmonize,
effect:grant-unearth-to-graveyard-until-eot, static:grant-encore-in-graveyard,
static:grant-sneak-to-graveyard, static:grant-unearth-to-graveyard,
static:graveyard-cards-gain-chosen-type, static:static-types-outside-battlefield,
static:grant-cycling-to-cards-in-hand, static:grant-foretell,
static:retrace-grant. Proving cards: Sedris, the Traitor King; Solemn Doomguide;
Wire Surgeons; Graywater's Fixer; Cursecloth Wrappings; Songcrafter Mage; Ninja
Teen; Deeproot Historian; Six; Wrenn and Six; Rhet-Tomb Mystic; Tectonic
Reformation; Homing Sliver; Dream Devourer; Ashes of the Fallen; Arcane
Adaptation; Conspiracy; Maskwood Nexus. Left: Ghost Ark's "becomes crewed"
trigger, Ethereal Valkyrie's "exile a card from your hand face down. It becomes
foretold", Araumi's cost, Arcade Gannon (static:graveyard-cast-filter-amount).

### Activations this turn: a limit of N, or of a count, and a condition on the count — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:ability-activated-count-this-turn`,
features only. An activated ability's `timesEachTurn` is "activate no more
than twice each turn" (a number) or "no more times each turn than the
number of snow Swamps you control" (a `CostReductionAmount` count, read as
it would be activated): rule 602.5b, kept with `oncePerTurn`'s record on
`GameObject.abilitiesUsedThisTurn`, one entry an activation, so it stays
with the object through a change of control. A mana ability with it is left
out of the auto-payer (activated by hand). The new `StaticCondition`
`activated-this-turn` (`atLeast`) is "if this ability has been activated
four or more times this turn", read inside the ability's own `conditional`
off `GameState.abilityActivationsThisTurn` (keyed like
`abilityResolutionsThisTurn`, counted as each activation is made, only for
an ability whose effect asks). The scaffolder reads both limit lines and the
condition's "if". Keys: condition:ability-activated-count-this-turn,
cost:activate-no-more-than-n-times-each-turn, cost:activation-limit-by-count.
Proving cards: Pit Imp, Sewer Rats, Withering Wisps, Dragon Whelp.

### Can't play lands, and casting barred by zone — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:cast-and-activate-restrictions`'s
first unit, features only. The `prohibit` effect takes `lands: true`
("target player can't play lands this turn" — rule 305.2, a land from any
zone; a `turnProhibitions` entry read by `whyLandPlayProhibited`). A static
`prohibits` takes `from` / `notFrom` (zone lists) binding its `spells` and
`lands` only to a card in, or outside, those zones as the cast or play
begins (rule 601.2a — `prohibitionReachesZone`): "your opponents can't cast
spells from anywhere other than their hands" (`notFrom: ["hand"]`), "you
can't play lands or cast spells from your hand" (`from: ["hand"]`). A
`player-effect`'s `cantCast` takes `from` too (Memory Vessel's lock). "Players
can't play lands" was already `prohibits.lands` (Null Chamber's) with `{}` —
only marked. The scaffolder reads the effect line and the three static
lines. Keys: effect:prohibit-land-plays, effect:prohibit-cast-by-zone.
Proving cards: Turf Wound, Pardic Miner, Territorial Dispute, Drannith
Magistrate, Experimental Frenzy. Left: Memory Vessel's "until your next
