### Face down by an effect — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:face-down`, features only (rule 708.2).
A fifth face-down kind, **`"effect"`**: put or turned face down by an effect
that neither manifests nor cloaks, turned face up only by a morph or disguise
cost (702.37e). **`turn-face-down`** turns a permanent face down in place
(708.2a; nothing to one already face down, 708.2b, or a double-faced one,
712.16). **`faceDown`** on `put-onto-battlefield` and on `look-and-choose`'s
`enterAs` enters a card face down (708.3) — `true` the plain 2/2, or the
characteristics the effect lists (**`FaceDownAs`**, `GameObject.faceDown.as`:
"2/2 Cyberman artifact creature", "5/5 artifact creature", "Forest land",
each an internal definition in `cards/face-down.ts`). **`manifest`'s `from`**
manifests the top card of another player's library under the manifesting
player (110.2a), and a look-and-choose from another's library puts its cards
under the effect's controller. The **`cant-turn-face-up`** restriction
refuses the turn-face-up special actions. Keys: mechanic:face-down (partial),
effect:turn-face-down, effect:put-onto-battlefield-face-down,
effect:manifest-from-other-library, static:cant-turn-face-up. Proving cards:
Cyber Conversion, Yedora, Grave Gardener, Tezzeret, Cruel Machinist, Orochi
Soul-Reaver, Unable to Scream. Still open: casting face down for X
(Illusionary Mask), a face-down exile linked to a token (Ugin, the
Ineffable), manifest from a hand with a "for each card manifested this way"
count (Kozilek, the Broken Reality), "as this enters, turn all other …
face down" (Ixidron), a copy that lasts until turned face down (Vesuvan
Shapeshifter), a trigger's "its owner" player (Thieving Amalgam).

### Casting abilities given to the spells you cast — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:grant-abilities-to-spells`, its group
(grant-convoke, grant-improvise, grant-prowl-to-spells,
grant-undaunted-to-exiled-spell), features only. `grantsToSpells` takes
`convoke`, `improvise` and `prowl` (a cost), judged through `castGrantReaches`
where the card is as it's cast — which now honours `firstEachTurn` there too
(Peri Brown). Convoke and improvise on one spell (one printed, one granted) tap
creatures for convoke and artifacts for improvise in one payment: a creature
pays generic or one of its colours, an artifact that isn't a creature only
generic (rules 702.51a, 702.126a); the offer says `alsoImprovise` and the
client words it. A granted prowl is an alternative cost under the printed
prowl's condition, answering `self-prowl-paid`. `allow-cast-from-exile` takes
`whileExiled` and `undaunted` (rule 702.125, an impulse permission's
`undaunted`, beside a printed one — 702.125c). The scaffolder reads "[kind]
spells you cast [from exile] have convoke / improvise / prowl {cost}". Proving
cards: Chief Engineer, Inspiring Statuary, Hunting Velociraptor, Durnan of the
Yawning Portal. Not built: "the next spell you cast this turn has
improvise" (Archway of Innovation; convoke is built, below), and a
`hasConvoke` filter still reads only a printed convoke.

### Alternative costs paid from the hand: discard, exile for X, reveal — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:alt-cost-discard-cards` with
cost:alt-cost-exile-card-sets-x and cost:alt-cost-reveal-hand, features only
(rule 118.9). `alternativeCost.discard` ("discard a Plains card", `others`
for Foil's "and another card"), chosen as the cost is paid (601.2h);
`exileFromHand.manaValueX` (the Shoals: the variant offered once per X a
matching card in hand has, X announced — 107.3a, 601.2b — and counted in the
mana value on the stack, 202.3e); `revealHand` (701.20a) under a `hand-size`
condition that now takes a `filter` ("no land cards in hand"). The scaffolder
reads all three lines. Proving cards: Abolish, Foil, Sickening Shoal,
Nourishing Shoal, Land Grant. Left to `author-freed`: Flameshot, Outbreak,
Snag, Blazing Shoal, Disrupting Shoal, Shining Shoal.

### Kicker costs that aren't mana, and life per target — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:kicker-nonmana-cost` with
cost:life-per-target, features only. `kicker.also` (buyback's non-mana
costs, rule 702.33a — any cost) already sacrificed a filtered permanent; it
now takes `returnToHand` (paid as an alternative cost's, 601.2h) and
`tapUntapped` (the kicked variant's `tapCost`, as teamwork's), and works on a
plain kicker and on entwine (702.42a) alike. `additionalCost.payLifePerTarget`
is "this spell costs N life more to cast for each target" (601.2f, the
targets chosen first, 601.2c): the offer's `targetCount` range stops at what
the caster's life pays (119.4). The scaffolder reads "Kicker—[return / tap /
sacrifice]" on a permanent, "Entwine—[cost]" and the life-per-target line.
Proving cards: Vicious Offering, Goblin Barrage, Betrayal of Flesh, Arctic
Merfolk, Blood Tribute, Phyrexian Purge. Left to `author-freed`: the sacrifice
kickers' other cards (Final Flourish, Stomped by the Foot, Vayne's Treachery,
Primal Growth) and Chocobo Kick; Dralnu's Pet still needs the discarded
card's mana value (`effect:cost-discarded-card-ref`).

### The card discarded to pay a cost — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:sacrificed-object-lki`'s open key
`effect:cost-discarded-card-ref`, features only. A discard that pays a
spell's or an ability's cost (an activated ability's `cost.discard`, a
spell's additional, optional, kicker, alternative, jump-start, retrace or
permission discard) is remembered on that spell or ability as it's paid
(rules 602.2b, 601.2h — once a chosen discard is answered, at once for a
forced or random one; `LastKnownRefs.discarded`, through the `discard`
decision's `forCost`), each card with its stint where the discard put it (a
madness card's exile too). Its effect reads it as the `"discarded"`
`AmountRef` ("the discarded card's mana value") and the `discarded`
condition ("if the discarded card was a creature card"). The scaffolder now
reads "Discard a card" / "Discard a [type] card" as an ability cost, and the
"discarded card" amount and condition lines. Proving cards: Volrath the
Fallen, Moria Scavenger, Grab the Prize, Stormscale Anarch. Left to
`author-freed`: Hisoka, Minamo Sensei, Land's Edge, Mercurial Chemister,
Necromancer's Stockpile, Pyromancy, Slumbering Tora; Dralnu's Pet's kicked
counters are an enters replacement, which doesn't read the spell's cost;
Malcolm, Alluring Scoundrel's discard is the effect's own, not a cost.

### A granted ability naming the permanent that granted it — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:modifier-grants-activated`'s group
cost:granted-ability-sacrifice-granter, effect:granted-activated-refers-to-granter
and effect:granted-trigger-refers-to-granter, features only (rule 201.5a).
A static's granted activated or triggered ability records the granting
permanent and its stint in its `GrantedAbilityRef` as it's activated or
triggered, kept on the stack object as `LastKnownRefs.granter`; the
`"granter"` `EffectTargetRef` names it (acted on only while it's that
permanent, 400.7; read as it last existed once gone, 608.2h), a `damage`'s
`from: "granter"` has it deal the damage, and `sacrifice: "granter"` is a
cost. The scaffolder reads "Equipped/Enchanted creature has '…'" lines
whose ability sacrifices, returns or has the granter deal damage. Proving
cards: Ninja's Kunai, Trusty Boomerang, Archery Training, Spare Dagger. Left
to `author-freed`: Blazing Torch, Rakdos Riteknife, Trickster's Talisman
(and Grothama, still waiting on its draw count). Still missing: Fishing
Pole's "Tap Fishing Pole" cost (a tap of the granter), Hold for Ransom's
"Hold for Ransom's controller" (the granter's controller as a player), and
Hellish Rebuke's granter, a spell's caster (a one-shot modifier grant, not a
static); Dire Flail's record has no Oracle text to check.

### One-shot grants to the next or first matching spell — built 2026-10-31

The rest of the need-keys Phase 2 family `static:grant-abilities-to-spells`,
its group (grant-cascade, grant-cascade-next-spell,
grant-sunburst-to-next-artifact, grant-to-first-matching-spell,
next-creature-spell-flash, next-spell-uncounterable) and the Tier B
effect:next-spell-has-convoke, features only. **`grant-spells-this-turn`**
takes `filter` and `next`: the first matching spell its controller casts
after it resolves uses it up as it becomes cast (rule 601.2i,
`Game.spendNextSpellGrants` — the record's `usedBy`), so the grant is that
spell's alone, cascade included (its "costs less" reads that spell, 702.85a).
Beside `triggered` (now optional) it gives `cantBeCountered` (701.5f,
`spellCantBeCountered`), `convoke` (702.51a, offered while the spell that
would use it up is cast) and `entersWithCounters`. Savage Summoning's flash
is Quicken's `castAsThoughFlash` `next`, already there. **`enters-with-counters`'
`counter: "sunburst"`** is a granted sunburst (702.44a), set by a next-spell
delayed trigger from a mana ability. **`grantsToSpells.firstEachTurn`** over a
filter's `castFrom` or `manaFrom` reads each earlier spell as it was cast. The
scaffolder reads "the next [kind] spell you cast this turn has cascade / has
convoke / can't be countered", "the next instant spell and the next sorcery
spell … each have cascade" and "the first spell you cast [from exile] each
turn [that mana from a Treasure was spent to cast] has cascade". Proving
cards: Dark Apostle, Sloppity Bilepiper, Overmaster, Insist, Savage
Summoning, Wand of the Worldsoul, Solar Array, Wild-Magic Sorcerer, Rain of
Riches. Left to `author-freed`: Smoldering Stagecoach, TARDIS (its "you may
planeswalk" does nothing outside Planechase, rule 701.31a), Bigger on the
Inside (a granted ability adding mana for a target player), Theorist's Proxy.

### Graveyard cast permissions: other graveyards, and a card's own costs — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `zone:graveyard-cast-permissions`, both of its
groups, features only. **Another player's graveyard**: the resolved
`player-effect` `castFromGraveyard` takes `whose` (a `PlayerScope` fixed as it
resolves — The Great Work's "any graveyard", Whispersteel Dagger's "that
player's"), `once` (one spell, then used up), `spendAs` (mana as though any
color, for that spell only — rule 609.4b, read through the permission the cast
names) and `exileAfterwards`; a one-shot grant on a card (`grant-graveyard-cast`)
now reaches a card its caster doesn't own; and `cast-now` takes `from:
"any-graveyard"` (Spectral Arcanist). The caster controls such a spell and its
owner owns it (rule 108.3). **A card's own permission with costs**
(`castSelfFromGraveyard`, the costs `GraveyardCastCosts` shared with a
permanent's `castFromGraveyard`): life, discards (chosen after the mana, as
retrace's), a sacrifice, exiling N other cards of a kind, an alternative mana
cost (rule 118.9 — no other alternative cost with it), {1} more per other card
of a kind (rule 601.2f), and "or from exile". None is offered that can't be
paid (601.2h). The scaffolder reads "You may cast ~ from your graveyard [or from
exile] by … in addition to paying its other costs / by paying {cost} [and …]
rather than paying its mana cost / if you pay {1} more … for each other
creature card in your graveyard". Keys: graveyard-cast-permissions,
cast-from-any-graveyard, cast-self-from-graveyard, graveyard-self-cast-extra-cost,
self-graveyard-cast-cost-increase, self-graveyard-cast-discard-cost,
graveyard-cast-exile-cost. Proving cards: Whispersteel Dagger, Spectral
Arcanist, Demonic Embrace, Raffine's Guidance, Helbrute, Risen Executioner,
Squee, the Immortal. Not built: Yawgmoth's Will (its turn-long exile
replacement, and the owner's choice between it and buyback — rule 616.1), a
count of sacrifices (Scourge of Nel Toth), and the riders on a spell cast
under a card's own permission (Worldheart Phoenix's counters, Undead
Sprinter's) — `zone:graveyard-cast-rider-own-permission`.

### Attack requirements narrowed — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:combat-restriction-extensions`, its top
group, features only. Kardur's **`attack-requirement`** rule (`AttackRequirementRule`)
narrowed by rule 508.1d: `target` (one creature, by stint — 400.7),
`controlledByTarget` (a player's creatures as attackers are declared),
`attacks` (`"you"`, `"source"` or a target slot: obeyed only by attacking that
player or planeswalker, so binding only while it can be attacked — counted
with encore's in `aimedRequirements`), and `duration` (`this-turn`,
`that-players-next-turn`, `controllers-next-turn`, `controllers-next-combat`;
until your next turn when absent). The scaffolder reads "target creature
attacks you / target opponent this turn if able", "… during its controller's
next combat phase if able", "creatures target opponent controls attack this
turn if able" and "during target player's next turn, creatures that player
controls attack you / ~ if able". Keys: effect:must-attack-specific-player,
effect:target-attacks-target-player-this-turn,
effect:must-attack-target-players-creatures,
effect:must-attack-controllers-next-combat, static:must-attack-planeswalker,
static:global-attack-requirement. Proving cards: Alluring Siren, Taunt, Dulcet
Sirens, Trench Behemoth, Imaginary Threats, Maximum Carnage. Still open:
static:must-attack-specific-player — a static's "all creatures attack enchanted
creature's controller each combat if able" (Public Enemy), Galactus's "an
opponent with the most life", Silver Surfer's "until the end of your next
turn"; static:attacks-this-turn-if-able-target (Gogo).

### Creatures in a blocking relation with the source — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 families `effect:target-spec-additions` (its top group,
the blocking-relation targets) and `filter:blocked-by-source`, built as one,
features only. New filter **`blockingRelation: { relation, of? }`**: a creature
blocking the permanent applying it (`"blocking"`), blocked by it
(`"blocked-by"`) or either, or — `of: "host"` — the creature that one is
attached to; through `blockedAttackersOf` (a band blocked whole, 702.22h), none
for a creature removed from combat (506.4), and a source that has left read off
its new `lastKnown.blockedAttackers`/`blockers` (608.2b, 608.2h — Tinder Wall
sacrificed to its own ability). The same filter serves targets, `-all` effects
and an `of: "host"` Aura or Equipment. The scaffolder reads the target phrases,
"each/all creatures blocking …", "At end of combat," and "It deals" on a
permanent's own activated ability. Keys: effect:target-creature-blocking-source,
effect:target-creature-source-is-blocking, effect:target-creature-blocking-attached,
filter:blocked-by-source, filter:blocking-or-blocked-by-source,
filter:blocking-specific-creature, and Tier B effect:damage-each-creature-blocking-source.
Proving cards: Goblin Javelineer, Tinder Wall, Plasma Caster, Wall of Nets,
Kjeldoran Frostbeast, Coils of the Medusa, Battle-Scarred Goblin. Still open:
"all creatures it blocked this turn" (Defiant Vanguard — a creature removed from
combat still counts, so a per-turn record).

### Each permanent of a kind deals damage — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:each-creature-damages-itself`, its group
whole, features only. One **`each-deals-damage`** effect: every permanent
matching a filter deals damage — its own power (`"its-power"`) or a fixed
amount — to `to`: `"itself"`, a target slot, the trigger's object, or `{ who }`
(each opponent), with `controlledByTarget`, `exceptSource` and `exceptTarget`.
Each permanent is its own source (rule 120.1: its lifelink, deathtouch, infect;
a lifelinker's own gain, 702.15e), who deals what is read once before any is
dealt (608.2h), and it is one damage event (`withDamageBatch` — enrage once); a
token stack deals once per token (to itself, each token woken; else as that
many sources). The scaffolder reads "each [kind] deals damage to itself equal
to its power", "… equal to its power / N damage to [target | each opponent]",
and "Choose target X. Each … to that creature." Keys:
each-creature-damages-itself, each-creature-deals-damage-to-each-opponent,
each-creature-deals-power-damage-to-target, each-permanent-deals-damage.
Proving cards: Solar Blaze, Wave of Reckoning, Moonlight Hunt, Bartz and Boko,
Signature Slam, Alpha Brawl.

### Distributing counters: a total that is an amount, and among permanents chosen as it resolves — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:distribute-counters`, its top group,
features only. **Among targets** (rule 601.2d) the total was already any
`EffectAmount` read as the division is announced (the divide-among-targets
family); proved here for counters — Undercity Upheaval's creature cards in the
graveyard "as you cast this spell", Quirion Beastcaller's counters it died
with, Broodlord's X. **Among permanents chosen on resolution** (rule 608.2d)
is the new `counters-distributed` effect: the total read as it resolves, the
recipients chosen untargeted (`choose-permanents`, a token stack once per
token, none to the total), then a share each (`choose-number` per chosen one
but the last, at least one each) — two existing decisions, no client change;
`controlledByTarget` reads "target player controls". `choose-value` takes a
`prompt`. The scaffolder reads "distribute X [kind] counters among any number
of [other] target …, where X is …" (a `counters-divided` with `divided`) and
the untargeted "distribute X … among any number of [permanents], where X is
…" / "a number of … equal to …" (counts, counters on it, creature cards in
your graveyard, {S} spent). Keys: effect:distribute-counters-among-chosen,
effect:distribute-counters-variable-total. Proving cards: Undercity Upheaval,
Quirion Beastcaller, Broodlord, Vastwood Hydra, Feast of the Victorious Dead,
Blessing of Frost (`counters-distributed.test.ts`). Left: Stumpsquall Hydra
(a required recipient, "among it and any number of commanders"), Numa (a
reflexive "you may pay {X}{X}. When you do"), Spoils of War (X defined by an
opponent's graveyard as cast), Yannik (check its exile-until-leaves reflexive),
Crashing Wave (waterbend {X} as an additional cost), Invoke Justice
(`controlledByTarget` is built; check its return), effect:saga-chapter-divided.

### Counters put or removed this way — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:this-way-results`, its top group, features
only. Most of it was there already (`ThisWayKind`'s `"counters-put"` and
`"counters-removed"`, the `thisWay` filter clause and amount); the records
predate them. What was missing: **a player's counters** — the `thisWay:
"counters-removed"` amount (no `filter`/`sumOf`) now adds the counters a
`remove-player-counters` took off (rule 122.1 — Leeches' "loses all poison
counters … that much damage"); **`removeCounter.count: "all"`** — the cost
"Remove all +1/+1 counters from [this]", payable with none, the number removed
the ability's X (rules 601.2h via 602.2b — Catti-brie of Mithral Hall, and
Molten Hydra, Relic Amulet, Torture Chamber, Geometric Nexus, Vish Kal, Jar of
Eyeballs, Essence Bottle, Sage of Hours, Hankyu untriaged); and **the
enters-battlefield replacement's `removeAllCountersFrom`** with `counters`'
`"removed-as-enters"` (rule 614.1c — Thief of Blood). The scaffolder reads
"then double … / goad each creature that had a [kind] counter put on it this
way", "that many / that much" after a removal (a player's too, "that player"
its target), "[target player] loses all poison counters", the remove-all cost
with its "where X is …", "damage … equal to the number of … removed this way"
and "that much damage", and Thief of Blood's line. Keys:
effect:counters-put-this-way, effect:counters-removed-this-way. Proving cards:
Fangs of Kalonia, Spectacular Showdown, Give // Take, Leeches, Catti-brie of
Mithral Hall, Thief of Blood (`counters-this-way.test.ts`). Not built:
Magnanimous Magistrate's "you may remove that many … If you do" (whether
fewer than that many may be removed is a rules question with no ruling);
overload isn't scaffolded.

### Cards exiled with the source put onto the battlefield — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:put-onto-battlefield-options`, its top
group, features only (rules 110.2a, 607.2a, 610.3c). **`look-and-choose`'s
`zone: "exiled-with-source"`** offers only the cards linked to the source in
the stint the ability refers to, under the chooser's control, with the exile
branch now carrying `withCounters`, `enterAs` and `attacking` as the others
do — Nautiloid Ship's "you may put a creature card exiled with this Vehicle
onto the battlefield under your control", Chaos Shrine's Black Crystal's
finality counter. **`return-exiled-by-source`'s linked battlefield return**
takes `under: "you"` ("put all cards exiled with it onto the battlefield" —
the putting player's control, 110.2a, Underworld Sentinel), `others` (not the
cards this resolution just exiled) and `filter` ("all other permanent cards"
— Mirror of Life Trapping). **`exile-graveyard`'s `linked`** exiles a
graveyard with the source. Keys: effect:put-exiled-with-source-onto-battlefield,
effect:return-other-cards-exiled-with-source, effect:exile-graveyard-linked.
Proving cards: Nautiloid Ship, Chaos Shrine's Black Crystal, Underworld
Sentinel, Mirror of Life Trapping (`put-exiled-with-source.test.ts`). Left:
effect:return-with-counters-this-way (Lagrella's "when an exiled card enters
under your control this way, put two +1/+1 counters on it" is a trigger on
the until-leaves return), Ghost Vacuum's "1/1 Spirit in addition" (a base
P/T as it enters), The Darkness Crystal's replacement exile and its target
card in exile, Yggdrasil's linked library exile, Sisters of Stone Death's
blocking filter.

### A card at random from a hand or from the cards exiled with the source — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:random-choice`, its top group, features
only (rules 400.2, 607.2a, 701.20a). **`choose-random-card`'s `zone: "hand"`**
picks from a hand — `whose` now also a player named through a target
(`{ controllerOfTarget: 0 }`) — and **`show`** says how it's seen: `"reveal"`
to every player (`revealedThisTurn`), `"look"` to the effect's controller
alone (a one-card `choose-from-zone` with nothing to pick, closed by its
viewer), or nothing when `then`'s move shows it. **`zone:
"exiled-with-source"`** picks among the cards linked to the source in the
stint the ability refers to (`cardsExiledWithSource`). **`slot`** puts the
picked card beside the ability's own targets (`ResolutionContext.withFoundAt`)
instead of replacing them as target 0 — Cursed Scroll's "any target" and
Singe-Mind Ogre's "that card's mana value". `then` is optional (a bare reveal
or look). Riding along: **`search-library`'s `linked`** exiles the finds with
the source. Keys: effect:random-card-from-hand,
effect:random-card-from-linked-exile. Proving cards: Cursed Scroll, Magus of
the Scroll, Hired Torturer, Singe-Mind Ogre, Urza's Bauble, Skyship
Weatherlight (`random-card-from-zone.test.ts`). The freed cards each need a
look (Wand of Ith's "unless they pay life", Friendly Fire's damage to the
creature and the player at once, Dementia Sliver's granted ability, River
Song's Diary's imprint replacement); the family's other units (random target,
random mode announced, random among chosen) are separate.

### Enlist — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `keyword:enlist`, features only (rule 702.154).
**`StaticAbility.enlist`** (the `enlist()` helper) is an optional cost to
attack (702.154b, 508.1g): the declaration taps its attackers and pays any
attack tax, then — before any attacker is announced, so before anything
triggers on the attack (508.1k) — each attacker with enlist is asked a
`choose-permanents` of up to one untapped, nonattacking creature its
controller controls with haste or no summoning sickness
(`GameState.pendingEnlist`, `Game.promptNextEnlist`/`applyEnlist`), and the
attack is announced once the last is answered (`Game.announceAttackers`).
The creature chosen taps, and the linked "when you do, +X/+0" (607.2h) is a
reflexive trigger the engine adds, X the tapped creature's power as it
resolves (`powerOf: "tapped"`). A **`creature-tapped-for`** event (`as:
"enlist"`) fires the new **`enlists`** trigger (702.154c — the trigger object
the enlisted creature), and the attacker records the combat
(`GameObject.enlistedIn`, kept in last-known information) for the new
**`enlistedThisCombat`** filter clause. No new decision kind: the bots'
`choose-permanents` "you may choose one" candidates answer it. Key:
keyword:enlist. Proving cards: Coalition Warbrute, Guardian of New Benalia,
Aradesh, the Founder (`enlist.test.ts`). Not built: 702.154d's several
instances on one creature (none printed).

### Who crewed what: crew tallies, crews-or-saddles triggers, can't crew — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `keyword:crew`, its top group and a Tier B key,
features only (rules 702.122b-d, 702.171c). Each creature tapped for a crew
cost records the Vehicle and its stint (`GameObject.crewedThisTurn`, cleared
at cleanup and by a zone change, as `saddledThisTurn` is): the new filter
clauses **`crewedSource`** ("a creature that crewed it this turn", bound to
`crewedBy` in an effect) and **`crewedBySource`** ("a Vehicle crewed by ~
this turn"). Crew and saddle costs now announce each creature as a
`creature-tapped-for` (`as: "crew" | "saddle"`), firing the new
**`crews-or-saddles`** trigger (`as` the keywords, the trigger object the
Vehicle or Mount). The **`cant-crew`** restriction keeps a creature out of a
crew cost's offer and payment. The client's log reads the new event. Keys:
crewed-it-this-turn, crews-or-saddles, cant-crew, and crewed-by-this-turn
(partly). Proving cards: Speedway Fanatic, Veteran Motorist, Reckless
Velocitaur, Subterranean Schooner, Getaway Car, Balthier and Fran, Revoke
Privileges (`crew-tallies.test.ts`). Still open: Golden Argosy's "exile each
creature that crewed it this turn, return them" (a `flicker` over a group),
Luxurious Locomotive's count of crewers including departed ones (the
ruling), Adrestia's "if an Assassin crewed it this turn", Mindlink Mech's
becomes-crewed trigger.

### Cards a player owns, taken from exile — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:put-opponent-exiled-card-into-graveyard`,
features only (rules 108.3, 406.3, 601.2h). **`look-and-choose`'s `zone:
"exile"`** offers the exiled cards a filter takes (`ownedBy: "opponent"`), or
with `from` the cards that player owns, to a graveyard (each to its owner's)
or onto the battlefield under the chooser's control; **`needsMin`** makes it
all of `min` or nothing. **`this-way: "chosen"`** reads the picks wherever
they went, so a processor's "If you do" holds under Rest in Peace (the
ruling). As a cost: **`AbilityCost.putFromExile`** and
**`additionalCost.putFromExile`**, chosen once on the stack. A face-down card
in exile (exiled face down — now stamped with `exiledFaceDown.group` — or
foretold) is choosable only by an owner-only filter, hidden from a chooser who
can't look, and taken from its group at random (the `choose-from-zone`
decision's `faceDownGroups`; the client's zone viewer picks it as its back).
Keys: cost:put-opponent-exiled-card-into-graveyard,
effect:put-exiled-card-into-graveyard, effect:put-exiled-lands-under-your-control.
Left: Ulamog's Despoiler's "As this creature enters, you may put two …" (an
optional cost in an enters replacement) and Pull from Eternity's
`target:card-in-exile`. Proving cards: Wasteland Strangler, Ulamog's
Nullifier, Oblivion Sower, Memory Theft, Void Attendant, Processor Assault.

### Meld — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:meld`, features only (rules 701.42,
712.4). The **`meld`** effect — "if you both own and control ~ and a creature
named X, exile them, then meld them into Y" — makes that test as it resolves,
exiles both, and puts two cards of Y's pair onto the battlefield as one new
object (`GameObject.melded`) with Y's characteristics; Y is a definition with
**`meldOf`** (the pair, never a deck card), and the permanent's mana value is
its fronts' summed (712.8g). The **`meld-ready`** condition is a trigger's
intervening if. Several counterparts are a `choose-permanents` choice. Leaving,
it's two cards (712.21), and `leftWith` lets a blink or an exile link find both
(mutate's components too). Key: meld. Proving cards: Graf Rats, Chittering
Host, Hanweir Battlements, Hanweir, the Writhing Township.

### "−X" loyalty costs — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:loyalty-x`, features only. **`loyaltyCost:
"-x"`**: X is announced as the ability is activated (rule 107.3a) on the
activation's existing `xValue`, offered as `xCost` from 0 to the loyalty the
source has (606.6), and that many loyalty counters are removed; the ability's X
is that value (107.3k), read as `amount: "x"` or a filter's `n: "x"`. No new
decision: the client's X prompt covers it. v2 weighs each X from 1 up; v1 keeps
a counter. No card has "+X" yet. Key: loyalty-x. Proving cards: Chandra Nalaar,
Chandra, Flamecaller, Tezzeret the Seeker.

### Choices by other players — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:choices-by-other-players`, its top group,
features only. **`choose-among-targets`**: as a spell resolves, a player (its
targets' controller, or an opponent through `choose-opponent`) picks `count`
of its still-legal targets, and the chosen and the rest each take a pile fate.
**`opponentChoosesTargets`** on a spell or activated ability (rule 601.2c):
offered once per opponent (`otherChooser`), the named opponent answers the
existing `choose-targets` mid-cast — a `cast-now` cast too — with
`controlledBy: "chooser"` for "they control". **`castModal.chosenBy:
"opponent"`** (rule 700.2e): they answer `choose-modes` as it's cast and are
"that player" of the modes. Not yet: a triggered ability's "of an opponent's
choice" (Mausoleum Turnkey), Mogg Assassin's "that opponent", targets chosen
after an opponent's mode (Fatal Lore). Keys: opponent-chooses-target,
opponent-chooses-mode, opponent-chooses-among-targets. Proving cards:
Incriminate, Barrin's Spite, Retribution, Wake to Slaughter, Evangelize,
Volcanic Offering, Magus of the Arena, Library of Lat-Nam, Misfortune.

### Assist — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:assist`, features only. A card's
**`assist: true`** (rule 702.132a): its cast offers come again once per other
player whose mana could pay some of it — the offer's **`assist`** and
**`assistMax`** — judged as though that player paid all they could, an `{X}`
spell's X reaching further. A cast naming one (`Action.assist`) waits on that
player's **`choose-number`** (the existing decision, with an `AssistAsk`
continuation): 0 up to the most their mana pays of the total cost's generic,
increases and reductions included. Their mana pays first and counts as spent on
the spell; the caster pays the rest, or the whole cast is reversed (rule 733.1).
Bots never ask; v1 answers 0 and v2 searches. Not offered on a `cast-now` cast
(Engine rules gaps). Key: assist. Proving cards: Charging Binox, Gang Up, Out of
Bounds.

### The turn history's graveyard tallies — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `stat:turn-event-tallies`, its graveyard group,
features only. **`TurnHistory.discarded`** — every card a player discarded
(rule 701.9a), a madness card discarded into exile too (702.35a): "for each
card you've discarded this turn" (`{ turnHistory: "discarded" }`), "if you've
discarded a card this turn" (`{ kind: "turn-history", what: "discarded" }`).
**`TurnHistory["to-graveyard"]`** — everything put into a player's graveyard,
each with the zone it came from: cards from a library, hand or the stack off
the graveyard event, permanents from the battlefield (tokens too, 111.7) off
the leave event with their stint, so a filter reads them as they last existed
there (603.10a). The `turnHistory` amount and `turn-history` condition take
**`from` / `notFrom`** (`TurnHistoryOrigin`); the amount a player **`target`**
slot. The **`discardedThisTurn`** filter is a card discarded this turn and
unmoved since — kept through a declined madness card's exile-to-graveyard move
(Shadow of the Grave's ruling). Keys: cards-discarded-this-turn,
cards-library-to-graveyard-this-turn, cards-put-into-graveyard-this-turn,
permanents-to-graveyard-from-battlefield-this-turn. Proving cards: Change of
Fortune, Gilt-Blade Prowler, Recalibrate, Cruel Calculations, Fraying Sanity,
Structural Assault, Ichor Shade, Shadow of the Grave. Still open in the family:
attackers by filter, entered-as-entered, and the one-card tallies (exiled,
sacrificed, searched, surveilled, tokens created, turned face up); gravestorm
(Follow the Bodies) is `effect:copy-spell-extensions`'.

### Can't-do effects for a duration — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:cant-activate-abilities-until-next-turn`,
features only. **`prohibit`'s `regeneration`** — "target creature can't be
regenerated this turn" (rule 701.19c: its shields stay but aren't applied),
on a target or, with `dealtDamageThisWay: "creature"`, on every creature the
resolution has dealt damage to so far (Incinerate, Flamebreak; damage
prevented was never dealt), kept as `GameObject.cantBeRegeneratedTurn`.
**`prohibit`'s `monarch`** — "you can't become the monarch this turn"
(`PlayerState.cantBecomeMonarchTurn`; a monarch leaving passes it to the next
player who can, rule 725.4), with **`become-monarch`'s target slot** ("target
opponent becomes the monarch"). **`prohibit`'s `duration:
"until-your-next-turn"`** for a target's abilities alone (Dovin Baan's +1,
still blocked by its emblem); "can't attack or block and its activated
abilities can't be activated until your next turn" is `detain` (Mythos of
Vadrok). A damage-prevention replacement's **`thenCountsUnprevented`** keeps
"that many" when the damage can't be prevented (Jared's ruling, rule 615.12).
Keys: cant-be-regenerated-this-turn, cant-become-monarch-this-turn,
target-player-becomes-monarch, cant-activate-abilities-until-next-turn.
Proving cards: Incinerate, Flamebreak, Disintegrate, Jared Carthalion, True
Heir, Mythos of Vadrok. Still open beside it: `effect:cant-phase-out` (Spatial
Binding, Ertai's Familiar — "until your next upkeep", and whether a
permanent that can't phase out still phases out indirectly with its host is
a rules question); Bone Shaman's granted "creatures dealt damage by this
creature this turn can't be regenerated this turn", Whippoorwill's and
Runesword's other halves.

### Base power or base toughness alone — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:set-base-power-only`, features only.
`animate`'s `power` and `toughness` are each optional: one alone sets base
power or base toughness alone in layer 7b (rule 613.4b — a new
`PtModifier.setPower` beside the existing `setToughness`, ordered with
`setPt` by timestamp), fixed or a live amount read once as it resolves
(608.2h), until end of turn or indefinitely, the other left as it is;
counters and pumps still add on top. Only a full set emits
`permanent-animated`. The bots read a small lone set without keywords as a
shrink. The scaffolder reads "(~ | target …) has base (power | toughness) N
until end of turn", "until end of turn, … has base … N (and gains K)", "until
end of turn, … gains K and has base … N" and "~'s base (power | toughness)
becomes equal to [N plus] (target creature's power | the number of … cards
in your graveyard | a battlefield count)". Keys:
effect:set-base-power-only, effect:set-base-toughness-only. Still open on
their cards: Vhati il-Dal ("base power 1 **or** base toughness 1", a choice
as it resolves — no effect-level "or" choice), Sentinel (a target "blocking
or blocked by this creature", effect:target-creature-source-is-blocking),
Belligerent Yearling ("you may", the entering Dinosaur's power — likely
authorable), PuPu UFO (Towns), Crater Elemental (formidable's activation
condition), Symmetry Sage, Singing Tree (likely authorable). Proving cards:
Island of Wak-Wak, Turtle-Duck, Riptide Mangler, Chariot of the Sun, Wall of
Tombstones.

### P/T scaled by a hand size or a life total — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:pt-per-amount`, its first group (a
granted P/T amount read from a player's own state), features only.
`grantPtPerCount.cardsInHand` (layer 7c) widens from the counting player's
own hand (`true`) to `"opponents"` (every opponent's hand together — Enemy of
Enlightenment) and `"affected-controller"` (each affected creature's
controller's — Righteous Authority); a new `grantPtPerCount.lifeTotal` reads
the controller's life total, unclamped (rule 107.1b — Death's Shadow at
negative life gets a bonus); and a `CountSpec` `"your-life-total"` is the CDA
(rule 604.3 — Serra Avatar, Soul of Eternity). The scaffolder reads "(~ |
Enchanted creature | Equipped creature | All creatures | Creatures you
control) get(s) ±N/±N for each card in (your hand | your opponents' hands |
its controller's hand)", "… get(s) ±X/±X|0, where X is (your life total |
the number of cards in your hand)" and "~'s power and toughness are each
equal to your life total". Keys: static:pt-per-cards-in-hand,
static:pt-per-life-total, static:cda-life-total. Still open on these keys'
cards: Alrund (filter:foretold), Enduring Angel (effect:double-life-total and
its transform-instead replacement), Roiling Horror (life minus an opponent's,
suspend X), Ajani Goldmane's Avatar token (a token with the CDA, authorable).
Proving cards: Grim Strider, Enemy of Enlightenment, Righteous Authority,
Death's Shadow, Serra Avatar.

### Damage to the source — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:damage-to-source`, features only: `damage`'s
**`target: "source"`**, untargeted, nothing once the source has left (rule 400.7),
one token of a stack hit. With `from: { target: 0 }` something else deals it
(Goblin Tinkerer). The scaffolder reads "~ deals N damage to X and M damage to
itself", "~ deals N damage to itself", "that artifact deals damage equal to its mana
value to ~" and a whole "Flip a coin. If you win the flip, … If you lose the flip, …",
which is new. Key: effect:damage-to-source. Karplusan Yeti and Tracker need none
of it (Tahngarth's one-sided `fight`); Goblin Kaboomist still needs its Land Mine
token. Proving cards: Psionic Entity, Psionic Sliver, Goblin Bangchuckers, Goblin
Tinkerer.

### The same name as another named object — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `filter:same-name-as-card-in-graveyard`, features
only (rule 201.2a). `ShareReference` gains `{ graveyard: filter }`, with
`itsController` for "their graveyard" (live only), read in a trigger's filter and
a `trigger-object` condition. **`sameNameAsAnother: filter`** asks the whole
battlefield, a token stack its own namesake. `destroy-all` gains
`exceptTriggerObject` (rule 400.7), one token of a stack spared, so namesakes
entering together destroy each other. `nameForMatching` now names a permanent
gone since as it last existed (rule 608.2h), so a dead copy is named as what it
copied; `sameNameAs` and `sameNameAsAny` match either half of a split card (the
Shrines missed those). A `tapped-for-mana` trigger's filter reads with its source.
The scaffolder reads each proving card's lines but Pyromancer Ascension's "while"
line. Keys: filter:same-name-as-card-in-graveyard,
filter:same-name-as-trigger-object, filter:same-name-as-another-permanent,
filter:same-name-as-card-in-casters-graveyard, keyword:imprint-same-name (partly:
Thought Prison's and Mourner's Shield's shared color or mana value and Spellweaver
Helix's copy need keys of their own). Proving cards: Pyromancer Ascension, Bazaar
of Wonders, Dragonlord Kolaghan, Remembrance, Verdant Succession, Eye of
Singularity, Invader Parasite, Strata Scythe, Extraplanar Lens.

### The same name as a reference object — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-card-property-clauses`, its
same-name group, features only, on the shared-type group's `ShareReference`
(below), which gains `"exiled-with-source"` (the cards exiled with the source,
rule 607.2a, or until it leaves, 610.3) and `{ permanents: filter }`. One
**`sameNameWith`** clause (rule 201.2a, a reference object itself included):
bound in an effect to `sameNameAsAny`, read live in a static's prohibition or
a trigger's filter for the source, exiled-with-source, controlled and
permanents references. `ResolutionContext.permanentsMatching` now reads with
the source (`isSource: false` — "another creature you control"). The
scaffolder reads "(Your opponents | Players) can't (cast spells | play
nonbasic lands) with the same name as (the exiled card | a nontoken
permanent)" — linking the card's own exile — and "this creature and each
other creature with the same name as it get …". Keys:
condition:filter-same-name-as-source, filter:same-name-as-a-permanent,
condition:filter-same-name-as-permanent-you-control. Left in the group:
filter:same-name-as-trigger-spell (Harness the Storm's target filter, read as
the trigger is put on the stack — target legality, not an effect's filter),
condition:filter-same-name-as-chosen (Canoptek Wraith's land chosen as it
resolves). Still open
on built keys' cards: the copy-with-an-ability cards (Callidus Assassin, Evil
Twin, Pirated Copy), Retraced Image's reveal-then-maybe, Search the City.
Proving cards: Ixalan's Binding, Exclusion Ritual, Cornered Market, Mitotic
Manipulation, Pattern Matcher, Cylian Sunsinger.

### Shares a type with a reference object — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-dynamic-compare`, its
shared-type group, features only. The `CardFilter` clauses
**`sharesCardTypeWith`**, **`sharesPermanentTypeWith`**,
**`sharesCreatureTypeWith`** and the new **`sharesLandTypeWith`** all take one
**`ShareReference`**: `"sacrificed"`, `"trigger-object"`, `"source"`,
`"attached"` (the host, or the one last attached to as the Aura was
sacrificed), `"chosen"`, a target slot (as it last existed if it moved), or
`{ controlled: filter }` (every permanent you control matching it). Bound as
the effect applies to a `sharesTypeWith` entry: the reference objects always
match ("target creature **and each other** creature that shares…"), anything
else needs one of their types of that kind (rules 205.2a, 110.4, 205.3i,
205.3m; changeling 702.73a through `hasSubtype`). The scaffolder reads
radiance's "and each other X that shares a creature/card type with it", the
Crowns' "enchanted creature and other creatures that share a creature type
with it get / gain …", "destroy all creatures that share a creature type with
the sacrificed creature" with "They can't be regenerated.", and Hiveheart
Shaman's search. Keys: shares-creature-type-with-attached,
shares-creature-type-with-sacrificed, shares-creature-type-with-target,
shares-card-type-with-target, shares-land-type-with-controlled. Left on their
cards: Raise the Draugr's two targets sharing a type with each other (a
target-legality constraint between slots), Cemetery Gatekeeper's linked
exiled card, Spirit-Sister's Call's granted replacement. Proving cards: Crown
of Vigor, Crown of Ascension, Endemic Plague, Pyre of Heroes, Coward //
Killer, Counterlash, Reality Scramble, Hiveheart Shaman.

### Counters of chosen kinds removed — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:remove-any-counter-cost`, features only.
Both keys' core was B254's, unmarked: the `remove-counters` decision, the
cost's `removeCounter.kind: "any"` ("remove a counter from this creature" —
Glen Elendra Guardian) and the `remove-counters` effect's `counter: "any"`
("remove up to X counters from target permanent" — Hex Parasite). Added: a
`permanent` target slot's **`orPlayer`** ("target artifact, creature,
planeswalker, or opponent" — Price of Betrayal), a `remove-counters` aimed at
a **player** (rule 122.1: poison, energy, experience, rad, asked kind by kind
on `choose-modes` and removed together), **`anyController`** ("from among
permanents on the battlefield" — Eventide's Shadow), and the bots' plain
answer taking an opponent's best counters. Keys: remove-any-counter-cost,
remove-counters-any-kind. Still open on its cards: Cemetery Desecrator
(`decision:choose-from-any-graveyard`), Gwen Stacy
(`zone:impulse-while-you-control-source`).

### A sweep that leaves out its source — built 2026-11-01

`destroy-all`'s `exceptSource`, as `damage-all`'s and `exile-all`'s: "destroy all
other creatures", "all permanents other than this Equipment", "except for
Mageta". One token of a stack is the source and the rest of the stack goes.
Proving cards: Novablast Wurm, Tranquil Grove, Mageta the Lion, Worldslayer,
Elspeth Tirel, Desolation Giant (`destroy-all-except-source.test.ts`). Key:
effect:destroy-all-except-source. Still on its cards, freed by this: Amalia
Benavides Aguirre (explore, then a power check), Magister of Worth (a council
vote), Myojin of Cleansing Fire (a counter if cast from hand).

### A life total set — built 2026-11-01

`set-life-total` (`who`, `amount`) and two amounts, `startingLifeTotal` and
`lowestLifeTotal`. Rule 119.5 says the player gains or loses the difference, so
it is a real gain or loss: the triage's fear of firing life triggers was the
reverse of the rule, and the rulings on doublers agree. Every player's amount is
read before any life moves (Repay in Kind). Proving cards: Magister Sphinx,
Biorhythm, Repay in Kind, Oketra's Last Mercy, The Endstone, Resolute Archangel
(`set-life-total.test.ts`). Keys: effect:life-total-becomes,
amount:starting-life-total. Still open on their cards: Captive Audience
(`effect:enters-under-opponent-control`), Sorin Markov (`mechanic:control-player`),
Providence (`mechanic:leyline-opening-hand`), Worldfire
(`effect:exile-all-hands`), Shadow of Mortality (its cost reduces by the
difference from the starting total).

### Choosing among cards revealed from a hand — built 2026-11-01

`discard-chosen`'s `reveals` (an amount, read as it resolves) and `picks`:
"target player reveals three cards from their hand and you choose one of them".
The hand's owner picks which to show, by a `choose-from-zone` of destination
`"reveal"`, unless the hand holds no more than that; the chooser's choice is
over the revealed cards alone. No new decision kind, and the client's popup
already words both. A controller is asked what to keep back
(`thenChooseFromRevealed` in `decisions/choose-from-zone.ts`), a v2 bot's
candidates show its worst first (`revealForChoice`). Proving cards: Blackmail,
Mire's Toll, Noggin Whack, Cabal Interrogator
(`discard-chosen-revealed.test.ts`). Key:
effect:reveal-n-from-hand-you-choose-discard. Still open on its cards: Taster
of Wares (a permission to cast the exiled card while you control it), Klaw
(one plus a count, no parser line but the amount is there), Pulse of the Dross
(`condition:target-player-more-cards-in-hand-than-you`).

### Impulse windows — built 2026-11-01

`ImpulseDuration`, one vocabulary for `impulse-exile`'s `duration` and `exile`'s
`mayCast` (now any duration, and `play` for a land too): `your-next-turn-only`,
`until-end-of-combat-your-next-turn`, `until-next-exile`, `while-you-control`
(over for good at the first moment you don't: rule 611.2b), and `sourceDid`, a
live gate off the turn history (`attackedWith`, `loreCountersOn`). Proving
cards: Galvanic Relay, Goblin Researcher, Brazen Cannonade, Furious Rise,
Lightning, Security Sergeant, Victor Mancha, Runaway, Scholar of Combustion,
Containment Construct (`impulse-windows.test.ts`). Keys:
impulse-during-lore-counter-turns, impulse-during-your-next-turn-only,
impulse-gated-source-attacked-this-turn, impulse-until-end-of-combat-next-turn,
impulse-until-next-exile, impulse-while-you-control-source,
exile-discarded-play-this-turn, exile-from-graveyard-play-this-turn,
exile-target-play-until-next-turn. Still open on their cards: Summon:
Brynhildr (its Gestalt Mode gives a spell haste), Elkin Lair (a random exile
from the hand), Advanced Reconstruction (a random exile from the graveyard),
Taster of Wares (`effect:reveal-n-from-hand-you-choose-discard`), Gwen Stacy
(its back face's cast-from-exile trigger).

### Token copies of a linked source — built 2026-11-01

`create-token-copy`'s `of` reaches cards now: `"exiled-with-source"` (rules
607.2a, 610.3; `chooseOne` for "a card exiled with this"), `"exiled-as-cost"`
(`LastKnownRefs.exiledCost`, recorded as `exileFromGraveyard` is paid),
`"exiled-this-way"`, `{ eachExiled }`, each read as the printed card under the
effect's controller, with no token for an instant or sorcery card (rule
111.5); `"enchanted"` is the Aura's host. Also `count` as an amount,
`sacrificeAtEndOfCombat`, `atYourNextEndStep`, `exceptions.static`, and
`return-exiled-by-source`'s `linked: "graveyard"`. Proving cards: Mimic Vat,
The Cloning of Shredder, Followed Footsteps, Mardu Siegebreaker, Altaïr
Ibn-La'Ahad, Dollhouse of Horrors, Foggy Swamp Visions, Rionya, Fire Dancer
(`token-copy-linked-sources.test.ts`). Keys: linked-exile-token-copy,
token-copy-of-enchanted, token-copy-of-exiled-card, token-copy-count-amount,
copy-exception-static-ability. Still open on their cards: Osgir (an exile
cost's filter reading X), Tawnos (sacrifice and exile in one cost), Phantom
Steed ("an Illusion" only if the token is a creature, rule 205.3d), Prototype
Portal and Soul Foundry (`cost:x-equals-linked-exiled-mana-value`), Nahiri
(`effect:attack-requirement-player-only`).

### Removing counters in bulk — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:remove-all-counters`, features only.
**`remove-counter`'s `amount: "all"`** takes every counter of its kind off one
permanent, and with **`counter: "any"`** every kind — "remove all counters
from target permanent" (Vampire Hexmage), "from that creature" (Blitz Leech),
"from it" (Enchanted River's Grasp). **`remove-counter-all`** does the same,
or a number, to every permanent matching a filter, read before any counter
comes off: "then remove all fate counters from all permanents" (Oblivion
Stone), "remove all counters from all permanents" (Aether Snap), "remove two
loyalty counters from each planeswalker" (Pestilent Haze). A token stack loses
them together in one `counter-removed` carrying its `tokens`, which "the
number of counters removed this way" multiplies. Keys: remove-all-counters,
remove-counters-all. Still open beside it: "remove all [kind] counters from
this" as a **cost** (`cost:remove-all-counters` — Relic Amulet, Sage of
Hours, Catti-brie), and a modal spell's modes sharing one "this way" (Nexus
Mentality, with both modes chosen).

### Revealing cards from your hand as a cost — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:additional-cost-reveal`, features only.
A `BeholdSpec` with `fromHand` and no `exile` is now a **reveal from the
hand** (rule 701.20a): asked as a `choose-from-zone` with the `"reveal"`
destination, the cards revealed to every player and left in the hand. Three
shapes use it: an `additionalCost.options` branch for Lorwyn's "reveal a
Kithkin card from your hand or pay {3}"; the kicker **`keyword: "reveal"`**
for Dragons of Tarkir's "you may reveal a Dragon card from your hand", with
**`kicker.orControls`** — "or controlled a Dragon as you cast this spell" —
kicking a cast whose caster controls one once its costs are paid
(`GameObject.kickedByControl`, which a copy of the spell doesn't inherit: the
Draconic Roar ruling); and the activated cost **`AbilityCost.reveal`**, the
Martyrs' "Reveal X white cards from your hand", X announced and capped by the
matching cards. The client's reveal prompt names the count. Key:
additional-cost-reveal. Proving cards: Goldmeadow Stalwart, Silvergill
Adept, Draconic Roar, Orator of Ojutai, Scaleguard Sentinels, Martyr of
Sands, Martyr of Spores, Martyr of Ashes.

### Shuffling a hand, graveyard and permanents in — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:shuffle-library`'s top unit, features
only. `shuffle-graveyard-into-library` takes **`zones`** — hand, graveyard,
and the permanents a player owns (whoever controls them; a phased-out one
isn't there) — all in one move, then the shuffle (rule 701.24a); and
**`drawThatMany`**, "then draws that many cards": each player draws what
they put in, a token counting (The Great Aurora's ruling). Whirlpool Rider
and Whirlpool Drake moved to it from a forced look-and-choose of the whole
hand. Keys: shuffle-hand-and-graveyard-into-library,
shuffle-hand-draw-that-many, shuffle-hand-and-permanents-draw-that-many,
shuffle-permanents-owned-into-library. Worldpurge's "keeps up to seven" is
still open.

### A mode chosen more than once — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:modal-repeat-modes`, features only.
**`castModal.repeatModes`** is "You may choose the same mode more than
once" (rule 700.2d — the Confluences): the cast's `modes` may repeat an
index, sorted into printed order, each copy taking its own target slots
(`concatModeSpecs`). The offer carries `repeatModes`; the target-count bounds,
the bots' candidates (`modeChoicesBetween`, multisets), the heuristic and
random drivers (`fillModes`, drawn with replacement) and the client's picker
(a click adds a copy, "×N", Clear) all read it. "Choose one that hasn't been
chosen" was built already (`modal`'s `notChosenBefore`, Silent Hallcreeper):
Kimoyo Beads proves it. Keys: modal-repeat-modes, mode-not-chosen-this-turn.

### Block minimums, "all must block" and one-blocker requirements — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:combat-restriction-extensions`, its top
two units, features only. **`blockedByAtLeast: N`** generalises menace's
restriction (rule 702.111b) to "except by N or more creatures": the blockers
offer's `minBlockers`, and `combat/blocking.ts`'s plans, violations and
`obeyingLure` read each attacker's minimum (`leastBlockers`) instead of "two
with menace". **`blockedOnlyByAll`** is Tromokratis' "unless all creatures
defending player controls block it" (`whyCannotBlock` refuses all of them when
one can't; a partial block is the `blocked-by-all` violation). The `controls`
condition's **`sharingACreatureType`** (Graxiplon) and a plain `{ countOf }` in
a block filter's amount (Kraken of the Straits). On one blocker:
`restrict`'s **`cantBlockAttacker`** ("target creature can't block this
creature this turn") and the **`"must-block"`** restriction ("blocks each
combat if able"), joined to the offer's `blockRequirements`. Two keys were
built already: "except by creatures with haste this turn" (Resilient
Roadrunner's `notKeyword`) and "can't be blocked as long as defending player
…" (Bouncing Beebles' `of-player` condition — the scaffolder now reads its
counts, "untapped" and player counters). Keys: blockable-only-by-filter,
cant-be-blocked-except-by-n, cant-be-blocked-unless-all-block,
cant-be-blocked-unless-defender-controls-shared-type,
unblockable-defending-player-condition, target-cant-block-source-this-turn,
target-must-block-source-this-turn, must-block-each-combat.

### Distinct values and player-sided counts — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:amount-new-variants`, its top five
units, features only. **`aggregate: "distinct"`** on an `AggregateSpec`
counts the different mana values, powers, names (`distinctNameOf`), colour
pairs, permanent types or subtypes of a type among matching permanents —
or, with **`in: "graveyards"`**, graveyard cards — in every place an
aggregate goes: an amount, an `aggregate` condition, a cost reduction, and
now a CDA's `CountSpec`. An activated ability's `costReduction` takes a
`condition`. **`countInGraveyard`** reads from one player's side
(`forTarget`, `forPlayer`, as `countOf` does). **`CardFilter.isSource`**
makes "Equipment attached to it" countable (the static P/T count now passes
its source to the filter). **`countPlayers`' `where`** counts the players a
condition holds for, read as a `whoIf` is. Several keys turned out to be
covered already by `countOf`'s `forTarget` / `forPlayer` / `forEachPlayer`
and `cardsInHand: { target }` (built after their cards were triaged).
Keys: count-distinct-mana-values, count-distinct-powers, distinct-names-count,
count-subtypes-among, amount-color-pairs, permanent-types-in-graveyard,
cards-in-target-player-hand, count-in-target-player-graveyard,
per-player-count-in-graveyard, count-equipment-attached-to-source,
count-for-trigger-player, count-from-chosen-player,
count-of-damaged-players-permanents, count-of-target-players-permanents,
players-controlling-more-amount, opponents-controlling-count,
opponents-with-less-life-count, count-graveyards-with-n-cards,
count-defending-player-controls, per-opponent-count-damage. 13 proving
cards: Lunar Insight, Golden Ratio, Aven Heartstabber, Sewer Crocodile,
Awakened Amalgam, Corpse Augur, Roiling Terrain, Goblin Gaveleer, Kemba,
Kha Regent, Oreskos Explorer, Priest of the Blessed Graf, Terra Ravager,
Treacherous Terrain. Left open: distinct *mana costs* (Embodiment of
Agonies — the {X} ruling unchecked), counter kinds on one object, colours
among spells cast, players who have lost, a sum over several target
opponents (Call the Coppercoats), and a margin ("at least two more lands").

### A value chosen as it resolves — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-card-name`'s chosen-value
group, features only. **`choose-value`** chooses a colour, a card type (rule
205.2a), a kind of counter on a target or a matching permanent, or a number
as the effect resolves, and runs `then` with it: the word through the
`choose-creature-type` decision's short menu, substituted for `CHOSEN_COLOR`
/ `CHOSEN_CARD_TYPE` / `CHOSEN_COUNTER`; the number through the new
**`choose-number`** decision (the 31st kind — a stepper in the client), read
by `then` as `"x"`. The whole resolution is carried across the choice
(`ResolutionResume`), so a trigger's targets and trigger value survive it.
**`reveal-hand`** shows a hand, its cards counted as `{ thisWay: "revealed"
}`. A resolving word menu now offers its options to the v2 search
(`resolving` on the offer), and v1 aims a resolving colour at the opponents'
permanents. Keys: choose-color-on-resolution, choose-number-on-resolution,
choose-card-type, revealed-hand-cards-of-chosen-type, choose-counter-kind,
remove-counter-chosen-kind (already `remove-counters { counter: "any" }`).
7 proving cards: Persecute, Sudden Demise, Void, Blood Oath, Fertile
Imagination, Ichormoon Gauntlet, Medicine Runner. Left open: "the last
chosen color" kept on a permanent (Chromatic Armor), Liquid Fire's number
chosen as it's cast, and the freed cards' other needs (Zombie Boa's delayed
trigger, Skrelv's hexproof from a colour), which `author-freed` narrows.

### Damage prevented to and by a target — marked built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:prevent-combat-damage-to-and-by-target`,
features only: no engine code. Its keys were triaged before B250's
`damage-rule` and static `would-deal-damage` prevention landed, which already
run them — "to and dealt by that creature" is two `damage-rule`s (Ith, High
Arcanist), "the next time target creature would deal damage" is `amount:
"next-time"` with `gainLifePrevented`, Tajic's line is Crystal Barricade's
static. The scaffolder learned the lines it missed. Keys:
prevent-combat-damage-to-and-by-target, prevent-damage-by-target-this-turn,
prevent-damage-to-other-filter. 4 proving cards: Ebony Horse, Awe Strike,
Stonewise Fortifier, Tajic, Legion's Edge. Left open: Silhouette's "a spell or
ability that targets that creature" (`replacement:prevent-damage-from-targeting-sources`);
Energy Arc's and Soul Parry's several targets go to `author-freed` to narrow.

### Skipping steps — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:skip-untap-step`, features only,
engine-only. **`skipsSteps`** (a static: `who`, `steps`) skips a player's
untap, upkeep or draw steps while it applies; the one-shot **`skip-step`**
(`step`, `who`, `thisTurn`) skips their next one, or this turn's. A skipped
upkeep or draw step is passed in `Game.enterStep` as though it didn't exist
(500.11) — a `step-skipped` event and no `step-began`, so nothing "at the
beginning of" it — and a static's skip leaves a "next" one waiting
(614.10a). Keys: skip-untap-step, skip-draw-step, skip-step,
skip-next-draw-step. 7 proving cards: Stasis, Eon Hub, Gibbering Descent,
Damia, Sage of Stone, Fatigue, Molten Firebird, Elfhame Sanctuary. Left open:
Possessed Portal's "skips that draw" (`replacement:skip-every-draw`, a draw
replacement beside Notion Thief's), Fasting's optional skip, Sands of Time's
tap swap.

### Player-state conditions on another player — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:player-state-thresholds`, features
only, engine-only. **`of-player`** reads any `StaticCondition` with another
player as its "you" (`ConditionOptions.asPlayer`): `"that-player"` (the
trigger's or `for-each-player`'s player — `ConditionOptions.thatPlayer`, now
passed to an intervening-if as it triggers and as it resolves, and to a
resolution's fallback), `"active-player"`, `{ target }`, `{ controllerOfTarget
}`. **`player-compare`** compares your life, hand or count of a filter with
each opponent's, some opponent's or one player's (`more` / `fewer` /
`at-least`). `for-each-player` gained **`whoIf`** ("each player who …", fixed
before anyone acts). `life-total` bounds take `{ startingPlus }` (rule 103.4);
`controls` takes `anyController`; `monarch` `who: "nobody"`. Keys:
defending-player-poisoned, target-player-life-total, target-player-zone-empty,
that-player-hand-size, that-player-life-intervening-if,
more-cards-in-hand-than-each-opponent, opponent-more-cards-in-hand,
player-controls-most, life-above-starting, that-player-controls,
condition-count-all-permanents, target-/that-player-turn-stat, no-monarch. 11
proving cards: Lavaborn Muse, Septic Rats, Hidetsugu's Second Rite, Depopulate,
Sunset Revelry, Rivalry, Tectonic Hellion, Speaker of the Heavens, Righteous
Valkyrie, Planar Collapse, Harbor Serpent. The scaffolder's `playerStateIf`
drafts the intervening-ifs.

With it, `condition:defending-player-controls` (and Goblin Goon's
controls-more-creatures-than-defending-player, already `youControlMore`): a
`"defending-player"` `ConditionPlayer`, the player the source attacks (rule
802.2a), for a static's "as long as defending player …"; the attack-trigger
ifs are `"that-player"`, the attack restrictions `cantAttackUnlessDefender`. 3
proving cards: Bouncing Beebles, Hooded Horror, Aerial Surveyor. Left to a
narrow key: a non-attacking source's "defending player" (Kjeldoran Guard,
Arcum's Sleigh), Avatar of Might's "at least four more".

### Cards outside the game: the sideboard — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:outside-the-game`, features only, and a
sideboard to make it real (the user's call, over keeping "outside the game is
empty"). A deck brings a **sideboard** (rule 100.4; at most 15, 100.4a; none in
a Commander game, 903.5e): `DeckList.sideboard`, kept as objects with
`zone: "outside"` in `GameState.outside` (rule 400.11 — not a zone), seen by
their owner alone (`zones.sideboard`, `sideboardSize` in the view).
`search-library` gained **`outside: true | "only"`** (rule 701.23j: "you may
reveal a sorcery card you own from outside the game") and **`faceUpExile`**
("or choose a face-up Eldrazi card you own in exile"); the find is revealed
"from their sideboard". **`learn()`** is now rule 701.48a whole: the rummage,
or else a Lesson from the sideboard — the eleven learn cards written inline
moved to it. The scaffolder drafts the wish lines and a spell's closing
"Exile ~." (`exileOnResolve`). The wire's `WireDeck.sideboard`, the decklist
import's `Sideboard` section and `SB:` lines, the deck editor's Sideboard
section, a "sideboard N" link in the player panel, and the scenario builder's
`"sideboard"` zone carry it. Proving cards: Burning Wish, Living Wish, Coax from
the Blind Eternities, Mastermind's Acquisition, Legion Angel. Not covered:
playing or casting from outside the game (Wish, Spawnsire of Ulamog), a draw
replaced (Ring of Ma'rûf), an opponent choosing among the finds (Turtles
Forever, Extrapolate the Impossible), a shuffle into the library (Research),
the library/graveyard/outside choice of which zones to search (Invasion of
Arcavios), copies of named cards (Garth One-Eye, a misfile) — `author-freed`
narrows them.

### Another player's library — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:look-and-choose-target-opponent-library`,
features only. `look-and-choose` gained **`from: EffectPlayerRef`**: another
player's library (a target, "that player"), the effect's controller still
looking and choosing — the zone's owner and picker of Tasigur's shape — with
the chosen cards going to that player's zones; a **`"graveyard"`** destination
("put one of them into their graveyard"); and **`leftover: "top-any-order"`**
("the rest on top of their library in any order"), the chooser ordering them
through `beginLibraryOrder`'s top. Proving cards: Architects of Will, Draugr
Thought-Thief, Cruel Fate, Sealed Fate, Balshan Beguiler. Keys:
`effect:look-and-choose-target-opponent-library`,
`effect:look-and-choose-target-player`,
`effect:reveal-top-opponent-library-you-choose-to-graveyard`. Not covered:
fateseal (`effect:fateseal` — the `scry` decision over another library), "you
may have that player shuffle" (`effect:shuffle-library`), the target player
choosing (Ashnod's Cylix), face-down exile playable with any mana (Siphon
Insight), a look at a hand (Vendilion Clique) — `author-freed` narrows them.

### Filtered discards — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:discard-all-matching`, features only.
`discard` gained a **`filter`** (the engine's discard already took one for
costs; the effect never passed it): "discard a creature card", and with
`anyNumber` "discard any number of land cards"; a `may` over it isn't offered
without enough matching cards (`mayBeDone` reads `handSizeOf(player, filter)`).
`discard-hand` gained **`filter`** ("discards all [filter] cards", nothing
chosen), **`reveal`** ("reveals their hand and discards…", rule 701.20a) and a
`who` that takes a target slot or an `EffectPlayerRef` object. Proving cards:
Amnesia, Trapfinder's Trick, Shatter Assumptions, Hidden Horror, Pack
Guardian, Nantuko Cultivator, Mind Maggots. Keys: `effect:discard-all-matching`,
`effect:discard-any-number-filtered`, `effect:discard-filtered`. Not covered:
a colour chosen on resolution (Persecute, Crosis — `decision:choose-color-on-resolution`),
the sacrificed creature's colours (Mind Extraction), the trigger spell's mana
value (Infernal Kirin) — `author-freed` narrows them.

### The chosen creature, and choices by another player — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-permanent`, its first unit
(features only), on the existing `choose-permanents` decision — no new
decision kind. "As this enters, choose another creature you control" (rule
614.1c) is the card field `choosePermanentOnEnter { filter, optional?,
prompt, attach?, counter? }`, kept with its stint as
`GameObject.chosenPermanent` (rule 607.2d) and read back by the `"chosen"`
`EffectTargetRef` and a static's `{ scope: "chosen" }` while it's still that
object (rule 400.7), as the source last existed once it has left (608.2h);
`attach` enters an Equipment attached to it (rule 301.5e), `counter` puts a
counter on it. `choose-permanents` gained `chooser` (an `EffectPlayerRef`:
"that player", "defending player" — filter from their side, `then` theirs),
`min` as an amount (Tangle Wire's "for each fade counter"), and
`exceptTriggerObject`; `CardFilter.sharesPermanentTypeWith` (rule 110.4). The
search bot weighs each option of a forced pick; the view names the chosen
permanent on its chooser's tile (`chosen`). Proving cards: Tyrannical
Pitlord, Dauntless Bodyguard, Grifter's Blade, Phylactery Lich, Sunken Hope,
Tangle Wire, Crashing Boars, Saw, Cloudstone Curio. Keys:
`decision:choose-permanent`, `decision:as-enters-choose-permanent`,
`decision:choose-permanents-exclude-trigger-object`,
`decision:that-player-chooses-permanent`. Not covered: the family's other
units — one per category (Tragic Arrogance, Global Ruin), a kept subset
(Covetous Elegy), a choice among the spell's targets (Wicked Slumber), one
per opponent (Ultimate Magic: Meteor) — and "gain its activated abilities"
(Scheming Fence); `author-freed` narrows the rest.

Two need-keys Phase 2 families, features only. **`amount:count-for-chosen-player`**:

### What a manifest or a create step put onto the battlefield — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:this-way-results`' top group:
`effect:manifested-this-way` and `effect:put-counters-of-created`.
`manifest-dread`'s `count` is an amount ("manifest dread X times"), "those
creatures" / "that creature" / "it" after a manifest is `add-counter-all` over
`{ thisWay: "put-onto-battlefield" }` (a manifested card is put onto the
battlefield, rule 701.40a), and `put-counters-of`'s `target: "created"` is the

### A card in exile, and the card exiled with this — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:linked-exiled-card`'s top group:
`effect:linked-exiled-card`, `effect:linked-face-down-exile-to-battlefield`,

target spec `card-in-exile` (`whose` its owner, `faceUp`, `linked` — rule
607.2a, the source's stint, exiled with it or until it leaves — and a filter a
face-down card never matches, 406.3a); the filter `hasFlashback`;
`allow-cast-linked-exile`'s `spendAs` (rule 609.4b). The rest was already
there: `turn-exiled-face-up` then `return-exiled-by-source { linked:

a creature card, put it onto the battlefield", and a `trigger-object` condition
over `sharesCardTypeWith: "exiled-with-source"` is "if it shares a card type
with the exiled card". `linked-exiled-card.test.ts`. Proving cards: Pull from
Eternity, Gelatinous Cube, Runic Repetition, Clone Shell, Cemetery Protector,
King Narfi's Betrayal. Still blocked under these keys: Summoner's Egg (a card
exiled from hand face down, `effect:exile-from-hand-face-down-linked`),

of the Lost; the family's one-card units (Diabolic Servitude, Dark Impostor,

### A secret choice — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 key `decision:secret-choice` (no new decision kind). A
player chosen secretly as a permanent enters (`secretChoiceOnEnter`) or as an
ability resolves (`choose-opponent { remember, secret }`), or a word from a short
list (`choose-creature-type { options, remember, secret }`), kept as
`GameObject.secretChoice`: `viewFor` leaves the answer out of every view but the
chooser's and the log has only `secret-choice-made`, until the cost `revealChosen`
(payable by the chooser alone) shows it with `secret-choice-revealed`. With it:
`onlyOnce` for a plain "Activate only once", which moves seven cards off `exhaust`;
`controlledBy: "chosen-player"`; `stackObjectTargets`, a filter's `targets` that
reads an ability too; a player's protection from a player
(`player-effect { protectionFromPlayers }`, rule 702.16k) and a grant's
`fromChosenPlayer`. Key also marked: `effect:protection-from-player-for-permanent`.
`secret-choice.test.ts` (engine and server, whose test reads real frames). Proving
cards: Stalking Leonin, Guardian Archon, Emissary of Grudges, A Killer Among Us,
Goblin Game. Two of the key's cards are still blocked: The Toymaker's Trap (a
number not chosen before, then an opponent's guess) and Stronghold Gambit (each
player secretly picks a card in hand, then the lowest creature enters).

### The chosen player — built 2026-10-29

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-player` (features only). A

`static:attacking-chosen-player-anthem`, `trigger:chosen-player-upkeep`. Not
covered: two chosen players (Bitter Feud, Sower of Discord), a secret choice,

over the chosen player's permanents (`amount:count-for-chosen-player`, the
