Plaguecrafter, Super Intelligence, Citadel Siege, Azog Moria's Ruin, Silumgar the
Drifting Death. Partly covered: Fall of the Impostor still needs an exile chosen, not
targeted, among a target opponent's creatures; Curse of Thirst and Curse of Surveillance
a count of Curses attached; Overencumbered its `effect:pay-or-cant-attack-you`.
`author-freed` narrows those.

### An activated ability's own reduction, under a condition — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:ability-cost-modification`, its top two units.
`costReduction.condition` had landed with Sewer Crocodile; new here are its
`reduceColored` (rule 118.7, through `reduceManaCost`) and the scaffolder reading "This
ability costs {N} less to activate if …", on an equip line too. `anyZone` and
`hostOfSource` already covered `cost:ability-cost-modification-all-zones` and
`static:ability-cost-increase-attached`. No new decision. Proving cards: Flying Drone,
Kami of Jealous Thirst, Esquire of the King, Crown of Gondor, Razorlash Transmogrant,
Hylda's Crown of Winter, Suppression Field, Oppressive Rays. Thaumaton Torpedo still
waits on `stat:attacked-with-filter-this-turn`. Left in the family: `cost:first-equip-each-turn-free`
and `cost:power-up-alternative-cost` need a {0} that replaces a whole cost, and an equip
can cost life, not mana, so the mana path alone wouldn't be faithful.

**Cycling's cost as a variant (2026-11-01, `cost:cycling-alt-cost`,
`cost:cycling-x`).** `CyclingAbility`'s `also` (any non-mana cost, with
`costText`) and an `{X}` in `cost`, both activated as an `activate-ability` on
the card in hand, `this-cycled` reading the X as `{ triggerValue: true }`; the
`alternativeCyclingCost` static, `firstEachTurn` from `cardsCycledThisTurn`.
Proving cards: Street Wraith, Edge of Autumn, Gavi, Nest Warden, New Perspectives,
Shark Typhoon, Webstrike Elite, Rampaging War Mammoth. Freed and unauthored:
Valor's Flagship.

**Free casts with a limit (2026-11-01, `cost:free-cast-from-hand-static`,
`cost:free-cast-once-each-turn`).** `alternativeCostForSpells` gained `fromZones`
(exile, the library's top, on `via: "library-top"` too) and `anyPlayer`; its
`filter` is now judged as the face cast and against the permanent, so an `{ amount
}` operand reads its counters or creatures; a grant's plain-mana offer now has an
`altCostText`. Proving cards: Omniscience, Omnipresence, Aluren, As Foretold,
Vision, Spectral Synthezoid, Zaffai and the Tempests, One with the Multiverse,
Tlincalli Hunter. Not covered: Jacob Hauken, Inspector, which also needs
`static:play-from-exile-once-each-turn` and `effect:exile-top-face-down-linked`.

### Ending the turn and the combat phase — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:end-the-combat-phase`, keys `effect:end-the-turn`
and `effect:end-the-combat-phase` (rule 724). The effects `end-the-turn` and
`end-the-combat-phase`: `Game.beginEndingTurnOrCombat` empties the waiting triggers and
exiles the stack as the effect happens, the resolving object last as it leaves
(`GameState.endingPhase` tells `leaveStackAfterResolving` so); `prepareForPriority` then
checks state-based actions, any answer they ask included, and `finishEndingTurnOrCombat`
skips to the cleanup step, or to the next phase through `endStep` as if end of combat had
ended. The turn's owed phases go with it; the combat phase's don't. No new decision (the
log gets `phase-ended-by-effect`). Proving cards: Time Stop, Ultima, Glorious End, Obeka
Brute Chronologist, Mandate of Peace. The scaffolder reads the lines, "if it's your turn,
end the turn" and "the player whose turn it is may end the turn" with them.

### Limits on how many creatures attack or block — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:combat-restriction-extensions`, its group
`static:attack-limit` + `static:max-attackers-blockers`. `StaticAbility.attackLimit
{ most, covers: "all" | "you" | "self" }` and `blockLimit { most }`, read by
`combat/limits.ts` into the offers (`company.caps`, `blockMost`); the effect
`limit-blockers { who, most }` for one combat. A declaration past one is refused, the
drivers cut theirs to fit, and requirements obey them first (rules 508.1d, 509.1c: a
matching under per-defender caps, and N blockers each obeying one is all there can be).
Menace under a limit of one can't be blocked. Counted across defending players, who are
asked in turn. No new decision. Proving cards: Crawlspace, Silent Arbiter, Caverns of
Despair, Dueling Grounds, Tomik Orzhov Lawmage, Mirri Weatherlight Duelist. `static:attack-limit`
was triaged also on cards it doesn't cover: "can't attack alone" (Raging Kronch, Militia
Rallier, Bonded Horncrest, Trusty Companion) is `combat/company.ts`'s, built already;
Scarred Puma's "unless a black or green creature also attacks" and Sightless Brawler's
granted restriction need `author-freed` to narrow; Mystic Barrier waits on
`static:attack-direction-restriction`.

**Choices kept, the rest destroyed (2026-11-01, `effect:destroy-all-except-chosen`,
`effect:each-player-chooses-destroy-rest`).** The `vote` with `choose` already asked
the question; `thisWay: "chosen"` reads its answers, so "destroy the rest" is
`notThisWay: "chosen"` on any mass effect's filter and "that hasn't been chosen" the
same clause on the next chooser's. `for.permanent` gained `count`, `upTo` (for
`upToOne`) and `keep`; `startingWith` gained `"you-then-active-player"`. Proving
cards: Divine Reckoning, Duneblast, Mount Doom, Fall of the First Civilization,
Fortunate Few, Druid of Purification. Freed and unauthored: Urza's Sylex, Selfless
Glyphweaver. The clause also reaches Consuming Tide, and Last One Standing given a
random choice (`effect:random-permanent`).

**Exchanges with a life total (2026-11-01, `effect:exchange-life-power`,
`effect:exchange-life-totals`).** `exchange-life-totals` (rule 701.12c, all or nothing
under rules 119.7–8) and `exchange-life-stat`, which was `exchange-life-toughness`,
now taking `stat: "power"`. The `{ lifeDifference }` amount and `player-compare`'s
`within`. Proving cards: Evra, Halcyon Witness, Mirror Universe, Soul Conduit, Mister
Negative, Psychic Transfer, Profane Transfusion. Freed and unauthored: Axis of
Mortality, Magus of the Mirror.

### Token-creation replacements as one event — built 2026-11-01

`replacement:token-creation-add-extra`, `replacement:artifact-tokens-instead`,
`replacement:token-creation-one-of-each`, `replacement:token-creation-substitute-replacement`
(its unconditional half). `would-create-token` gains `plusOneToken`, `tokensInstead`,
`oneOfEach` and `ofTokens`, and every token creation now plans its replacements through
`token-replacements.ts`: the event changes as each applies (rule 616.1), each has one
opportunity (rule 614.5), and the order is the one making the most, doublers last. One
instruction's several kinds of token, or copies of several permanents, are one event
(`withTokenEvent`). A token copy in a stack counts as its own replacement, doublers too.
No new decision. Proving cards: Xorn, Stridehangar Automaton, Tippy-Toe, Queen Allenal,
Draconic Visitor, Fisher's Talent, Academy Manufactor. Left in the family:
`replacement:token-creation-replace-with-copies` (Esix, Mirrormind Crown, Moonlit Meditation)
and Jinnie Fay, all "you may instead", which needs a choice asked in the middle of creating a
token, and the first three a first-time-each-turn record too.

### What a permanent has dealt damage to — built 2026-11-01

`stat:filter-dealt-damage-this-turn`, `stat:filter-dealt-damage-to-you-this-turn`,
`filter:dealt-combat-damage-to-you-this-turn`, `condition:source-hasnt-dealt-damage`,
`condition:source-hasnt-dealt-combat-damage`, `trigger:creature-damaged-by-source-dies`
(already Sengir Vampire's shape; marked with a proving card). `GameObject.dealtDamage`, kept
by `dealDamage` for each permanent, and read by the filter clause `dealtDamageThisTurn`
(`true`, or `{ to: "you", combat? }`) and the condition `self-hasnt-dealt-damage`. No new
decision. Proving cards: Reciprocate, Retaliate, Spear of Heliod, Restore the Peace, Red
Guardian, Karakyk Guardian, Ruric Thar, Soul Collector, Witch-king of Angmar. Left: Inferno
Trap counts creatures that dealt you damage, those gone included, which this record can't;
Bushi Tenderfoot, Scythe of the Wretched and Repentant Vampire need more of their own; the
family's one-card stats and the player targets (Wicked Akuba, Diseased Vermin, Hope of
Ghirapur) stay open.

### Paying or bidding an amount, player by player — built 2026-11-01

`mechanic:join-forces`, `effect:life-bidding`. `players-pay-any-amount` (mana as a `choose-modes`
with an `{X}`, life as a `choose-number`; the total as `then`'s `"x"`, what each paid as `each`'s;
`untilNoOnePays`) and `bid-life` (an opening bid, then a yes or no and how much round the table
until it comes back to the high bidder), on no new decision: Minds Aglow, Alliance of Arms,
Mana-Charged Dragon, Liege of the Hollows, Plague of Vermin, Illicit Auction, Pain's Reward, Mages'
Contest. Left: Collective Voyage's lands would enter player by player on `for-each-player`, where
its text has them entering together, and a `who` on the effect would reach Necrodominance's and
Errant Minion's one payer (`effect:pay-any-amount-of-mana`, `effect:pay-any-amount-of-life`).

### A discard an opponent caused — built 2026-11-01

`replacement:discarded-by-opponent-to-battlefield`, `trigger:discarded-by-opponent-effect`. The card
field `discardedByOpponentEnters { withCounters? }`; `discards`' `byOpponent`, beside
`this-discarded`'s; `return-to-hand`'s `from: "where-discarded"`: Obstinate Baloth, Dodecapod, Quagnoth,
Spiritual Focus. Left: Pure Intentions' "this turn" trigger returning those cards, Mangara's Blessing's
and Sand Golem's return at the next end step (delayed, from the graveyard, if it never left), Nephalia
Academy's replacement from a land. Rule 616.1's choice against a graveyard replacement (Leyline of the
Void) isn't asked: the battlefield is taken, as a player would all but always choose.

### Acting from the hand before the game: the opening hand and "any time you could mulligan" — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:leyline-opening-hand`, its group with
`mechanic:mulligan-hand-ability`. Rule 103.6's two actions were already there
(`beginsOnBattlefield`, `openingHandReveal`) and the key needed only marking and
proving; new is rule 103.5b: `mulliganHandAbility: "exile-hand-draw-that-many"`, a
third action on the `mulligan` decision, not a mulligan (`taken` stays, nothing
shuffled, the holder declares again). Also rule 103.5's floor, closing a soft-lock
past seven mulligans: `mayTakeMulligan`, the offer's `mustKeep`, the bottoming owed
capped at the hand. The scaffolder reads Serum Powder's line and both word orders of
a reveal whose effect it reads whole. The builder can now start at the mulligans
(`ScenarioSpec.openingHands`). Keys: mechanic:leyline-opening-hand,
mechanic:mulligan-hand-ability. Proving cards: Serum Powder, Providence, Leyline Axe,
Devourer of Destiny. Left: the freed Leylines' other lines are `author-freed`'s to
narrow (Leyline of Combustion's trigger, Guildpact's all-colors, Transformation's
types); Gemstone Caverns has its own key, `mechanic:opening-hand-not-starting-player`;
Chancellor of the Annex's reveal fires on a first spell, not at a step.

### Changes of control as trigger events — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:lose-control-trigger`, features only. `control-changed` names its
`previousController`. Three triggers: **`loses-control`** ("when you lose control of this": another
player gains it or it leaves the battlefield, Khârn's ruling), **`gains-control`** ("from another
player") and **`control-taken`** ("an opponent gains control of a permanent from you"). The first and
last look back (rule 603.10d): the ability is its former controller's, and `withControlBatch` keeps
that for permanents changing hands at once. `delayed-trigger`'s **`at: { losesControl, thisTurn?,
onlyIf? }`** is the delayed form. A "prevent that damage and …" follow-up that asks waits behind one
already asking. The scaffolder reads the three triggers and "when you lose control of the creature,
tap it / unattach it". Keys: lose-control-trigger, gains-control-of-this. Proving cards: Khârn the
Betrayer, Ray of Command, Magus of the Unseen, Ogre Geargrabber, Stolen Uniform, Seraph, Zidane,
Tantalus Thief, Risky Move. Freed but not authored: Duplicity (and Gustha's Scepter) need their
face-down exile. Coffin Queen needs "becomes untapped or you lose control". Krovikan Vampire needs its
end-step intervening "if" over creatures that died.

### References to a permanent's attachments and to the source's host — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-attachments`, features only. `CardFilter.attachments`
(`filter?`, `atLeast?`, `otherThanSource?`) asks what is attached to a permanent, live, whoever
controls it. `sharesColorWithHost` and `sharesCreatureTypeWithHost` are static reads of the source's
host, which shares with itself only if it has a color or type (Konda's Banner's ruling). The
`ConditionPlayer` `"host-controller"` is "its controller", which a static can ask; `controls`'
`excludeHost` is "another creature" beside the host; `sacrifice`'s `exceptHost` is "other than
enchanted creature" for an edict, whose filter is read after the source context has gone. A `source`
condition's filter now sees its source. `hostOfSource: false` already covered a target's "other than
enchanted creature". The scaffolder reads the two target phrases, the Banner's two lines and Face of
Divinity's. Keys: attached-host-condition, not-attached-to-source, shares-color-with-attached,
shares-creature-type-with-equipped, enchanted-by-another-aura, auras-attached-count, equipped-by-filter,
equipment-attached-count-condition, named-equipment-attached-to-your-creature. Proving cards: Konda's
Banner, So Tiny, Face of Divinity, Favorable Destiny, Champion's Helm, Due Diligence, Kjeldoran Pride,
Sporogenic Infection, Daybreak Coronet, Bride's Gown, Groom's Finery. Freed but not authored: Combat
Research, Ghost Lantern. Left for `author-freed` to narrow: Crown of Convergence needs a library-top
condition, a live color read of that card and "put the top card on the bottom"; Kitsune Mystic its
attach on resolution; Kassandra her search of the hand; Brass Knuckles its cast-trigger copy.
### The need-key recheck: stale notes, and five small general reads — built 2026-11-02

328 open keys' notes said the vocabulary lacks something. 33 of them ran with no engine change, each on a real
card (`need-key-recheck.test.ts`): a note gone stale, or a composition nobody tried. The compositions worth
knowing: `isSource: false` in a filter is "another", in a trigger, a choice or a static; `as-player` reads a
"you" amount or scope as someone else's; `for-each-permanent`'s `controlledByTarget` is whose side its filter
reads from, so the filter still says `controlledBy: "you"`; of a `whileCondition`, `of-player` with
`"defending-player"` reads the attacked player.

Seven general reads, each smaller than a key: energy as a held player counter (`HeldPlayerCounter`); `hasAbilities`
inside the layer fold, in a last pass; a combat restriction scoped by power or toughness, applied by
`restrictionsOf` after the fold; a `shares…TypeWith` clause outside an effect, answered from what the filter's
context can name; no maximum hand size for each player (`noMaxHandSize: "each-player"`), from a one-shot
(`player-effect`'s `noMaxHandSize`, `duration: "rest-of-game"`) and from a granted static; `choose-permanents`'s
`random`; `of-player`'s `"an-opponent"`. Keys: the 44 this pass added to `built`, listed in COMPLETED.md.
Proving cards: Hoarding Dragon, Worldfire, Clone Legion and the rest of `need-key-recheck.test.ts`.

### A search that adds the hand and the graveyard — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:search-hand-graveyard-library`. `search-library`'s
`zones` was library, graveyard, both, or the extract family's three together; it now takes
every set of the three (`SearchZones`, read through `SEARCHED_ZONES`), and the
`searchZonesAndOr` helper writes "your graveyard, hand, and/or library" as a `modal` over all
seven as it resolves, the Finale of Devastation shape. No new decision. The exile half
(`-exile`) was already the extract family's: Quash and Crumble to Dust needed only authoring.
`effect:search-library-and-graveyard-exile-rest` is `exileRest` and `inAnyOrder` (Doomsday).
Proving cards: Boonweaver Giant, Dark Supplicant, Mishra, Artificer Prodigy, Quash, Crumble to
Dust, The Stone Brain, Doomsday. Left: Kassandra still waits on `condition:equipped-by-filter`
and Kotose on `zone:choose-one-of-exiled`; Agency Outfitter's two names are a `together:
oneEach` the scaffolder doesn't draft.

### "Up to" in a public zone, and the hand and/or library — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-from-zone-extensions`, its two search keys.
`effect:search-up-to-public-zone` is `search-library`'s `upTo`: a graveyard searched alone must
yield a match for "a card" (701.23b frees only a hidden zone), but "up to five" may find none.
`decision:search-hand-and-library` was already `zones` and `searchZonesAndOr` from the family
above, triaged before they landed; it needed its cards. No new decision: both are the
`choose-from-zone` a search raises, whose popup labels each card with its zone, checked live at
four players. Proving cards: The Five Doctors, Gate to the Afterlife, Last Light of Durin's Day,
Ancient Vendetta.

### A target's namesakes off the battlefield, and tokens only — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-card-property-clauses`, its keys
`condition:same-name-as-target-off-battlefield` and `-tokens-only`. The first needed no
binding: `bindDynamicCompares` has bound `sameNameAsTarget` in every effect since the extract
family, whatever zone it reads (the key's "battlefield only" was stale), so `return-from-graveyard`,
`search-library` and `exile-graveyard` already took it. New are `sameNameTokensOnly` (the
namesakes must be tokens, the target needn't be: Legions to Ashes) and `exile-graveyard`'s
`fromHand: "also" | "only"`, riding along from `effect:exile-from-hand-by-filter` for Legion's
End's "hand and graveyard". Proving cards: Echoing Return, Rat King, Verminister, Moratorium Stone,
Secret Salvage, Mask of the Mimic, Legion's End, Legions to Ashes, Hour of Glory, Thought
Distortion. Left: March of Burgeoning Life waits on its exile-from-hand cost reduction and
Tibalt's Trickery on `effect:cast-now-other-player`. `effect:exile-from-hand-by-filter` is marked
too (Thought Distortion, Hour of Glory, Identity Crisis): Azor's Gateway's distinct mana values and
Ice Cauldron's noted mana are still their own and will need narrowing. A countered copy of a spell
now keeps its last-known record, so Quash and Counterbore read its name and controller (Quash's ruling).
### A cost exiling a count of graveyard cards — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:choose-cards-as-cost`, features only.
`additionalCost.exileFromGraveyard`'s **`count: "x"`** is X announced as it's cast (rule 107.3a),
capped at the matching cards there, and the same X as a `{X}` in the mana cost; **`optional`** always
asks, none an answer; **`linked`** links what's exiled to the permanent the spell becomes (rule
607.2a). The `AmountRef` **`"exiled-as-cost"`** reads the one card exiled, a copy reading the
original's (rule 707.10, `SpellSnapshot.exiledCost`); `{ exiledWithSourcePower }` and the
`exiled-with-source` condition's **`now`** and `filter` read a linked one. An activated ability's
**`count: "opponents"`** is read as it's activated. The scaffolder reads "exile X … cards" and Corpse
Explosion's damage line. Keys: additional-cost-exile-from-graveyard, exile-x-cards-cost,
exile-x-from-graveyard-additional, condition:exiled-with-source-filter. Proving cards: Harvest Pyre,
Chill Haunting, Skeletal Scrying, Corpse Explosion, Draconic Intervention, Redemptor Dreadnought,
Araumi of the Dead Tide. Left: Ludevic, Necrogenius needs its other keys; a copy's other cost refs
(`sacrificed`, `discarded`, `revealed`) still aren't carried to a copy.

### Morph and disguise costs beyond fixed mana — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:face-down`, features only. `MorphCost.also.reveal` reveals
a card from the hand through `beginBehold`; an `{X}` in `morph.cost` is announced on the
`turn-face-up` action (`xValue`, the offer's `xCost`, the client's X banner) and read by a "turned
face up" trigger as `{ triggerValue: true }` (rules 702.37f, 702.168e); `morph.costReduction` takes
a live count off generic mana; the static's `morphCostIncrease` adds to morph and megamorph costs
(702.37b says a megamorph cost is one), never disguise. The offer shows the cost as it stands now
(702.37e). The scaffolder reads "Morph—Reveal a … card in your hand" and "All morph costs cost {N}
more". Keys: morph-reveal-card, disguise-x-cost, disguise-cost-reduction, morph-cost-increase.
Proving cards: Watcher of the Roost, Ruthless Ripper, Bane of the Living, Warbreak Trumpeter, Fugitive
Codebreaker, Exiled Doomsayer. Left: Aurelia's Vindicator's "up to X other target" over the
battlefield and graveyards needs a trigger's target count reading the X, for `author-freed` to narrow.
### A loop over each kind of counter on one thing — built 2026-11-02

The family `effect:double-counters-each-kind`. `double-counters` already did the doubling (Deepglow Skate,
Ferrafor), so its key only needed marking and a template. New: `another-counter-of-each-kind { target,
orRemove? }`: proliferate's step on one permanent or player (it shares `Game.anotherCounterOfEachKind` with
the `proliferate` answer, so energy and a Doubling Season behave the same), and with `orRemove` one
`choose-modes` a kind. Keys: effect:double-counters-each-kind, effect:one-more-of-each-counter-kind,
effect:add-or-remove-each-counter-kind. Proving cards: Vorel of the Hull Clade, Powerful Broker, Maulfist
Revolutionary, Quarry Hauler, Dramatist's Puppet. Left: Arna Kennerüd's "for each nontoken permanent attached
to it, create a token that's a copy of that permanent attached to that creature" and Ultimate Spider-Man's
"each kind of counter on each Spider and legendary creature you control" (`double-counters-all` takes one
kind) are not reached; `author-freed` should narrow them.

### Free casts under a limit — built 2026-11-02

The family `decision:free-cast-choices`. `cast-now`'s `repeat` already offered one spell after another, so
no decision kind is new: `upTo` and `totalManaValue` are limits read once, carried in `CastNowProgress`, with
a running total narrowing `spell`'s `manaValue` and both on the offer (`castsLeft`, `manaValueLeft`, shown in
its prompt). Also `from: "face-up-exile-opponents-own"` and `EnterInstead`'s `exileUnless`. Keys:
decision:cast-up-to-n-free, decision:cast-now-total-mana-value, decision:cast-copies-total-mana-value. Proving
cards: Collected Conjuring, Doom Reigns Supreme, Ashiok, Nightmare Muse, Primeval Spawn, Uldaros Theorix. Left:
`decision:cast-one-per-card-type-from-exile` (Aminatou's Augury) is an until-end-of-turn permission, not a cast
as it resolves, and how a card of two types uses the permissions needs its rulings, which the snapshot doesn't
hold. Plargg and Nassari needs `new:opponent-chooses-exiled`, Wand of Wonder the die's rows around an
exile-until per opponent, Baron Helmut Zemo and Rod of Absorption their other keys.

### A creature the triggering attacker's defending player controls — found built 2026-11-07

Key `effect:defending-player-of-attached-attacker` (family `effect:player-scope-extensions`): no engine
code. On an attack trigger whose source isn't the attacker, "target creature defending player controls" is
`{ kind: "permanent", whose: "trigger-player", filter: { type: "creature" } }` — Thunder Lasso's shape,
which the triage predated. The scaffolder now reads "Whenever equipped creature attacks", "Whenever a
creature you control with a +1/+1 counter on it attacks" and the tap line on a non-self attack trigger.
Proving cards: Captain America's Shield, Elite Scaleguard (`defending-player-of-attacker.test.ts`). Yare
(an instant's "target creature defending player controls", with no attack trigger to name one) still
waits: in a multiplayer combat there may be several defending players.

### A permanent chosen on resolution kept for a leaves trigger, a player chosen as it transforms, and a player losing the game — built 2026-11-07

Keys `decision:remember-chosen-permanent-on-resolution`, `trigger:chosen-permanent-leaves`,
`trigger:chosen-player-loses-game` (family `decision:choose-permanent`). No new decision kind:
`choose-permanents { remember }` keeps the one chosen as the source's `chosenPermanent` and announces it
(`cards-chosen-from-zone`, so `thisWay: "chosen"` spares it later in the resolution); `TriggerWho`'s
`"chosen"` matches it, in the stint it was chosen in (rule 400.7); the card field
`choosePlayerOnTransform` (rule 712.20) is asked as the `transform` effect resolves (a `choose-modes` of
the transforming permanent's controller, only among two or more) and lands on `chosenPlayer`; the
`player-loses-game` trigger (rule 104.3, conceding included) matches `player-lost` before the player leaves.
Proving card: Zenos yae Galvus // Shinryu, Transcendent Rival (`chosen-permanent-leaves.test.ts`).

### An additional end step — built 2026-11-07

Key `effect:additional-end-step`. The `additional-end-step` effect (rule 500.9) owes another end step straight
after the one under way (`TurnState.extraEndSteps`, read by `Game.endStep`), with no cleanup between;
`TurnState.endSteps` counts the turn's end steps for `turn-structure`'s `endStep` ("if it's the first end step
of the turn"), and a delayed "next end step" made during one (`DelayedTrigger.createdInEndStep`) fires in a
later one that turn. Proving card: Y'shtola Rhul.

### A discarded card exiled with the source, then one taken back by type — found built 2026-11-07

Key `effect:linked-exile-from-discard`: no engine code. A `may` over `exile` with `target: "trigger-object"`,
`linked: true` on a per-card `discards` trigger (Bag of Holding's shape), and a `look-and-choose` over
`zone: "exiled-with-source"` to the graveyard whose `then` branches on target 0's type. Proving card:
Currency Converter. Toluz, Clever Conductor still waits on `trigger:discards-batch-objects`.

### An opponent's pick left out of the free casts — built 2026-11-07

Key `effect:opponent-chooses-exiled-this-way-excluded` (family `effect:choices-by-other-players`). No new
decision: a `look-and-choose` over `zone: "exiled-this-way"` with `chooser: "an-opponent"` and
`destination: "stay"` records the pick (the `"chosen"` this-way kind), and the next step's `cast-now`
takes `exceptChosen: true`, passing those cards over. Proving card: Plargg and Nassari.

### Each opponent's chosen creature exiled with the source, one returned later — found built 2026-11-07

Key `effect:linked-exile-return`: no engine code. A `vote` with `choose: true`, `who: "each-opponent"` over
their own creatures, then `for-each-voted` (`simultaneous`) `exile` with `linked: true`; the return is a
`look-and-choose` over `zone: "exiled-with-source"` to the battlefield with `withCounters`, which still finds
the linked cards after a `sacrifice-source` earlier in the same resolution (the stint the ability came from,
rule 607.2a). "If a player controls no creatures" is `of-player` `"a-player"`. Proving card: Sothera, the
Supervoid.

### Returned with no abilities, lost before it enters — built 2026-11-07

Key `bug:return-without-abilities-as-it-enters`. `EnterTypes.loseAbilities` ("…and has no abilities") puts a
layer-6 loss on the entry modifier, and `GameObject.enterChoice.withoutAbilities` marks the card before its
"as this enters" choices are asked (put-onto-battlefield, look-and-choose, a chosen card from a zone, held
entries), which `entersWithoutAbilities` reads as it reads Humility's: no Clone copy, no "enters tapped", no
counters of its own, and no enters trigger (rule 614.12, the ruling). Proving card: Xu-Ifit, Osteoharmonist.

### Colorless hybrid mana, and copying everything you control on the stack — built 2026-11-07

Keys `cost:colorless-hybrid-mana` and `keyword:devoid`. `parseManaCost` reads `{C/W}`-style symbols as a
hybrid pip of `{ kind: "colorless" }` and its colour (rule 107.4e: paid with one colorless mana or one of its
colour, a symbol of that colour, mana value 1 — 202.3f), which payment, cost reductions and colour identity
already handled through the widened-pip path. Devoid needed nothing: the pool writes it as `colors: []` with
the keyword line in `text` (702.114a). The new `copy-all` effect is "copy all spells you control, then copy
all other activated and triggered abilities you control": each copied once, the resolving ability left out
(`Game.resolvingAbilities`, or a parked one's `leaveStack`), the controller picking which goes next among two
or more by `choose-modes`. Proving card: Ulalek, Fused Atrocity. Benthic Anomaly and Corrupted Crossroads,
on `keyword:devoid` alone, go to `author-freed`.

### What restricted mana may pay for, and one type of mana spent as a colour — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:mana-spending-rules`, features only. `ManaSpendOnly.purposes` /
`ManaRestriction.purposes` name what a filter can't (`ManaSpendPurpose`): the special actions
turn-face-up and unlock-door, a disturb cost, equip and power-up abilities, a Class's level bar, and
any cost with `{X}` in it, read from the total cost being paid (Rosheen Meanderer's ruling: an
alternative cost and a kicker count, and a suspend cost and one asked for as something resolves do
too). `ManaPurpose` gained `{ kind: "special" }`, and a cast's `via` and `xCost`; every X sizing now
passes its purpose, so restricted mana counts toward it. `SpendUnitAs` is one type of mana spent as
one colour or any (rule 609.4b): `pipsAsSpendable` widens each covered pip to a hybrid one, its own
colour first, for `resolveHybridCost` to settle. The scaffolder reads the lines as a list. Keys:
spend-only-disturb-cost, spend-only-equip-abilities, spend-only-power-up, spend-only-turn-face-up,
spend-only-unlock-door, mana-spend-only-x-costs, mana-restriction-class-level,
spend-colorless-as-any-color, spend-color-as-another-color. Proving cards: Overgrown Zealot, Creeping
Peeper, Unblinking Observer, Freya Crescent, Quinjet Technician, Rosheen Meanderer, Elementalist's
Palette, Sunglasses of Urza, Abstruse Appropriation. Left: "Spend only black mana on X" (Drain Life,
Atalya) needs its own narrow key; North Star's one-spell permission and Grell Philosopher stay on
spend-mana-as-any-color.

### Counts and total power on an attack — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:combat-trigger-extensions`, its unit of
attack counts, features only. `attack-with` takes `exactly`, `totalPower: {
atLeast, firstTimeEachTurn? }` (the declaration's power as declared, off
`TurnHistory.attackedPower`, whose `earlierMost` remembers the turn's earlier
declarations) and `includingThis`, and its `atLeast` defaults to 1;
`attacks-player` takes `atLeast`, each player attacked judged alone. "That many"
ran already, as `{ triggerValue: true }` on `attacks-batch`. Keys:
attack-with-exactly, attack-with-total-power, attacks-player-at-least,
attack-with-count-amount. Proving cards: Alluring Suitor, Desert Were-Worm, Horn
of the Mark, Landroval, Horizon Witness, Grand Warlord Radha, The Earth King.

### Attacking one of your opponents, and not attacking you — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:attacking-an-opponent-filter`: its key,
`trigger:attacks-your-opponent` and `trigger:player-attacks-not-you`. The filter
was already there (`CardFilter.attackingOpponent`, from Blast-Furnace Hellkite):
"whenever a creature … attacks one of your opponents" is an `attacks` trigger
with `who: "any"` and that clause (the player, not a planeswalker or battle of
theirs, rule 508.3a), which the scaffolder now reads with "with a [+1/+1]
counter on it" and "enchanted". New: `attack-with`'s `notAttackingYou` —
"whenever a player attacks, if they aren't attacking you" (Mirkwood Trapper; a
planeswalker or battle of yours doesn't count, the ruling), an intervening if
asked again on resolution (rule 603.4). `attacking-an-opponent.test.ts`.
Proving cards: Skyboon Evangelist, Soulblade Corrupter, Mirkwood Trapper. Left
for `author-freed`: Oviya and Mazzy (their other abilities not checked here);
Scriv still waits on a token created attached.

### Attacked a battle this turn — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 key `condition:attacked-battle-this-turn`.
`GameObject.attackedBattleThisTurn`, set as a creature is declared attacking a
battle (rule 506.3) and kept for the turn whatever becomes of the battle (War
Historian's ruling), reset like `attackedThisTurn` (and kept in the
last-known snapshot as `attackedBattleOnTurn`); `CardFilter.attackedBattleThisTurn`
asks it. The scaffolder reads "… as long as it attacked [a battle] this turn"
and "… as long as it entered this turn". `attacked-battle-this-turn.test.ts`.
Proving card: War Historian.

### How many creatures are blocking it — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 keys `amount:creatures-blocking-it`,
`effect:amount-creatures-blocking-it` and, riding along,
`condition:source-blocked-by-at-least-one`. `{ blockersOf }` names the source or
the trigger object (Spined Sliver, General Marhault Elsdragon, She-Hulk), its
count now `filter.ts`'s `creaturesBlocking`, and a new `CardFilter.blockerCount`
asks the same of a `{ kind: "source" }` condition (Rampaging Cyclops, Grizzled
Wolverine: blocking now, not `blocked`, rule 509.1h). "For each creature it's
blocking" (Guardian of the Gateless) needed nothing new: `countOf` over
`blockingRelation: "blocked-by"`.

### The forms of "becomes tapped" — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 group `trigger:becomes-tapped-batch`. `becomes-tapped` gained
`batched` ("one or more … become tapped": once per tapping at the same time, a whole
attack declaration or `tap-all` or one spell's sequence of taps; Deeproot Pilgrimage),
`firstTimeEachTurn` (`GameObject.tappedOnTurn`, set in `Game.emit` for every tapping;
Tale of Katara and Toph with a `your-turn` condition) and `notAttacking` (Verity
Circle). Captain America, Living Legend's "if it's the first time" is an intervening
if, so it also has to hold as the ability resolves: not read yet, so that card waits
on a look at the creature's count.

### Combat requirements only a whole declaration answers — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:combat-restriction-extensions`' first units.
`StaticAbility.mustBeBlockedBy` is "must be blocked by [filter] if able" (one of
them: Slayer's Cleaver) or, with `all`, "all [filter] able to block this creature
do so" (Talruum Piper, Marble Priest); the restriction
`must-be-blocked-by-exactly-one` is Nacatl War-Pride's, one blocker obeying it and
none or two not; `blocked-by-at-most-one` is the static's restriction for a `restrict`.
`block-if-able` takes a `filter`, a rule for the turn binding what matches later
(rule 611.2c): with `attacker`, Magnetic Web; without, Predatory Rampage. A token
stack under a requirement each token answers for wakes as blockers are asked for
(`Game.wakeStacksBoundToBlock`). `StaticAbility.attacksIfAttacks` is "if [a creature]
attacks, this attacks if able" (Viashino Bey, Ekundu Cyclops, Magnetic Web's first
line), the offer's `attackIf`, `attackingViolations` asking once the declaration is
known (rule 508.1d). "Cowards can't block Warriors" (Boldwyr Intimidator) and Gogo's
target "attacks this turn if able" needed no code: `cantBeBlockedBy` over a filter
scope, and `attack-requirement`'s `target`. Still open in the family: a requirement
aimed at a chosen player (`static:must-attack-specific-player`), a player's "must
attack with at least one creature", and an illegal attacker slot, blank in `targets`
by rule 608.2b, which a requirement staying on it would need read back.

### The creatures blocking a trigger's object — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 key `filter:blocking-trigger-object`. `blockingRelation`
gained `of: "trigger-object"`, bound as the effect applies, for an ability about
a creature other than its own source: Trailblazer's Torch, Ib Halfheart and
Labyrinth Raptor are the proving cards, with Plague Wight for `of: "source"`
reaching `modify-pt-all`. A trigger object that has left is read as it last
was, so Ib's sacrificed Goblin (a token too) still finds its blockers.
`sacrifice-target` now checks `triggerObjectLost`, so a Goblin flickered in
response isn't sacrificed (rule 400.7). The scaffolder reads `becomes-blocked`
on anything but the source.

### Skipping a combat phase — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 unit `effect:skip-next-combat-phase`. `skip-step` gained
`step: "combat"`, a whole phase by rules 500.11 and 614.10, counted as its steps
are (614.10a) and waiting for that player's own turn, with `thisTurn` for "next
combat phase this turn" and `allOfNextTurn` for "all combat phases of their next
turn". The turn goes on to whatever follows a combat phase: an additional one
owed, or the postcombat main phase. All six key cards are proving cards: Stonehorn
Dignitary, Blinding Angel, Revenant Patriarch, Moment of Silence, False Peace,
Empty City Ruse.

### A target chosen at random — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 unit `effect:random-target`. One ability field,
`targetsAtRandom`, on triggered and activated abilities: the game picks each slot
from its legal targets with the seeded randomness, as the ability goes on the
stack or before an activation's costs. They're real targets, so hexproof keeps one
out and ward answers one, and each token of a stack is as likely as any other
permanent. An activated one offers no choice and refuses a named target. Proving
cards: Witch Hunt, Knight Rampager, Scab-Clan Giant, Goblin Test Pilot, Power Pack.
Capricious Efreet needed no code: it's Wild Swing's random modal over chosen
targets. Grip of Chaos is left: it also needs a trigger on anything going on the
stack, `trigger:spell-or-ability-put-on-stack`.

### Choosing one result of a roll — built 2026-11-03

`decision:choose-roll-result`: a `choose-value` of `"roll-result"` as a `roll-dice`'s
`then`, asked only when the dice differ, through the `choose-creature-type` menu of
the numbers and `xByOption`, as `"commander-mana-value"` is. `then` reads that result
as `"x"`, a filter's `n: "x"` too, and `{ roll: "other" }` the other. No new decision.
Proving cards: Reckless Endeavor, Valiant Endeavor, Wild Endeavor. Arcane Endeavor
and Grave Endeavor wait for `author-freed`.

### Odd and even — built 2026-11-03

`decision:choose-odd-or-even`, with `condition:power-parity` and
`effect:parity-restrictions` riding along: the filter clauses `manaValueParity` and
`powerParity`, printed, `$chosen` as a spell resolves or `"chosen"` off the
permanent that chose as it entered. No new decision: `choose-creature-type`'s short
menu and `chooseOnEnter`, `ODD_OR_EVEN` their options. Proving cards: Extinction
Event, Zimone's Hypothesis, Ashling's Prerogative, Gollum, Riddle Master, Void
Winnower, Kianne, Corrupted Memory. Mutinous Massacre and Thanos, the Mad Titan
wait for `author-freed`; Twins of Discord for bloodthirst, Lavabrink Venturer for
protection from a mana value, whose "of the chosen quality" this clause will read.

### A choice beside a spell's targets — built 2026-11-03

`decision:choose-among-targets`: `choose-among-targets` was there, read with `chooser: "you"` and in
a `sequence` of its own steps. `decision:choose-permanents-keeps-targets`: `choose-permanents`'
`keepTargets`, `then` reading the targets from slot 1. No new decision. Proving cards: Aerith Rescue
Mission, Wicked Slumber, Trial of Agony, Crush Underfoot. Cannibalize waits for `author-freed`: with
one target illegal its one ruling says nothing of which fate the other takes.

### Keeping some of yours, by count, by total power or under what you chose — built 2026-11-03

`decision:choose-total-power-subset`, `effect:affect-unchosen-permanents`,
`effect:each-player-keeps-one-exile-rest`, `effect:sacrifice-all-but-up-to-n`: no new decision.
A sum to reach on `choose-permanents` (`together: { totalPowerAtLeast }`, reached through `unless`'s
sacrifice option), a cap on `look-and-choose` (`together: { totalPowerAtMost }`), the filter operand
`{ chosenThisWay: "power" }`, and a `vote`'s `prompt`; `sacrifice-all-but`'s `upTo`, `vote`,
`this-way` and `look-and-choose`'s `leftover: "exile"` were there. Proving cards: Phyrexian
Dreadnought, Moorland Rescuer, Sculpted Sunburst, Thunderwave, Watchers of the Dead, Covetous Elegy.

### One for each of several categories — built 2026-11-03

`decision:choose-land-of-each-basic-type`, `decision:choose-one-of-each-type-sacrifice-rest`,
`decision:keep-one-of-each-color-discard-rest`, `effect:choose-land-per-basic-type`: the
`choose-one-of-each` effect, on the existing `choose-permanents` and `choose-from-zone`
decisions with a new set rule, `"one-of-each"`. Proving cards: Cataclysm, Global Ruin, Planar
Overlay, Sundering Titan, Noxious Vapors. Ajani, Nacatl Pariah keeps that second key but isn't
this shape: its -4 is one permanent kept, and it needs its own read.

### Chroma, what a permanent devoured, a commander's mana value here, a shared creature type — built 2026-11-03

`amount:chroma-of-cards`: `colorSymbols` (mana.ts), as `{ manaSymbolsOf, color }`, an
aggregate's and `ofAffected`'s `{ manaSymbols }`, and a this-way `sumOf`. `amount:creatures-devoured`:
`GameObject.enteredDevoured`, each as it last existed, `{ devoured: "source", filter? }`,
`self-devoured`. `effect:amount-commander-mana-value-battlefield-or-command-zone`,
`effect:choose-commander-for-x`: `greatestCommanderManaValue`'s `onBattlefieldOrInCommandZone`,
`choose-value`'s `"commander-mana-value"` on the existing menu decision.
`effect:amount-greatest-shared-creature-type`, `effect:count-sharing-creature-type`: the
aggregate `"greatest-shared-creature-type"`, `countOf`'s `excludeTriggerObject`. Proving cards:
Fiery Bombardment, Light from Within, Umbra Stalker, Phosphorescent Feast, Marrow Chomper,
Skullmulcher, Hellkite Hatchling, Voracious Dragon, Tar Fiend, Cloudkill, Imposing Grandeur,
Stinging Study, Visions of Dominance, Skemfar Shadowsage, Basalt Ravager, White Lotus Tile,
Synchronized Eviction, Shared Animosity. Left: Sanity Grinding (its ten cards stay on top until
after the mill, which `look-and-choose` can't), Visions of Dread.

### The greatest of an amount among players, and a hand counted through a filter — built 2026-11-03

`effect:amount-greatest-count-among-players`, `amount:most-cards-in-hand-among-players`:
`{ greatestAmongPlayers, who?, includingLeft? }`, any amount read per player, inside
`difference` "the player with the most"; the `CountSpec` `"cards-in-largest-opponent-hand"`;
a scoped `draw` reads every number before anyone draws (608.2h).
`amount:cards-in-hand-matching-filter` needed no code: `reveal-hand` then `{ thisWay:
"revealed", filter }`, and the `this-way` condition for a named card.
`effect:gain-control-to-player-with-most-cards-in-hand` neither: `for-each-player`'s `whoIf`.
Proving cards: Inquisition, Baleful Stare, Withering Gaze, Darigaaz, Isperia, Adamaro,
Investigator's Journal, Thought Sponge, Tales of the Ancestors, Scholarship Sponsor, Sokenzan
Renegade.

### Suspend X, and a suspend cost paid to cast — built 2026-11-03

`mechanic:suspend-x`: `suspend: { n: "x", cost }`, the action's `xValue` at least 1
(107.3a), the offer's `xCost`, and X time counters. `effect:cast-paying-suspend-cost`:
`cast-now`'s `suspendCost` (118.9), with `CardFilter.hasSuspend`. Proving cards: Benalish
Commander, Aeon Chronicler, Detritivore, The Face of Boe. Fungal Behemoth and Roiling
Horror are left for `author-freed`.

### Protection outside your commander's color identity — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 units `effect:protection-choice-and-dynamic` and
`effect:protection-from-each-color-overload`. Only one line was missing:
`protection.outsideCommanderIdentity`, read off the static's controller as it
applies, nothing with no commander (rule 903.4f; `hasCommander`, because
`commanderIdentity` is empty both with none and with a colourless one).
Mother of Runes ran on `grant-protection`'s `{ choose: "color" }`, and Eldritch
Immunity on `overload` with `grant-protection`'s `filter`, unchanged. The
scaffolder reads the Plate's line and "Equip commander"; it still reads no
"Overload" line (hand-authored, every one of the 118).

### Looking at a hand as it enters, and more of another's library — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 units `effect:look-at-opponent-hand-as-enters` and
`effect:look-at-target-player-library`. `chooseOnEnterLook` puts Sorcerous
Spyglass's look (`lookAtOpponentHandOnEnter`, now shared) before a `chooseOnEnter`,
and the `choose-creature-type` carries the hand as `looked`, stripped from every
other seat's view as a `choose-card-name`'s is. No filter for "the chosen type":
Archon of Valor's Reach's one static per word covers it. `look-at-library-top`
takes a `count` (Orcish Spy); Dimir Charm ran on `look-and-choose`'s `from` and
needed nothing. Eye Spy, Elemental Augury and Moonring Island are the same shapes.

### Looking at the top of another player's library — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 unit `effect:look-at-top-of-target-players-library`. Its
note said only `look-and-choose` in a `for-each-player` reached another player's
library, but `from` has done that since Architects of Will: Rootwater Mystic,
Lurking Informant, Coral Fighters and Precognition ran unchanged. Two small
changes: `put-into-graveyard` also takes a card in a library, so a `stay` choice's
`then` can move it once paid for (Wand of Denial), and `look-at-library-top` says
in its own prompt that it's a library, which the client titled "hand". Medomai's
Prophecy wasn't taken: chapter III's "for the first time this turn" needs a
ruling no local source has (a spell of that name cast earlier that turn).

### "Those creatures": the batch a trigger fired on — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:attack-batch-those-creatures`, features
only. One filter clause, `triggerBatch`, bound by `bindDynamicCompares` to
`amongObjects` from the trigger's `attackedTogether`, `enteredTogether` or
`arrivedTogether`, so every effect with a filter names them and no effect kind
needed an option. `attacks-player` and a `whenever` delayed trigger now record
`attackedTogether` too (one helper, `withAttackedTogether`), and `DelayedWhenever`
gains `until: "end-of-combat"`. Keys: attack-batch-those-creatures,
attack-batch-per-defender-those-creatures. Proving cards: Sabotage Strategist,
Lightmine Field, Love on the Battlefield, Creepy Puppeteer, Garruk, Curse
Breaker. Still open: Frontier Warmonger's "or a planeswalker they control" and
Squall's intervening if over those creatures.

### Damage replacements from another source, by a live amount, and for the turn — built 2026-11-02

The need-keys Phase 2 family `replacement:damage-modification`, features only. A `would-deal-damage`
static takes `fromOther` ("another source you control", by object, so a namesake's damage counts) and
`plusOf` (`"this-power"`, `{ countersOnThis }`, read as the damage would be dealt).
`player-effect`'s `damageReplacement` makes one for a duration: `plus` fixed as it resolves, `setTo`
("it deals 2 damage instead", a new `set` damage step), `source` asked as the damage would be dealt,
`combat`, and `to: "player-or-battle"`. `bestDamageOrder` already works out the order that hurts least
(rule 616.1), so the ordering key was stale, as were two more: a prevention's `then` reads the
damage's source (Swans), and "1 damage to itself" is a simultaneous `sequence`. The scaffolder reads
the "another source" statics and the for-the-turn sentences. Keys: damage-becomes-fixed-amount-this-turn,
damage-plus-from-other-sources, damage-plus-source-power, damage-plus-this-turn-to-player-or-battle,
damage-plus-x-this-turn, damage-plus-counters-on-source, affected-player-orders-damage-modifiers,
damage-source-itself, damage-source-controller-draws. Proving cards: Thor, Asgard's Avenger; Embermaw
Hellion; Hawkeye, Young Avenger; Fated Firepower; Equal Treatment; The Flame of Keld; Rankle and
Torbran; Swans of Bryn Argoll; Reckless Embermage. Left: Taii Wakeen's "deals noncombat damage equal
to that creature's toughness" trigger, for `author-freed` to narrow.

**Casting from a zone other than the hand** (2026-11-02). `cast-now`'s `from: { handOf, look? }` is another
player's hand during a resolution, seen whole through the decision's `looked`; `player-effect`'s
`playFromHandOf` and `handRevealed` are Sen Triplets' turn-long permission, a `via: "other-hand"` cast
and the land play beside it (`Game.handsOpenTo`); `castSelfFromExile` is the card's own permission;
`exile-graveyard`'s `untilSourceLeaves` with `allow-cast-linked-exile` (`GameObject.linkedCastGrants`,
one spell per resolution, live over the linked cards) is Idol of Endurance; `castWhileSearchingLibrary`
offers a `cast-now` (`from: "while-searching"`) as a search of your own library begins. Look-and-choose's
`zone: "hand"` with `from` already took Zara. Keys: cast-from-opponents-hand, cast-self-from-exile,
cast-from-linked-exile-free-this-turn, cast-while-searching-library. Proving cards: Silent-Blade Oni,
Mindleech Mass, Mindclaw Shaman, Sen Triplets, Zara, Renegade Recruiter, Eternal Scourge, Misthollow
Griffin, Idol of Endurance, Planeswalker's Mischief, Panglacial Wurm.

**Damage dealt by another object** (2026-11-02). Most of it was there: `DamageFrom`'s `"attached"`, a slot
and `"trigger-object"`, a `reflexive-trigger`'s `about: "put-this-way"`, `each-deals-damage`. New:
`OtherThan`'s `"host"` (Pain for All's "any other target"), `each-deals-damage`'s `to: "host"` (Aura
Barbs), and the `host-now` condition (Consuming Ferocity's "if that creature has three or more +1/+0
counters", on the creature it was attached to even once the Aura has gone). Keys: damage-from-attached,
damage-from-created-token, damage-from-exiled-card, damage-from-this-way. Proving cards: Bind the Monster,
Dizzying Gaze, Pain for All, Consuming Ferocity, Aura Barbs, Chainer's Torment, Selfless Exorcist,
Vivien's Invocation.

**A filter clause for double-faced cards (2026-11-02).** `CardFilter`'s `doubleFaced`: nonmodal, modal
and meld cards (rule 712.1), any zone, either face up, with `meldCard` on a meld pair's fronts.
Keys: condition:filter-double-faced, effect:look-and-choose-may-enter-transformed (which misread its one
card: Nick Fury puts the card, then may transform it, so the key needed no "enters transformed" option).
Proving cards: Overgrown Pest, Nick Fury, Agent of S.H.I.E.L.D.

**An effect done to each matching permanent (2026-11-02).** `for-each-permanent`: `filter`, `effect` bound
to slot `as`, `exceptSource`, `exceptTriggerObject`, `exceptTarget`, `controlledByTarget`, `simultaneous`; a
token stack is woken first (rule 700.3b) and what's gone by its turn is skipped (400.7). Replaces a new `-all`
twin per effect. Keys: effect:tap-all-except-source, effect:animate-all-excluding-source, effect:return-to-hand-all-except-source, effect:mass-bounce-except-source, effect:sacrifice-all-except-source, effect:grant-activated-all-except-source, effect:lose-keywords-all-except-source, effect:mass-effect-except-trigger-object, effect:damage-all-except-target, effect:pump-all-except-target, effect:animate-all-lose-abilities, effect:transform-all, effect:set-creature-type-all, effect:set-color-all, effect:shuffle-into-library-all, effect:set-land-types-all, effect:animate-all-controlled-by-target, effect:unattach-effect, effect:damage-all-per-object. Proving cards: Thundermare, Disarm, Nightcreep, Baki's Curse,
Public Execution, Exhilarating Elocution, Aethersquall Ancient, Polymorphist's Jest, Thundercloud Elemental,
Moonmist.

**Keys the vocabulary already covered (2026-11-02).** A pilot wrote these keys' cards with no engine change: the
note had gone stale or a composition hadn't been tried. Keys: effect:search-library-to-exile, effect:shuffle-library, effect:delayed-life-gain-trigger, effect:target-player-exiles-from-graveyard, condition:cant-be-blocked-except-by-flying-or-reach. Cards: Selective Memory,
Foresight, Lantern of Insight, Natural Selection, Vizkopa Guildmage, Merrow Bonegnawer, Signal Pest
(`test/need-key-recheck.test.ts`).

**Permissions over exiled cards (2026-11-02).** One `ExilePermission` is `exile`'s and `discard-chosen`'s `mayCast` and
`look-and-choose`'s `playable`: `byOwner`, `free`, `tax`, `landsEnterTapped`, `castOnly`. Over the source's linked
cards: `playFromExile.linked` (static) and `allow-cast-linked-exile`'s `play`, `anyNumber`, `reduce` (resolved, rule
611.2c, live); `exile-from-library`'s and `impulse-exile`'s `linked`; `allow-cast-from-exile`'s `spendAs`. Keys:
zone:exiled-with-source-play, zone:exile-owner-may-play, zone:exiled-from-hand-play-permission-taxed,
zone:exile-owner-may-cast-now, zone:exile-trigger-object-play-this-turn (no code), zone:cast-from-linked-exile,
zone:cast-linked-exiled-card-gated, zone:court-free-cast-from-linked-exile, zone:allow-cast-from-exile-spend-any-type.
Proving cards: Nightveil Specter, Intellect Devourer, Raphael, Most Attitude, Soul Partition, Release to the Wind,
Suspend Aggression, Elite Spellbinder, Lightstall Inquisitor, Rona, Disciple of Gix, Serpent's Soul-Jar, Null Summoner,
Court of Locthwain, Gonti, Lord of Luxury, Norin, Swift Survivalist (`test/exile-permission-options.test.ts`).

**Exiled face down, linked, with no permission (2026-11-02).** `exile-from-library`'s `faceDown` (`true`: nobody may
look; `"looked"`: the effect's controller alone), with `exiledFaceDown.lookersOnly` keeping a linked source's next
controller out, as a hideaway's isn't; `turn-exiled-face-up`; `search-library`'s `"exile-face-down"` with `linked` was
there. Keys: effect:exile-top-face-down-linked, effect:search-to-linked-exile-face-down. Proving cards: Bomat Courier,
Knowledge Vault, Pyxis of Pandemonium, Wall of Mourning, Kayla's Music Box, The Creation of Avacyn
(`test/exile-face-down-linked.test.ts`).

**An effect done by another player (2026-11-02).** `as-player`: `who` (any `EffectPlayerRef`, with the new
`"source-owner"`), `effect`; inside it "you" is them, what it asks is theirs and what it makes is theirs. Replaces a
`who` per effect. Keys: effect:target-player-scries, effect:incubate-for-player, effect:discard-hand-target-players, effect:owner-of-source-player-ref, effect:discover-by-other-player, effect:sacrifice-controller-of-target. Proving cards: Excise the Imperfect, Wheel and Deal, Gandalf, Wandering Wizard.

**Damage prevented this way (2026-11-02).** The `damagePreventedThisWay` amount: what the shields of this
effect's resolution have prevented so far, read off `damage-prevented` events that name it (`shieldMadeIn`),
in a delayed ability it set up too (`LastKnownRefs.madeIn`). A chosen source's `then` now reads the spell's own
targets, not the source chosen (`ownTargets`). Key: amount:damage-prevented-this-way. Found to need nothing:
effect:prevent-all-damage-by-filter (`damage-rule`'s `by.filter`). Proving cards: Sacred Boon, Scars of the
Veteran, Bone Mask, Brace for Impact, Refraction Trap, Chant of Vitu-Ghazi.

**What an earlier step did: control and counts (2026-11-02).** `ThisWayKind`'s `"gained-control"` (what really changed
hands) and `"control-effect"` (what a control effect applied to, already yours or not: Mob Rule's ruling;
`ControlEffect.madeAtSeq`), and the `keptOnTopThisWay` amount (a count off `scried`, the cards stay hidden). A revealed
card's mana value now reads in a hidden zone. Dihada, Rowan and Tibalt's "Untap them" moved to `"control-effect"`.
Keys: effect:this-way-gained-control, effect:surveil-kept-on-top-count. Found to need no code: effect:returned-this-way-grant
(`put-onto-battlefield`), amount:cards-revealed-this-way (`reveal-until` already reports them), amount:cards-shuffled-this-way
(`shuffled-into-library`). Proving cards: Broadcast Takeover, Mob Rule, Wake the Past, Coveted Falcon, Kain, Yes Man, Madcap
Experiment, Goblin Charbelcher, Kamahl's Summons, Naya Soulbeast, Psychic Spiral, Starving Revenant.
**An unattach as a cost (2026-11-02).** `AbilityCost.unattach`: `"self"`, `"granter"` or a chosen `{ filter }`
(rule 701.3d), unpayable with nothing attached; the chosen one is read back as `"sacrificed"`, and a `divided` total
that reads it gets one offer per total. Keys: cost:unattach-cost, cost:unattach-equipment. Proving cards: Leonin Bola,
Razor Boomerang, Sunforger, Captain America, First Avenger (`test/unattach-cost.test.ts`).

**A keyword's cost that isn't mana (2026-11-02).** `KickerAlsoCost` on `splice.also` as well as `kicker.also`, with
`exileFromGraveyard` and `opponentGainsLife` added, multiplied for squad's "any number of times" and bounding its
offers; several spliced cards' costs paid in turn (`GameState.pendingAlsoCosts`). Keys: cost:splice-non-mana,
keyword:squad-nonmana-cost. Proving cards: Horobi's Whisper, Hundred-Talon Strike, Roar of Jukai, Torrent of Stone,
Veil of Secrecy, Ruthless Radrat, Thrill-Kill Disciple (`test/keyword-nonmana-cost.test.ts`).

**Can't have or gain a keyword (2026-11-02).** `StaticAbility.cantHaveKeywords` and `lose-keywords`'s `cantHave`
(`PtModifier.cantHaveKeywords`): removed after everything else in the keyword fold, so no timestamp beats it, and a
keyword counter stays but gives nothing. `grantableKeywords` drops the keyword from a `grant-keyword` or `animate` made
meanwhile, which the rulings say isn't created. A loss of hexproof now takes every "hexproof from" (702.11e), which
Shadowspear's had missed. Key: static:cant-have-keyword. Proving cards: Archetype of Imagination, Archetype of Endurance,
Arcane Lighthouse; Colossus Hammer needed no code (`loseKeywords`).

**A static that adds a supertype (2026-11-02).** `StaticAbility.addSupertypes`, also `typesOutsideBattlefield.addSupertypes`:
layer 4 (613.1d), folded with the types and read through `effectiveSupertypes` by every filter, the legend and world rules,
snow mana, last-known information and the view. After the fold's first pass a scope sees the supertypes the last pass ended
with, so an effect adding "basic" comes before a "nonbasic lands" one whatever their timestamps (613.8a). Keys:
static:static-grant-supertype, static:characteristics-of-library-cards (the "land cards in your library" half). Found to
need no code: static:static-add-chosen-type (`addsChosenCreatureType`). Proving cards: On Serra's Wings, In Bolas's Clutches,
Leyline of Singularity, Glittering Frost, Rimefeather Owl, Rootpath Purifier, Lifecraft Engine.

**Every graveyard at once (2026-11-02).** No new vocabulary: `return-from-graveyard`'s `from: "each-graveyard"` (each card
under its owner's control, `enterTapped`, `withCounters`) and `from: "all-graveyards"` with `destination: "hand"` (each to its
owner's) already ran these, and the scaffolder now reads them. `sacrifice-all`'s filter now sees its source, for "all other
creatures". Keys: effect:return-all-graveyards-under-owners-control, effect:return-from-all-graveyards-to-hand. Proving cards:
Open the Vaults, Planar Birth, Pyrrhic Revival, Bringer of the Last Gift, Infernal Caretaker, Saprazzan Bailiff.

**Whole hands exiled face down, and back later (2026-11-02).** `exile-graveyard`'s `faceDown` with `fromHand: "only"` (through
the face-down step `exile-from-library` has, `turnExiledFaceDown`), read by a delayed ability's `about: "exiled"`, whose new
`orNone` sets it up with nothing done, or by a linked ability: `return-exiled-by-source`'s `linked: "hand"` now takes `filter`
("cards you own") and `others`. Timing `your-next-turn-end-step`, and `turnOf` for a `your-…` timing on another player's turn
(rule 603.7d). The view leaves hidden cards out of a stack object's targets. Keys: effect:exile-hands-face-down-return-later,
effect:delayed-trigger-next-turn-end-step. Proving cards: Memory Jar, Magus of the Jar, Ignorant Bliss, Suppress, Induced
Amnesia, Bottled Cloister, Moonring Mirror, Kav Landseeker.
**Durations to a point in a later turn (2026-11-02).** `UntilPointDuration`, values of `PtDuration` so every effect taking
one has them, and of `gain-control`'s `until`: `"until-your-next-upkeep"`, `"until-end-of-your-next-upkeep"`,
`"until-end-of-your-next-turn"`, `"until-its-controllers-next-untap-step"`. Stored as `UntilPoint` on a modifier or a control
effect and passed by one `Game.passUntilPoints`: as a step begins (500.4), as it ends (500.5), at that turn's cleanup (514.2),
past a skipped step (614.10a), and as the turn of a player who has left would have begun (800.4m). `ImpulseDuration` gains
`"until-your-next-turn"` and `"until-your-next-upkeep"`. Keys: duration:until-your-next-upkeep,
effect:until-controllers-next-untap-step-duration, effect:until-end-of-your-next-turn,
zone:impulse-until-your-next-turn-begins. Found to need no code: effect:exile-may-cast-until-your-next-turn (`exile`'s `mayCast`).
Proving cards: Xenic Poltergeist, Gabriel Angelfire, Halfdane, Elkin Bottle, Cycle of Life, Orcish Farmer, Stilt-Man, Towering
Terror, Hurl Through Hell, Nivix, Aerie of the Firemind.

**An animate that sets creature characteristics (2026-11-02).** A live P/T on an animated permanent is `animate` (with no P/T of
its own) beside a `grant-static`'s `setBasePtFromCount`, not a CDA once granted (rule 604.3a), with the new `CountSpec`
`{ countersOnThis }`. `add-types`' `removeTypes`: "isn't a creature until end of turn", its subtypes going too and an attacker or
blocker out of combat (506.4). `StaticAbility.selfOutsideBattlefield`: a card's own types and base P/T everywhere but the
battlefield (113.6c). Keys: effect:animate-with-cda-pt (the counters half), effect:remove-card-type-until-eot,
effect:stops-being-creature, effect:stops-being-creature-until-eot, static:self-type-changes (the off-the-battlefield half). Found
to need no code: effect:animate-planeswalker-with-trigger, effect:set-pt-from-target, static:set-characteristics. Proving cards:
Myth Realized, Chimeric Mass, Svogthos, the Restless Tomb, Beorn's Hospitality, Druid Class, Oko, Thief of Crowns, Sparkshaper
Visionary, Sworn Defender, Neurok Transmuter, Blink, Grist, the Hunger Tide.

**Combat restrictions read as another player (2026-11-02).** `PlayerTest.holds`: any condition read with the defending or attacking
player as its "you", in place of the `monarch` and `poisoned` clauses it replaces. The same asked of you is the static's own
`condition` over `restrictions`. Keys: static:cant-attack-unless-defender-controls (a graveyard count was the only code), found to
need no code: static:cant-attack-if-defender-controls, static:block-restriction-attacking-player-condition. Proving cards: Vantress
Gargoyle, Monstrous Hound, Branded Brawlers, Lurking Green Dragon, Crown-Hunter Hireling.

**An animate that grants a whole ability (2026-11-02).** All composition: `animate`, then `grant-triggered`, `grant-static` or
`grant-protection` in one `sequence`, by one duration. Two pieces of code: `combat/eligibility.ts`'s `ownStatics` reads granted statics
too, so a granted "can't attack unless defending player …" binds, and `add-types`' `removeSubtypes` for "it isn't an Equipment". Keys:
effect:animate-grants-conditional-static, effect:animate-grants-protection, effect:animate-grants-toxic, effect:animate-grants-triggered.
Proving cards: Raging Ravine, Restless Spire, Veiled Serpent, Veiled Apparition, Opal Guardian, Monument to Perfection, Dancing Sword.
**An alternative cost's other parts, and which cost was paid (2026-11-02).** `alternativeCost.also` is a `KickerAlsoCost`, paid with
the keywords' and a splice's by the same code. `self-alternative-cost-paid` reads whether the spell was cast for its own alternative
cost (`madness: true`: for its madness cost), kept by a copy. A `target` condition's filter reads `n: "x"`. `kicker.keyword: "additional
cost"`: an optional additional cost with no keyword (rule 118.8), not a kicked spell to anything else. Keys:
condition:alternative-cost-paid, cost:alternative-cost-exile-from-graveyard, cost:optional-additional-mana-cost (Chorus of the Conclave's
half stays narrow). Found to need no code: cost:conditional-alternative-cost (`condition` took any `StaticCondition` already). Proving
cards: Stalwart Valkyrie, Blasphemous Edict, Asmoranomardicadaistinaculdacar, Fervent Mastery, Ingenious Mastery, From Under the
Floorboards, Welcome to the Fold, Undergrowth.

**A spell gaining an ability, carried onto the permanent (2026-11-02).** `grant-keyword` and `grant-triggered` on a permanent spell are
kept by the permanent it becomes (rule 400.7a). `"rebound"` is a `Keyword`, read as the spell resolves. `enters-with-counters`'
`ifAsItEnters` is a gained bloodthirst. Riot from a static or from what the spell gained is asked as it enters (614.12:
`enteringKeywordInstances`), each instance apart (702.136b). `add-mana`'s `spellGains.lasting`. Keys: effect:cast-spell-gains-bloodthirst,
effect:grant-ability-to-permanent-spell, effect:grant-rebound-to-spell, static:grant-riot. Found already built: static:grant-cascade,
static:next-spell-uncounterable. Proving cards: Taigam, Ojutai Master; Cast Through Time; Bloodlord of Vaasgoth; Rhythm of the Wild; Uncivil
Unrest; Domri, Chaos Bringer; Rivaz of the Claw; Thief of Existence.
**One unless clause, whoever answers and whatever they do (2026-11-02).** `unless`'s `chooser` takes any `EffectPlayerRef`, read as
the effect applies, and `{ anyOf: <scope> }` (each in turn order until one takes it); its options gain `{ doing }` (any effect, the
chooser's own, offered as a `may` of it would be: `mayBeDone`, which now asks a hand for a `look-and-choose`), `{ payManaCostOf }`
(rule 107.3h's X, 118.6's no cost) and a sacrifice's `exceptTarget`. `sacrifice-target` waits while choices are held (rule 101.4), as
a chosen sacrifice does. `player-effect`'s `cantAttack` gains `"all"` and `{ planeswalkers }`, with `cantAttackBy` and `duration:
"this-combat"`. `CardFilter.inZone`. Keys: cost:unless-pay-generic-per-counter, effect:pay-or-cant-attack-you, effect:unless-exile-graveyard,
effect:unless-put-card-from-hand-on-top, effect:unless-sacrifice-other-than-target, effect:other-player-may-pay-spell-mana-cost,
effect:sacrifice-greatest-unless-pay-its-mana-cost, effect:unless-target-controller, effect:unless-that-player, effect:unless-discard-hand.
Found to need no code: effect:unless-discard-filtered-card (`may` with `else`: Hidden Horror's shape), and "{B} or {3}" (two `unless`es
nested). Proving cards: Grip of Amnesia, Perplex, Tainted Specter, Cursed Monstrosity, Torment of Venom, Energy Vortex, Lim-Dûl's Hex,
Sivriss, Nightmare Speaker, Ice Cave, Tariff, Champions of Minas Tirith, Jace, Multiverse Architect, Overencumbered.

**A copy for each permanent a spell could target, by whoever makes it (2026-11-02).** `copy-spell`'s `forEachItCouldTarget` takes
`controller` (the caster's copies, `filter` from their side), judges "could target" from the spell's caster always (Ink-Treader
Nephilim's ruling), `other: "targeted"` (other than what the spell targets), and `chooseAndPayEach` (a `choose-permanents` of up to
what can be paid for, `min` 0, each answer paying then copying). A `trigger-object` condition now knows its `source`, so an intervening
"if that spell targets only this creature" reads. `gravestorm()` (rule 702.69a) needed no engine code: `copy-spell`'s `count` of
`turnHistory: "to-graveyard"`. Keys: effect:copy-spell-for-each-by-caster, effect:copy-spell-for-each-chosen-paid, keyword:gravestorm.
Proving cards: Mirrorwing Dragon, Precursor Golem, Ink-Treader Nephilim, Feather, Radiant Arbiter, Ominous Harvest, Follow the Bodies,
Bitter Ordeal.

**A delayed trigger that watches named permanents (2026-11-02).** `delayed-trigger`'s `at: { whenever, watching, until, once, orLosesControl }`
(`delayed-trigger-watching.test.ts`): `watching` is what `"self"` in the trigger reads as, so any trigger that says "self" can watch a target,
and it replaced `at: { dealsCombatDamage }`. `until: "game"` and `once` for a delayed "when" with no duration. Plague Reaver's return is
`turnOf`, and "until your next turn, whenever" was built already. Keys: effect:delayed-trigger-rest-of-game, effect:delayed-trigger-target-deals-damage,
effect:delayed-trigger-target-player-next-upkeep, effect:delayed-trigger-until-your-next-turn. Proving cards: Paladin of Prahv, Arcbond, Tamiyo, Field Researcher, Coffin Queen, Plague Reaver.

**A spell's additional cost in a keyword cost's shape (2026-11-02).** `additionalCost.also` and an option's `also`: one `KickerAlsoCost`, read by `castAlsoCosts`, so
a spell's additional cost pays whatever a kicker's can, and `tapUntapped` and `returnToHand` needed no code of their own (`additional-cost-also.test.ts`). New: `tapAnyNumber`
(`TapCostOffer.anyNumber`, X the count, the client's tap prompt too) and `additionalCost.discardFilter`. Keys: cost:additional-cost-tap-permanents,
cost:additional-cost-discard-filter, and cost:discard-x-cost, built already (Firestorm, Channeled Force). Proving cards: Warlord's Elite, Shared Discovery, Gaze of Justice,
Burn at the Stake, Disruption Protocol, Deprive, Magmatic Insight, Scorched Earth.

**An ability's life cost read as it's paid (2026-11-02).** `AbilityLifeCost`, read only through `abilityLifeCost(state, player, cost, x, source)`, gains `"half-rounded-up"`, `"x"` (its X capped by
the life there is) and `{ each, per }` (`dynamic-life-cost.test.ts`). Key: cost:dynamic-life-cost. Proving cards: Lurking Evil, Murderous Betrayal, Krumar Initiate, Tornado. Hand of Vecna's
`per: "card-in-hand"` is built, but it waits on the Vecna token; Protection Racket's life is an effect's.

**A spell's cost raised by coloured pips (2026-11-03).** `costModification.increaseColored` adds coloured pips to matching spells' total cost before any reduction (rule 601.2f), to an alternative cost as well (118.9d); the shown cost adds them (`cost-increase-colored.test.ts`). Key: static:cost-increase-colored. Proving cards: Derelor, Sapphire Leech, Andradite Leech.

**Controlled continuously since the turn began (2026-11-03).** `CardFilter.controlledContinuously` reads rule 302.6's test off `summoningSick`, on a permanent of any type, haste or no haste: the source's `condition`, a target with `controlledBy: "active-player"`, what a player sacrifices. Keys: condition:controlled-since-turn-start, filter:controlled-continuously-since-turn-start. Cards: Rocket Launcher, Nettling Imp, Norritt, Keldon Twilight (`controlled-continuously.test.ts`). Total War and Siren's Call each ask more: "didn't attack" of one declaration, not of the turn, and a cast timing condition. Arcum's Whistle's optional payment is unchecked.

**Different mana costs and the mana value threshold (2026-11-03).** `of: "mana-cost"` on a `distinct` aggregate counts the symbols of a mana cost rather than their total, a card with none adding nothing (rule 202.1b); "five or more mana values among cards in your graveyard" was already an `aggregate` condition, now read by the scaffolder on `flashIf`, an intervening if and a spell's "unless" as well as a static. Keys: amount:count-distinct-mana-costs, condition:distinct-mana-values-in-graveyard. Cards: Embodiment of Agonies, Graveyard Shift, Sanguine Spy, Tainted Indulgence (`distinct-mana-costs.test.ts`).
**One target slot over players and permanents (2026-11-03).** No new target kind: `permanent`'s `orPlayer` with `typesAnyOf` or `filter.anyOf` already says each part with its own filter (rule 115.4 for "any target"). What was missing was the cards, the scaffolder's phrases and `describeTargetSpec`, which dropped the player part of a slot with `whose` and read `anyOf` as "permanent" (`target-players-and-permanents.test.ts`). Keys: effect:any-target-filter, target:creature-planeswalker-or-player, target:creature-token-player-or-planeswalker, target:player-planeswalker-or-filtered-creature, target:opponent-or-creature-or-planeswalker-an-opponent-controls. Proving cards: Wrathful Red Dragon, Wrathful Raptors, Lozhan, Dragons' Legacy, Coalborn Entity, Cautery Sliver, Captain's Maneuver. Needle Drop's "any target that was dealt damage this turn" stays open: nothing records a player being dealt damage this turn.

**Tokens created with this (2026-11-03).** `create-token`/`create-token-copy`'s `linkedToSource` stamps each token with its source and stint (`GameObject.createdWith`, in the fold key, minted apart); the filter clause `createdWithSource` finds them, bound by `bindDynamicCompares`, in a `delayed-trigger`'s effect as that's created; `countersOnMaker` reads the counters on that source as a token's P/T; `create-token-copy`'s `who` takes a player slot (`tokens-created-with-source.test.ts`). Keys: effect:tokens-created-with-source, static:token-pt-from-source-counters, static:token-pt-from-creator-counters. Proving cards: Saproling Burst, Faerie Artisans, Arcane Artisan, Gutter Grime, Mysterio, Master of Illusion.

**A token linked to what made it (2026-11-03).** `DelayedLeaves.leaves: "created"` is "when that token leaves": every token the resolution made, woken out of a stack and watched on its own (`createdTokensApart`), with `about` on a `leaves` watch now too (`about: "exiled"` for Ugin's "put the exiled card into your hand"; `leaves: "source"`, `about: "created"` for Stangg's "exile that token when Stangg leaves"). `link-exiled-to-created` makes each token hold the card it means by "the exiled card" (`GameObject.linkedExile`, in the fold key and `LastKnownInfo`), read by `return-exiled-by-source` of `linked`, which has `enterTapped` (`linked-token-leaves.test.ts`). Keys: effect:linked-token-leaves, effect:token-trigger-returns-linked-exiled-card. Proving cards: Ugin, the Ineffable, Hofri Ghostforge, Timothar, Baron of Bats, Stangg, Tatsumasa, the Dragon's Fang. Dance of Many waits on printed triggers naming its token, Gutter Grime on static:token-pt-from-creator-counters.

**"Whenever one or more … die" (2026-11-03).** A `batched` `dies` trigger fires once per simultaneous event off `permanents-died`, emitted when `withLeaveBatch` finishes (or at once for a lone death), every permanent that left in that event looking back (rule 603.10a); the matches are `triggerValue`, a doubler counts only the deaths that caused the trigger, and `put-arrived-onto-battlefield` (now with `enterTapped`, `underOwner`, `withCounters`) takes "one of them" from the cards among them (`dies-batch-trigger.test.ts`). Key: trigger:dies-batch-trigger. Proving cards: Vengeful Townsfolk, Seer of Stolen Sight, Rinoa, Angel Wing.

**Offering (2026-11-03).** Rule 702.48: `kicker: { cost: "", keyword: "offering", flash: true, offering: <quality> }`, one kicked offer per permanent of the quality, named as the cast's `sacrifice`, its total cost less that permanent's mana cost (`manaCostReductions`, `ownManaCostOf`; rule 702.48c), a twobrid half that matters its own offer by `costOption` (118.7e). No new decision; the client's buttons already read `emergeSacrifice` and `kickerCost`. Also the filter `attackingOpponent` (`offering.test.ts`). Key: keyword:offering. Proving cards: Patron of the Akki, Patron of the Kitsune, Blast-Furnace Hellkite.
**A static making a planeswalker a creature (2026-11-03).** The type statics already said it: `addTypes` for "that's still a
planeswalker" (rule 205.1b), `setTypes` where the words are missing (205.1a), under any `condition`. What it took: a planeswalker being
attacked that stops being one is removed from combat, and what attacks it attacks nothing (506.4, 506.4c; `attackingNothing`, also for a
battle); `setBasePtFromAffectedCounters` (each permanent's own count, layer 7b); `grantPtPerCount.anyCountersOnAffected`; `attach`'s
`planeswalkerToo` for "Equip planeswalker" (702.6e). Key: static:planeswalker-becomes-creature. Proving cards: Gideon Blackblade, Grand
Master of Flowers, Kaito, Bane of Nightmares, Spark Rupture, Luxior, Giada's Gift.

**A mass change of types, P/T left alone (2026-11-03).** No new effect: `add-types` under `for-each-permanent`, as Nightcreep's. What it took:
`choose-creature-type`'s `sentinel` (`FIRST_CHOSEN_TYPE`) nests a second choice inside the first. Keys: effect:add-types-all,
effect:add-land-types-all. Proving cards: Start Your Engines, The Thanos-Copter, Volatile Claws, Energybending, Terraformer, Vision Charm.

**A may another player answers (2026-11-03).** No new engine feature was the core of it: `unless { anyOf: "each-opponent" }` with a `{ doing }` option already asks each in turn (rule 101.4), stops at the first yes and runs `otherwise` if nobody did, and `each-player-may` of `who: { controllerOfTarget }` or `"trigger-controller"` already named one answerer. Added: `return-to-hand`'s `from: "library"`, `mayBeDone` on `put-into-graveyard` and `move-exiled-this-way` (rule 608.2d: an option that can't be done isn't offered), and effect worth reading `{ manaValueOf: slot }`, so the bots refuse a lethal yes (`any-opponent-may.test.ts`). Keys: decision:any-opponent-may, decision:may-answered-by-other-player. Proving: Sin Prodder, Distant Memories, Arcum Dagsson, Farrel's Mantle. The scaffolder reads "its controller / that player may search …", "target X's controller sacrifices it", and both any-opponent lines.
**A tap cost on one permanent, referred back to (2026-11-03).** `tapOthers` of `{ hostOfSource: true }` is an Aura's "Tap enchanted creature" (the candidates now read their filter with the source), and the `EffectTargetRef` `"tapped"` is the one permanent a cost tapped, which a spell now remembers too and a copy inherits. Keys: cost:tap-enchanted-permanent, cost:tap-untapped-creature-additional. Proving cards: Earthlore, Krovikan Plague, Nature's Chosen, Swallow Whole.

**Mana abilities from another zone, or exiling from a graveyard (2026-11-03).** `isManaAbility` no longer asks where an ability works from (rule 605.1a), `exilesFromHand` is the hand's exile, and `exileFromGraveyard` and `collectEvidence` wait on `thenManaAbility` as `exileFromHand` already did. Keys: effect:hand-mana-ability, effect:mana-ability-graveyard-exile-cost. Proving cards: Simian Spirit Guide, Elvish Spirit Guide, Molt Tender, Rubble Rouser, Cryptex, Jack-o'-Lantern, Titans' Nest.

**Landwalk of any kind (2026-11-03).** `StaticAbility.landwalk` is the land as a filter, or `"chosen-land-type"` off `chooseOnEnter`, and shows as the `landwalk` keyword (what Hammerheim and Staff of the Ages name); a lone land type with a keyword of its own gives that keyword, so Traveler's Cloak on Island is islandwalk to Quagmire and Merfolk Assassin. `combat/eligibility.ts`'s `landwalkStopsBlocksBy` is what a block and the bots' `canBlock` ask. It can write `keyword:snow-landwalk` and `keyword:legendary-landwalk` too (`{ supertype: "snow", subtype: "Swamp" }`, `{ supertype: "legendary" }`), which aren't marked built: neither has a proving card yet. Keys: keyword:artifact-landwalk, keyword:chosen-land-type-landwalk, keyword:nonbasic-landwalk. Proving cards: Dryad Sophisticate, Trailblazer's Boots, Vectis Gloves, Traveler's Cloak, Illusionary Presence.
