**The greatest of a zone (2026-11-03).** `greatestInZone: { of, among }` is one filter clause, one comparison over everything in the object's zone that matches `among`, ties counted: every permanent on the battlefield whoever controls it, or that graveyard. It's a target filter (Topple), a static's scope with mana value (Favor of the Mighty; P/T isn't known inside the fold), `return-from-graveyard`'s filter (Desecrator Hag), and through existing conditions a target (Getaway Glamer), the triggering object (Strength-Testing Hammer), an Aura's host (Historian's Wisdom), and `not` of `opponent-controls` for "you control each" (Might Makes Right). `ConditionOptions.outsideFold` lets a trigger's condition see its source's own statics. Okk's `static:cant-attack-block-unless-greater-power-joins` needs a declaration check of its own and stays open; Summon: Fenrir still needs `effect:next-creature-enters-with-counter`. Keys: condition:greatest-power-condition, condition:no-other-creature-greater-power-condition, condition:trigger-object-greatest-power, filter:greatest-mana-value-among-all-creatures, filter:greatest-power-on-battlefield. Proving cards: Topple, Favor of the Mighty, Getaway Glamer, Historian's Wisdom, Might Makes Right, Strength-Testing Hammer, Desecrator Hag.

**What an Aura says of its host (2026-11-03).** `filter:enchanted-by-source` was already `hostOfSource`: Power Artifact (`abilityCostModification.applies`), Desert's Hold and Detainment Spell (`prohibits.abilitiesOf`) and Demonic Torment (`fromAttached`, needed no filter) only wanted authoring. New: `controlledBy: "monarch"`, through `attachedTo` in a `not`'d `source` condition (Fall from Favor), and `StaticAbility.blocksDespiteLandwalk`, the blocker's side of rule 702.14 (Street Savvy), which `blockableDespiteLandwalk` isn't. Keys: condition:enchanted-controller-is-monarch, filter:enchanted-by-source, static:enchanted-blocks-despite-landwalk. Proving cards: Fall from Favor, Power Artifact, Demonic Torment, Desert's Hold, Detainment Spell, Street Savvy.

**Triggered mana abilities that do more (2026-11-03).** A `tapped-for-mana` trigger's `add-mana` carries the rest of the ability in `also`, off the stack (rule 605.4a), with `"trigger-object"` the tapped permanent and `"trigger-player"` its tapper; its `condition` is read as the mana's made (`tappedForManaExtras`), `producing` takes `"chosen"`, `all-creatures` takes `chosenColorOnly`, and `TurnStat` `"lands-tapped-for-mana"` is new. Crime Novelist's key was misread: a sacrifice trigger is no mana ability (605.5a), so it's a plain trigger and needed nothing; Hall of the Bandit Lord's was `spellGains.lasting`. Keys: effect:tapped-for-mana-condition, effect:tapped-for-mana-land-deals-damage, effect:triggered-mana-ability-rider, effect:sacrifice-triggered-mana-ability, effect:tapped-for-mana-chosen-color-any-player, effect:mana-spell-gains-permanent-keyword. Proving cards: Overabundance, Barbflare Gremlin, Winter's Night, Regal Behemoth, Gauntlet of Power, Desolation, Crime Novelist, Hall of the Bandit Lord.

**More to spend mana only on (2026-11-03).** `ManaSpendPurpose` gains `"foretell"`, `"cumulative-upkeep"` (an `unless` paying `times` the source's age counters is cumulative upkeep's, `UnlessOption.paysFor`, so the 24 written out count as the helper's do) and `"cost-with-c"`, as `"cost-with-x"` is; `ManaSpendOnly.orSpell` is a second filter beside a chosen type. A triggered mana ability's `add-mana` keeps its `spendOnly` (`ManaExtraUnit.tag`), and `mayAdd` makes it the player's: two offers by hand, and paying a cost, taken only when that payment can spend it. Throne of Makindi needed nothing new. Keys: cost:spend-only-chosen-type-or-filter, cost:spend-only-foretell, mana:spend-only-colorless-or-c-cost, mana:spend-only-cumulative-upkeep, mana:spend-only-kicked-spells. Proving cards: Kolvori, God of Kinship // The Ringhart Crest, Karfell Harbinger, Cultivator Drone, Adarkar Unicorn, Snowfall, Throne of Makindi.
**A control change whose recipient is computed (2026-11-03).** Seats (`player-to-your-right`), `for-each-player` + `whoIf` for "the player who controls the most" (with `of-player` `a-player`), `trigger-recipient`, a `choose-permanents` by `trigger-controller` narrowed by `controlledByPlayer`, `distribute-control` for a different opponent per permanent; and `entersUnderChosenPlayer` / `flicker.under` for entering or returning under an opponent. Keys: effect:control-to-adjacent-seat, effect:distribute-control-among-opponents, effect:gain-control-chosen-by-trigger-controller, effect:gain-control-to-player-with-most-wizards, effect:gain-control-trigger-recipient, effect:enters-under-opponent-control. Proving cards: Hithlain Rope, Bucknard's Everfull Purse, Thoughtbound Primoc, Charisma, Turf War, Dack Fayden, Helping Hand, Xantcha, Sleeper Agent, Captive Audience, Pendant of Prosperity, Sol'Kanar the Tainted, Zuko, Conflicted.

**A choice by a player other than the controller (2026-11-03).** `targetsChosenBy: "active-player"` and `each-player-may` were there already: `opponent-compared` gained `comparedTo: "active-player"`, a comparison from that player that holds as it resolves. A targeted player's choice from hand is a `for-each-player` of `{ target }` over a `look-and-choose`. Keys: decision:active-player-chooses-target, target:opponent-comparison-chosen-by-active-player, effect:target-player-puts-cards-from-hand-on-library (Head Games and Jester's Mask still need a "search for that many" of a count put on the library). Proving cards: Magus of the Abyss, Oath of Druids, Oath of Ghouls, Oath of Lieges, Oath of Mages, Oath of Scholars, Stunted Growth, Prying Questions.

**How one player's state compares with another's (2026-11-03).** No new condition: `of-player` and `player-compare` already said all of it, composed. An `enters-battlefield` trigger's "that player" is now who the permanent entered under (`refsAtTrigger`), which was the one real gap. Keys: condition:defender-no-opponent-more-life, condition:that-player-controls-more, condition:controls-more-than-each-other-player, condition:enchanted-controller-controls-no-other-creatures, condition:target-controller-controls-no-other-creatures. Proving cards: Guild Artisan, Agent of the Shadow Thieves, Sword Coast Sailor, Archaeomancer's Map, Unified Will, Peer Pressure, Predator's Gambit, Skulking Killer. The scaffolder reads a Background's quoted trigger, "attacks a player" and "you create".

**What has happened to a player, and counts of distinct things (2026-11-03).** `TurnHistory`'s `combat-damaged-by` (each source as it dealt combat damage to that player, `asDealt`, read by `turn-history`, `selfOnly` for "it") and the `attacked-you-last-turn` condition, on the tracking Weathered Sentinels already had. `DistinctOf`'s `"card-type"` and `"counter-kind"`, an aggregate condition's `countsSelf` and `compare: "prime"`; `cards-in-graveyard`'s `excludeSelf`; `controls`' `sharingAName` beside `distinctNames`, which now reads every name a permanent has (rule 201.2), as `nameDiffersFromEach` now does (a Room card in a library has both doors' names). Avatar of Woe's and Maze's End's conditions were already said. Keys: condition:combat-damage-by-filter-this-turn, condition:player-attacked-you-last-turn, condition:card-types-in-graveyard-threshold, condition:counter-kinds-among-permanents (Bribe Taker still needs its "for each kind" choice), condition:cast-restriction-excludes-self, condition:condition-cards-in-all-graveyards, condition:prime-count, condition:condition-lands-same-name, condition:control-distinct-names-count. Proving cards: Lost Monarch of Ifnir, Blitzball, Wave of Rats, Estinien Varlineau, Avenge, O-Kagachi, Omnivorous Flytrap, Hundred-Battle Veteran, Avatar of Woe, Proft, Zimone, Endless Atlas, Sceptre of Eternal Glory, Chrome Replicator, Maze's End, Central Elevator // Promising Stairs.
**An effect's block, and a block's other creature (2026-11-03).** `become-blocked` (`target` or `filter`): blocked with nothing blocking it (rule 509.1h), trample through (702.19e), `becomes-blocked` only for one that was unblocked (509.3c), never `blocked-by` (509.3d). The damage and the triggers already read `blocked` rather than `blockedBy`. `trigger:blocks-creature-trigger` was the pool's vocabulary already (Wooden Stake's `object: "blocked"` and `whileCondition`, a `blocked-by`, `grantsTriggered`), so it took only proving cards and the scaffolder. Keys: effect:becomes-blocked, trigger:blocks-creature-trigger. Proving cards: Fog Patch, Choking Vines, Trap Runner, High-Rise Sawjack, Barrow-Blade, Mirror Shield. The scaffolder also reads "Cast this spell only during the declare blockers step" and "during combat after blockers are declared" now.

**A creature's record of combat, read as a filter (2026-11-03).** `attackedThisCombat` (`lastAttack`, and `blockedThisTurn`'s `thisCombat` for the block), `attackedYouThisTurn` (`attackedPlayersThisTurn`) and `attackedDuringControllersLastTurn` (`GameObject.attackedOnTurns`, a new record pruned as each turn begins and part of the token fold key, with `PlayerState.turnTakenBefore` for "last" on their own turn, `controllersLastTurn`). All three read a left object as it last was (`LastKnownInfo`). `turn-structure`'s `afterCombat` is rule 506.8d. "A creature is attacking you" needed no engine: `controls`'s `anyController` over `attackingYou`. Clockwork's "up to X, no more than seven" is `choose-value`'s `max` with `leastOf`. Keys: condition:attacked-or-blocked-this-combat, filter:attacked-during-controllers-last-turn, condition:attached-attacked-during-controllers-last-turn, condition:creature-attacking-you, filter:attacking-you, filter:attacked-you-this-turn. Proving cards: Kjeldoran Home Guard, Daredevil Dragster, Clockwork Beast, Halls of Mist, Tangle Kelp, Jabari's Influence, Heroic Return, Watchdog.

**Reselecting what an attacker attacks (2026-11-03).** `reselect-attack` (rule 508.7) reuses the `enter-attacking` decision and the client's attack bar: `EnterAttackingChoice.reselect`, with what it's attacking first so that staying is an answer, and an `attack-reselected` event instead of `entered-attacking`. The client also now lights an ability on another player's permanent when this seat is offered it (Capricopian, Excavation): `tileFor` only did so on the seat's own board. Key: effect:reselect-attack-target. Proving cards: Misleading Signpost, Windshaper Planetar, Capricopian. Portal Mage is Misleading Signpost's text and waits for `author-freed`. Still open: Portal Manipulator's "choose target player and any number of target attacking creatures their opponents control. Those creatures are now attacking that player", with no choice and a target slot read from another.

**Libraries that can't be searched, and a floor under a life total as a static (2026-11-03).** `StaticAbility.cantSearchLibraries` and `player-effect`'s, read by `playerCantSearchLibrary` in `beginLibrarySearch`, the one way into a search; the `ignore-static-effect` special action (rule 116.2d) with a menu item in the client; `GameState.resolvingController`, for who caused a search. `StaticAbility.damageLifeFloor`, read with the effect's by `playerDamageLifeFloor`. Mindlock Orb, Stranglehold, Ashiok, Dream Render, Shadow of Doubt, Leonin Arbiter; Ali from Cairo, Worship, Elderscale Wurm.

**"This effect doesn't remove" with colours and for every Aura (2026-11-03).** `protection`'s `doesntRemoveSource` read colours already; `doesntRemoveAuras` (rule 702.16n's "all Auras", a `sparing` instance of `aura: "all"`) and `colorsAmong` (layer 5's colours of a filter's permanents, read by protection in layer 6) are new. White Ward, Spectra Ward, Pledge of Loyalty, Cho-Manno's Blessing.
**A cost to attack paid in permanents (2026-11-03).** `combatTax.permanents` (`CombatPermanentTax`, rule 508.1h): sacrifice or return `count` of what matches `filter`, a creature at a time, added up per kind. The `declare-attackers` offer carries each in `attackTax.perAttacker[…].permanents`, `withinAttackTax` keeps a driver from sacrificing its last three, `GameState.pendingAttackCost` holds the choices (the existing `sacrifice` and `choose-permanents` decisions), and all of it is paid after the mana (508.1j). Keys: `cost:attack-cost-sacrifice`, `cost:attack-cost-return-permanent`. Proving cards: Exalted Dragon, Leviathan, Reclamation, Floodtide Serpent.

**Two typecyclings, and flashback that taps or discards (2026-11-03).** `moreCycling` beside `cycling`, each `name`d: a card with several is activated, each on its own, through `handGrantedActivated`, never the plain `cycle` action. `flashback.tapCreatures` reuses the cast offer's `tapCost`, and `flashback.discard` takes `"x"`, announced for the cast. Keys: `keyword:multiple-typecycling`, `cost:flashback-tap-creatures-cost`, `cost:flashback-discard-cost`. Proving cards: Valley Rannet, Igneous Pouncer, Battle Screech, Group Project, Twinned Vision, Conflagrate.

**A sacrifice attributed to its ability (2026-11-03).** The `activates-ability` trigger's `sacrificedToActivate`, read off the `ability-activated` event: the ability's own cost, so never a Treasure sacrificed for the mana. `sacrifice: "granter"` from a token's grant needed nothing new. Keys: `condition:trigger-ability-sacrificed-to-activate`, `cost:sacrifice-granting-source`. Proving cards: Ashnod the Uncaring, Toggo, Goblin Weaponsmith (the Rock token).
**Exile instead, for a turn or with something besides (2026-11-03).** `player-effect`'s `graveyardExile` (`filter`, `from`, `returnAtEndStep`) is a `GraveyardExileReplacement` a resolved spell made, read live; `playLandsFromGraveyard` beside `castFromGraveyard`; `GraveyardExileReplacement.then`; `self` with `from: "battlefield"`. Proving cards: Malicious Eclipse, Malicious Malfunction, Gaea's Will, Magus of the Will, Cosmic Intervention, Wildwood Escort, Kumano's Blessing, Head of the Hunt, Kalitas, Traitor of Ghet.

**Who creates a token (2026-11-03).** Nothing to record: a token enters under the player who creates it (rule 111.2), so "whenever you create" is an `enters-battlefield` of a token under you, with `firstCreationEachTurn` for the first time each turn. `player-effect`'s `tokensComeToOwner` changes who creates it, ahead of every other replacement. Proving cards: Crafty Cutpurse, Mirkwood Bats, Akim, the Soaring Wind.

**Limits on drawing, and the next draw replaced (2026-11-03).** `prohibits`'s `drawsPerTurn` (1 for Narset, Parter of Veils and Spirit of the Labyrinth, 0 for "players can't draw cards"), read from `cardsDrawnThisTurn`, ahead of every replacement of the draw. `player-effect`'s `nextDrawInstead` (`{ effect, text }`), with its choice among several through `choose-modes`, as dredge's is; Mangara's Tome's and Parallel Thoughts' exiled pile is still `replacement:next-draw-from-exiled-pile`. Proving cards: Narset, Parter of Veils, Spirit of the Labyrinth, Maralen of the Mornsong, Mornsong Aria, Words of Worship, Words of Wilding, Words of War, Words of Waste.

**Pawprint modes (2026-11-03).** `CastModalSpec.pawprintBudget` and `ModeOption.pawprints` (rule 700.2i): the offer lists every multiset within the budget of the modes with legal targets as `castModal.modeSets` (`budgetedModeSets`), the same field spree uses, so the bots, the fuzzer and the client take it with no new decision; `whyCannotChooseCastModes` refuses more. The client's mode prompt counts a set's copies now, and shows "up to 5 {P} worth of modes (n/5)". Season of the Burrow, Season of Loss; the scaffolder reads the head line and the "{P}{P} —" lines.

**Friend or foe (2026-11-03).** A `vote` with `choose`, `who: "you"` and `for: { options: ["friend", "foe"], about: "each-player" }` asks the caster one existing `choose-modes` per player as the spell resolves, starting with the caster, and each answer is a `VoteChoice` with `about`. The scopes `"each-friend"` and `"each-foe"` read every answer the resolution has, so any effect taking a `PlayerScope` reads them, and a later `vote` doesn't hide them. No new decision. Pir's Whim, Virtus's Maneuver, Khorvath's Fury; the scaffolder reads "Each friend …" and "Each foe …" as each player's sentences with the scope swapped.

**Paradigm (2026-11-03).** Rule 702.192a: `{ kind: "paradigm" }` closes the spell's effect, with `exileOnResolve` on the card. The first spell with a name its controller controls to resolve (`PlayerState.paradigmResolved`) gives them an emblem, as epic does, whose precombat main trigger is a `cast-now` with `copyOfCardNamed`: a copy made in exile by name, so the exiled card needn't stay there (the ruling), cast free or left to cease to exist. A `cast-now` decision, nothing new. Decorum Dissertation, Germination Practicum, Restoration Seminar; the scaffolder reads the keyword line.

**Lands with an Adventure (2026-11-03).** Rule 715 and the Town rulings: from hand the land is played or its Adventure cast, as a modal land already was; once the Adventure has exiled it (`onAdventure`), its owner gets a `play-land` from exile, at sorcery timing with a land drop (`landPlayableReason`), never a cast. Midgar, City of Mako // Reactor Raid, Lindblum, Industrial Regency // Mage Siege. Nothing new for the scaffolder: `adventure` layouts already read each face.

**Exiled face down from a hand, foretold, and looks at face-down creatures (2026-11-03).** `look-and-choose` from a hand already reached `exile-face-down`: its new `faceDownLooks` says who may look (406.3). `become-foretold` (702.143d) makes a just-exiled card foretold, from a hand or the battlefield, with `costReducedBy` a given cost beside any printed (`via: "foretell-given"`), on the mana cost of the face cast; `GameObject.foretoldReducedBy` replaces `foretoldCost`, so Dream Devourer's grant reads a modal card's faces too. `look-at-face-down` is a one-time look they close (708.5, Smoke Teller's ruling), shown through `viewFor` only while it's open. Keys: `effect:exile-from-hand-face-down-linked`, `effect:exile-becomes-foretold`, `effect:look-at-face-down-permanents`. Proving cards: Gustha's Scepter, Bane Alley Broker, Summoner's Egg, The Foretold Soldier, Ethereal Valkyrie, Smoke Teller, Revealing Wind, Spy Network.

**What a source reads off the cards still exiled with it (2026-11-03).** The `exiled-with-source` condition's `now` and `sameNameWith: "exiled-with-source"` already existed; new are `cardTypes` (the card types among them) and that a card exiled face down has no name to share (406.3a). Tried first on real cards: the keys needed the reads proved and the scaffolder taught more than new machinery. Keys: `filter:same-name-as-exiled-with-source`, `condition:cards-currently-exiled-with-source-condition`, `condition:linked-exile-currently-exiled` (not Eater of Virtue's and Death-Mask Duplicant's keywords of exiled cards, which still need a narrow key), `condition:card-types-among-exiled-with-source`. Proving cards: Keen-Eyed Curator, Veteran Survivor, Smirking Spelljacker, Ugin's Labyrinth, Circle of Confinement, Circu, Dimir Lobotomist, Mindreaver.

**One instruction, one event: simultaneous zone moves (2026-11-03).** `choicesHeld` entries leave graveyards in one move (`enterHeldEntries`) and a held discard no longer drops them; a search's `alongExiledThisWay` (`enterAlong`) enters what the resolution exiled with the find; `castModal.simultaneous` makes a modal spell's modes one instruction. Exhume, Crypt Champion, Tempting Wurm, Sword of Hearth and Home, Call Damage Control.

**Done as it enters: exile from a graveyard, return a permanent (2026-11-03).** `AsEntersAction` (`Game.askAsEnters`): chosen before it moves on any entry, the cards linked to it as it enters (rule 607.2a), `"done-as-enters"` counters, `counterForEach`, `{ exiledWithThis }`. Rescuer Sphinx, Living Lore, Sutured Ghoul, Dermotaxi, Mimeoplasm, Revered One, Frankenstein's Monster.

**Damage dealt this turn by what and to what (2026-11-03).** The turn's `damageDealt` records the source's name and types and the recipient and its stint; `{ damageDealtThisTurn }`'s `to` and `by`, the condition's `toSelf`, `choose-value`'s `"spell-damage"`, `choose-opponent`'s `whoIf`. Burning-Eye Zubera, Rushing-Tide Zubera, Blazing Effigy, Reverse Polarity, Backdraft.

**A delayed ability's "that player", and what a turn began with (2026-11-04).** The filter clause `controlledByAsSetUp` (bound to `controllerIn` as a step-timed delayed ability is set up, `bindSetUpPlayers`), `controlledSinceTurnBegan` (`TurnState.beganAtSeq` against `controlSinceSeq`), the condition `those-attackers-at-you` (that declaration, not the turn). Siren's Call, Firemane Commando.

**Combat triggers: which defender, how many, once for the lot (2026-11-04).** `attacks`' `attackedPlayerOrPlaneswalker` and `attackedMonarch`, `attacks-alone`'s `defender`, `attack-with`'s `eachOf` and `attackingYouDirectly`, `becomes-blocked`'s `blockers` and `attackedPlayer`, `blocks`' `blocking`, `become-blocked-batch` (`attackers-blocked`). Genestealer Locus, Gahiji, M'Baku, The Spear of Bashenga, Yuriko, Jiang Yanggu, Fearless Swashbuckler, Everett K. Ross, Firemane Commando, Aurelia, Seifer, Serra Inquisitors, Hezrou // Demonic Stench. Left open in the family: Squall (intervening if over those attackers and a chosen number), Arthur (`includingThis` exists; put attacking, then back at end of combat), Tide of War (`one-or-more-creatures-block`), Suppressor Skyguard, Herald of Anafenza, Tenuous Truce, Aegis of the Legion, Party Dude, Icewrought Sentry.

**The turn's attackers as they were declared, and six more tallies (2026-11-04).** `TurnHistoryKind` gains `"attackers"` (each creature-stint once, `asDeclared`, rule 508.1; never one put onto the battlefield attacking, 508.4), `"turned-face-up"` and `"regenerated"` (the amount's new `selfOnly`); `TurnStat` gains `"surveilled"`, `"own-library-searched"` and `"cards-put-into-exile"`; the filter clause `turnedFaceUpThisTurn`; `impulse-exile`'s `sourceDid: "attacked-with-a-token"`. `entered`'s and `sacrificed`'s existing lists already answered "as they entered", "tokens you created" (rule 111.2) and "sacrificed an artifact". Deepway Navigator, Avengers Assemble!, Thaumaton Torpedo, Minas Tirith, Neyali, Hobgoblin Bandit Lord, Oblivious Bookworm, Kaust, Suspicious Detonation, Darkblade Agent, Archive Trap, Ennis, Ellyn Harbreeze, Spiny Starfish. Left open: Excess's creatures that dealt combat damage, Case of the Burning Masks' distinct sources, Command the Stage's last turn, Moraug's attack counts; Ojer Taq and Cloudspire Coordinator wait on other keys.

**Facts fixed as a spell is cast (2026-11-04).** `CardDefinition.asCast` (rule 601.2i), `GameObject.asCastFacts`, the condition `as-cast` and the amount `asCast`; a copy has none (rule 707.10). `condition:controlled-as-cast`, `effect:amount-counted-as-cast`; `condition:cast-timing-condition` and `condition:if-kicked-enters-with-counters` proved on what was already there (`BEFORE_ATTACKERS_ANY_TURN`, `self-kicked` over a `trample` counter and a keyword). Steer Clear, Faerie Fencing, Flame Discharge, Lethal Exploit, Siren's Call, Gnarlid Colony, Voidpouncer. Rapid Fire still waits on `effect:grant-rampage-if-lacking`, and its "before blockers" would be the same `any` one step later.

**Removed from combat, and what comes of it (2026-11-03).** `additional-combat`'s `forcedAttackers` (`AddedPhase.forced`, `boundThisCombat`); `remove-from-combat`'s `unblockSoleBlocked` (`lostBlockerIn`) and `thenMayBlock` (`block-attacker`); the filter clause `controlledByDefendingPlayer`. Illusionist's Gambit, False Orders.

**Untargeted picks from graveyards and exile (2026-11-04).** No new decision: `look-and-choose` over `"graveyards"`, a named player's graveyard or exile, and held across a `for-each-player` (Cemetery Gatekeeper, Cemetery Desecrator, Dreams of Steel and Oil, Breach the Multiverse, The Deck of Many Things, Binding Negotiation, Sengir Nosferatu's Bat); `cast-now`'s `"opponents-graveyards"` and `onePerGraveyard` (Jetsam); a signed `roll-dice` `modifier`; half of what a player controls through `choose-permanents` (Split the Party) and `sacrifice` (Vona's Hunger); a land played as a trigger's object and player. `choose-from-batch` stays open, and Mirror of Fate needs a `then` that runs with nothing chosen.

**For each object, pay or else (2026-11-04).** `for-each-unless` (permanents, a hand's cards, the top of a library; who answers; held to the end), `unless`'s `aboutObject`, the `lifePaymentsOf` amount. Killing Wave, Fade Away, Cut the Tethers, Sirocco, Sword-Point Diplomacy, Moonlight Bargain.

**Exact counts and one per filter in `discard-chosen` (2026-11-04).** `picks` without `reveals`, `oneEach` (a `one-each` set rule). Mind Warp, Abandon Hope, Gruesome Discovery, Last Rites, Distended Mindbender.

**Return X as a cost (2026-11-04).** `additionalCost.returnToHandX`: X announced as it's cast, capped by what matches, paid as `also.returnToHand`. Infernal Harvest.

**One untargeted permanent or card per player (2026-11-04).** `vote` with `choose` and `for.permanent.about` ("for each player, you choose") or `who` and `own` ("each opponent chooses"), then `for-each-voted`: Fell Beast's Shriek, Ultimate Magic: Meteor, Krenko's Buzzcrusher, Winnowing (`sacrifice-all` of one named player, `not: { sharesCreatureTypeWith: 0 }`). Held entries take `enterAs` (Ghouls' Night Out). The Day of the Doctor's IV reads the same way; its I-III still want a while-source play permission from `reveal-until`.
**Restrictions for a duration: until your next turn, this combat, its controller's next turn (2026-11-04).** `restrict`'s `duration` on an `asItResolves` filter (Taunt from the Rampart); `PtDuration`'s `"until-end-of-combat"` (Ma Chao, Yuan Shao's Infantry, Flummoxed Cyclops, Battering Ram); `restrict`'s `"its-controllers-next-turn"` and `PtModifier.restrictionsDuringTurnOf` (Wall of Dust); and, on what was already there, Linvala, Shield of Sea Gate, Edifice of Authority and Dovin Baan. `test/restrict-durations.test.ts`.

**A goad whose end is a condition (2026-11-04).** `goad`'s `duration` (any `PtDuration`: `PtModifier.goadedBy`, read by `goadersOf`) and trigger-object target; `"while-its-controller-controls-source"`; `ungoad`; a goad granted as a copy exception (`goadingStatics` reads `grantedStatics`); `restrict`'s `cantAttackYou` and `cantBlockYours`; the `players-lost` condition; `cantBecomeSuspected`. The War Games, Hot Pursuit, Vislor Turlough, Immortal Obligation, Kros, Serene Sleuth, Mocking Doppelganger, Bloodthirsty Blade, Airtight Alibi. `test/goad-durations.test.ts`.

**The countered spell's controller casts what's turned up (2026-11-05).** Nearly all there already: `cast-now`'s `by`, `reveal-until`'s `whose`, a random `modal`, `not: { sameNameAsTarget }`. New: `nameForMatching` reads a targeted spell gone from the stack by its `lastOnStack.cardName`, a modal double-faced card's back face being a name it hasn't in the graveyard. Tibalt's Trickery. `test/tibalts-trickery.test.ts`.

**What an opponent's search comes to (2026-11-05).** The statics `controlsOpponentsSearching` (a `PlayerControl` with `during: "search"`, from the search's choice raised to answered, placed by `createdAt` against other controls so rule 723.1a holds), `exilesOpponentsFinds` (finds exiled whatever the search said, its `then` still running with nothing found and no "this way" read seeing them, `GameState.unseenFinds`, the permission `impulseGrant`; two players' Agents: `searchExileChoice`, a modal per find, rule 616.1) and `opponentsSearchTop` (`librarySearchTop`). `search-library`'s `enterUnder`. `allow-cast-from-exile`'s `plays` and `to`. Opposition Agent, Aven Mindcensor, Yavimaya Dryad. `test/search-control.test.ts`.

**Addendum (2026-11-05).** No engine change: the pool's shape (`conditional` on "the source was cast, your turn, a main phase") already answered it, a copy having never been cast. Unbreakable Formation, and the scaffolder now reads the line. `test/addendum.test.ts`.

**Hexproof from a quality for a while (2026-11-05).** `grant-protection`'s `asHexproof` (`PtModifier.hexproofFrom`, in layer 6, gone with a later loss of hexproof) and `grant-player-hexproof`'s `from` (`GameState.hexproofFromPlayers`, `playerHexproofFromBlocks`). "Spells you control can't be countered this turn" was already `grant-spells-this-turn` without `next`, which reaches spells on the stack (rule 611.2c). Veil of Summer, Mondo Gecko. `test/hexproof-from-grant.test.ts`.

**Become-copy for a duration read as a condition (2026-11-04).** `become-copy`'s `until`: `"while-source"` (Secret Invasion), `"while-source-attached"` (Blade of Shared Souls, on the new `becomes-attached` trigger), `{ whileTapped }` (Zygon Infiltrator), `CopyEnds.whileSource` and `WhileSource.attachedTo`; `of: "sacrificed"` (The Ever-Changing 'Dane); `ofCard: "when-a-card"` (Taskmaster, Mercenary Mimic); a card gone from the slot does nothing (The Animus); chosen as it resolves with `choose-permanents`' `keepTargets` (Cytoshape, Polymorphous Rush). Left: Assimilation Aegis (a choice among cards exiled with it), Metamorphic Alteration (a static copy that follows the Aura), Shapeshifter's Marrow, Ludevic. `test/become-copy-durations.test.ts`.

**Beginning the game on the battlefield, with more to it (2026-11-04).** `mechanic:opening-hand-not-starting-player`: `beginsOnBattlefield`'s object form (`notStartingPlayer`, `counters`, `thenExileFromHand` — Gemstone Caverns, its only card); and `BoardManaList`'s `ifCondition`, a mana ability's *type* depending on a condition as an `ifCondition` amount makes its number. `test/gemstone-caverns.test.ts`.

**Cards drawn this turn (2026-11-04).** `stat:drawn-this-turn-choice`: `GameObject.drawnOnTurn` and `CardFilter.drawnThisTurn` (only a real draw, gone with any move); the `may`'s `aboutTarget`, naming the card a per-card question is about; the `look-and-choose`'s `prompt` and `givesUp`. Sylvan Library, its only card. `test/sylvan-library.test.ts`.

**Mana that gives what it's spent on something (2026-11-04).** `stat:mana-spent-grants-haste`: nothing new in the engine. `spellGains` and `exertSelf` already did Arena of Glory, and `whenSpent` with `enters-with-counters` and `grant-keyword` on the spell (slot 0) did Jade Orb of Dragonkind. The scaffolder reads "if any of that mana …" and "… until end of turn". `test/mana-spent-rider.test.ts`.

**"The next spell you cast this turn can't be countered" from a land (2026-11-04).** `static:grant-uncounterable`: nothing new; it is Overmaster's `next` `grant-spells-this-turn` with no filter. Mistrise Village. The scaffolder now reads a check land's "enters tapped unless you control a Mountain or a Forest". `test/mistrise-village.test.ts`.

**A cast as something resolves, on its own terms (2026-11-04).** `cast-now` takes `byPaying` (a fixed alternative mana cost), `byPayingLife`, `alsoPaying`, `spendAs`, `mustCast` ("if able", within rules 118.3c and 118.8c, and only with a whole legal cast to be held to: `Game.castNowForced`), and `from: { graveyardOf } | { zones } | { exiledOwnedWithCounter }`; `thisWay: "cast"` reads back what it cast; `put-exiled-this-way-onto-battlefield` takes a `filter`. Blue Mage's Cane, Bismuth Mindrender, Anrakyr the Traveller, Ogre Battlecaster, Tinybones the Pickpocket, Quistis Trepe, Sorcerous Squall, Zethi Arcane Blademaster, Omen Machine, Spelltwine.

**Escape as a grant, what an escape carries, and one-shot graveyard casts (2026-11-04).** `grant-graveyard-keyword`'s `keyword: "escape"` with `exileCount`; `escape.counterChoice`; `escape.exileLinked` beside a `castVia: "escape"` static; `grant-graveyard-cast`'s `free`, `exileAfter`, `filter`; `cast-now` `from: "milled-this-way"`. Desdemona Freedom's Edge, The Grim Captain's Locker, Confession Dial, Tizerus Charger, Skyway Robber, Sins of the Past, Case of the Uneaten Feast, Jace's Mindseeker.

**Activated abilities granted until a card is cast, and by copy (2026-11-04).** `PtDuration`'s `"until-source-cast"` (`PtModifier.untilCast`, `endUntilCastEffects`), `landUntilCastFromExile` for the cycle; `grant-activated-of`. Glamorous Outlaw, Masked Bandits, Shattered Seraph, Quicksilver Elemental; Jiang Yanggu Wildcrafter and Leyline Immersion, which needed no new code.
**Mana in any combination, the player's split every way it's made (2026-11-04).** `effect:mana-any-combination-choice`: a printed amount past the list's budget gets `manaSplit` as a live one has (Cascading Cataracts); a `tapped-for-mana` `oneOf` over 1 is one pick a unit, offered once a combination (Market Festival, and Dawn's Reflection when authored); `greatestInZone`'s `strictly` for Selvala, Heart of the Wilds' draw. `test/mana-any-combination.test.ts`.

**Mana and the chosen color (2026-11-04).** `mana:spend-only-chosen-color`, `trigger:mana-trigger-chosen-color`: `spendOnly`'s `chosenColor`, the cost's `spendOnlyChosenColor` (Throne of Eldraine), and a `tapped-for-mana` trigger's `anyAbility`, any ability of the permanent causing the mana (Caged Sun). `test/mana-chosen-color.test.ts`.

**"This way" across a `repeat` (2026-11-04).** `condition:this-way-across-repeat-passes`: a `this-way` condition's `acrossPasses`, read from `GameState.repeatSince` rather than the pass (and its `atLeast: "x"`): Tainted Pact, Helm of Obedience. `test/repeat-across-passes.test.ts`.

**Library searches together (2026-11-04).** `effect:each-player-may-held-search`, `effect:each-player-search`: an `each-player-may` over a bare `search-library` asks first, then everyone who said yes searches with the finds held in their libraries, and it all enters at once, shuffling after (rule 101.4); a `for-each-player`'s `choicesHeld` over a `search-library` of `who: "that-player"` is "each player searches"; `search-library`'s `shufflesLater` and a `shuffle-library` of `searchers` are "then each player who searched a library this way shuffles". Tempt with Discovery, Field of Ruin, Veteran Explorer. `test/each-player-search.test.ts`.

**Harness and ∞ (2026-11-04).** `mechanic:harness-infinity`: the `harness` effect, the `GameObject.harnessed` designation and filter clause (rule 701.64), and the `infinity()` helper, a static on the card with the ability under that condition (rule 702.186b): The Soul Stone, The Mind Stone. `test/harness-infinity.test.ts`.

**Every graveyard onto the battlefield (2026-11-04).** `effect:mass-graveyard-to-battlefield` was already `return-from-graveyard`'s `all-graveyards` and `each-graveyard`, so no engine change: Rise of the Dark Realms, Roar of Reclamation, Myr Servitor (`hasName`, an intervening `source-zone`). `test/mass-graveyard-to-battlefield.test.ts`.

**Different names as an intervening if (2026-11-04).** `condition:distinct-names-condition` was already `controls`' `distinctNames`, so no engine change: Field of the Dead, Liliana's Contract. `test/distinct-names-condition.test.ts`.

**Boast twice (2026-11-04).** `static:boast-twice`: the static's `boastsEachOfYourTurns: 2` (Birgi, God of Storytelling // Harnfel, Horn of Bounty), read per boast ability on its controller's own turn. `test/boast-twice.test.ts`.
**Manifest or cloak with a choosable source; piles in exile; a return face down (2026-11-04).** `look-and-choose`'s `destination: "cloak"`, and `"manifest"` or `"cloak"` over any zone, one at a time (701.40e, 701.58e): Scroll of Fate, Vannifar, Hide in Plain Sight. `cloak-exiled-pile` and `choose-permanents`' `thenTogether` (one `then` over everything chosen), the filter `hasDisguise`: Become Anonymous, Expose the Culprit. `exile-into-piles` with `PileFate`'s `{ together, word }` and `cast-now`'s `rest: "hand"`: Hostile Negotiations, Abstract Performance. `flicker`'s `faceDown`: Shorecrasher Elemental. `put-onto-battlefield`'s `ifPermanentCard`: Yarus; a `modal` with `minModes: 0` over a face-up and a face-down return: Deathmist Raptor. Scaffolder taught, "face-down" read in a type phrase. `manifest-chosen-source.test.ts`, `exile-into-piles.test.ts`, `return-face-down.test.ts`. Left: `mechanic:morph-land` (Zoetic Cavern), `replacement:as-turned-face-up-dynamic-counters` (Crowd-Control Warden), `trigger:manifests-dread` (Paranormal Analyst); Phyrexian Portal's two face-down piles and search within a pile still need a key of their own.

**Time counters that reach a suspended card (2026-11-04).** `addCounter` reaches a suspended card for time counters (`addTimeCounterInExile`), the filter `suspended`, `one-of` with `card-in-exile` as the target (Shivan Sand-Mage, Jhoira's Timebug); whileSuspended triggers adding them (Pardic Dragon, Curse of the Cabal); `remove-counter-all`/`add-counter-all`'s `andSuspended` (Dust of Moments); `give-suspend` of `"source"` as a spell resolves (Charnel Serenade); `exile-spell-as-it-resolves`' `timeCounters` (Gandalf of the Secret Fire); `give-suspend`'s `timeCast`, `remove-time-counters-in-exile` and `cast-now`'s `gainsHaste` (Alaundo the Seer). `time-counters-suspended.test.ts`. Left: Amy Pond's effect reads as a `look-and-choose` over `{ suspended: true, ownedBy: "you" }`, but she has two pairing abilities (partner with, Doctor's companion) and `CardDefinition.pairing` holds one.

**A name an effect gives a permanent (2026-11-04).** `StaticAbility.setName` (kept on `GameObject.staticName` by `refreshStaticNames` at each state-based check, as a stored field like layer 2) and `animate`'s `setName` and `addSupertypes` (a modifier that isn't copiable, read by `nameOf` and `effectiveSupertypes`): Witness Protection, Honest Work, Awakening of Vitu-Ghazi. `set-name.test.ts`. `animate`'s `addSupertypes` is `effect:animate-add-supertype`'s "becomes legendary" (Genju of the Realm's shape); left there: Arcum's Weathervane and Thermal Flux's snow on `add-types`, and a supertype lost. Left of the names: The Curse of Fenric's chapter I, Tenth District Hero's granted static on an `animate`.
**An anthem per counter on its source (2026-11-04).** Already there: `grantPtPerCount.countersOnSource`, and an "enters with" count reading `ofChosenType`. Banner of Kinship; the parser reads both lines. `test/enters-chosen-type-counters.test.ts`.

**Proliferating more than once for one (2026-11-04).** The `would-proliferate` replacement (`times`, several multiplying, rule 614.5): `Game.beginProliferate` asks once per time over the board as the last left it, `awaiting.more`, the effect's `then` after the last. A proliferate with nothing to choose now announces `proliferated` too (the rulings). Tekuthal, Inquiry Dominus; `test/proliferate-replacement.test.ts`.

**A target per opponent (2026-11-04).** Nothing new: `whose: { seat }` slots (Sepulchral Primordial, Haytham Kenway) already said it, so `effect:target-per-opponent` was Grasp of Fate alone and the scaffolder's template. Geth's Summons still waits on `condition:per-opponent-target-slot-condition`. `test/target-per-opponent.test.ts`.

**Paying 2 life rather than a coloured pip (2026-11-04).** The static `payLifeForPips`, `withLifeForPips` in `planPayment`, so every mana cost reads it; the player says how many through `phyrexianLife`, now on `cycle`, `suspend`, `plot`, `turn-face-up`, `unlock-door`, `end-licid-effect` and `choose-modes` as well as casts and activations. Life by choice goes first on pips mana couldn't pay. K'rrik, Son of Yawgmoth; `test/pay-life-for-pips.test.ts`. Not the Defilers (an optional additional cost that reduces a pip), and not paying from sources rather than floating mana (`decision:manual-payment-choices`, Mr. House, Ozai).

**A "may" that can't be done, for each player asked (2026-11-04).** `each-player-may`'s `effect` through `mayBeDone` as that player, a player not offered it counting as not doing it; `mayBeDone` learned tap and untap (rule 701.26). Charismatic Conqueror; `test/each-player-may-feasibility.test.ts`.

**Mana that adds counters to what it casts (2026-11-04).** `add-mana`'s `spellEntersWith` (`spell`, `counter`, `amount` read as it enters, each unit its own, spent ahead of plain mana when it reaches the spell), `entersWithCounters`' `amountAsItEnters`, `{ commanderCasts: <slot> }`. Opal Palace, Guildmages' Forum, Animal Attendant, Commander's Insight. Biophagus reads the same way; Jade Orb of Dragonkind still wants its hexproof. `test/mana-spent-enters-with.test.ts`.

**Counters by who puts them, on permanents or players (2026-11-04).** `would-add-counter`'s `whoPuts`, `onPlayers` and `halved` (`counterTotalInBestOrder`, rule 616.1's order worked out); `addCounter`'s `by` read, and given to player counters and energy (infect and toxic: the source's controller; a loyalty plus cost reaches it, Doubling Season's "an effect" still not). Innkeeper's Talent, Vorinclex, Monstrous Raider, Lae'zel, Vlaakith's Champion. `test/counter-replacement-players.test.ts`.

**A live maximum in an intervening if (2026-11-04).** Already there: `controls-greatest` on a trigger's `condition`, asked again on resolution (rule 603.4). Padeem, Consul of Innovation; the parser reads the clause. `test/controls-greatest-intervening.test.ts`.

**A kept creature with a counter, and a restriction while it stays (2026-11-04).** Divine Reckoning's `vote` with `keep`, then `add-counter-all`, `sacrifice-all` and `restrict` over `thisWay: "chosen"`, with `duration: { whileCounter }`; `restrict`'s `cantAttackYou: "you-and-planeswalkers"` (`PtModifier.cantAttackYouCovers`) names no battle. Promise of Loyalty; the parser reads the whole card. `test/vow-counters.test.ts`.

**A creature's own count of attacks, and additional combats placed where they say (2026-11-04).** `grantPtPerCount.ofAffected: "times-attacked-this-turn"` on `GameObject.attacksThisTurn`; `additional-combat`'s `atBeginning` (a delayed ability on that one phase, `{ thatCombat: mark }`) and `afterSecondMain`. One made in the precombat main now comes before the turn's own combat, and combats owed go ahead of a main phase owed (rule 500.8). Moraug, Fury of Akoum; World at War; the parser reads both. `test/additional-combat-that-combat.test.ts`.

**A win on eight with one name (2026-11-04).** Already there: `win-game` behind `controls`' `sharingAName`, and `create-token-copy`'s `of: "enchanted"`. Mechanized Production; the parser reads the whole card, Followed Footsteps' copy too. Halo Fountain's win is there as well, waiting on its `cost:untap-cost`. `test/win-game-same-name.test.ts`.
**Token and counter doublers for every player (2026-11-04).** `anyController` on `would-create-token` and `would-add-counter`. Primal Vigor. `test/replacement-any-controller.test.ts`.

**Equip's timing and its costs (2026-11-04).** `equipAtInstantSpeed` and `alternativeEquipCost` (`Game.withAlternativeEquipCosts`, a second ability beside each equip ability, as `alternativeCyclingCost`), `PlayerState.equipsActivatedThisTurn`. Forge Anew, Leonin Shikari; "Equip—Pay {3} or …" is two entries: Bloodthorn Flail, Gavel of the Righteous. `test/equip-timing-and-costs.test.ts`.

**Tokens that each fight a different creature (2026-11-04).** `created-tokens-fight`: `Game.createdTokensFight` pairs this resolution's tokens with what `among` matches, asking with `choose-permanents` only where tokens differ (`interchangeableKinds`), then `fightAtOnce`. Ezuri's Predation. `test/token-per-object-fights.test.ts`.

**A multiplier of only some tokens (2026-11-04).** `would-create-token`'s `multiplier` with `ofTokens`: in `planTokenEvent`'s search rather than last, since whether it applies can depend on another replacement (Chatterfang's Squirrel from a Treasure). Ojer Taq, Deepest Foundation // Temple of Civilization; the parser reads "twice" and "three times that many of those tokens". `test/token-multiplier-filter.test.ts`.

**A player searching their own library, as a trigger (2026-11-04).** `{ on: "searches-library", who }` on the new `library-searched` event, `"trigger-player"` who searched. Archivist of Oghma, Wan Shi Tong, Librarian, Ob Nixilis, Unshackled. River Song still wants `replacement:draw-from-bottom`. `test/search-trigger.test.ts`.

**A player's own turns of the game (2026-11-04).** `condition:own-turn-count`: `PlayerState.turnsTaken`, the `turns-taken` condition, `FIRST_THREE_TURNS` with `your-turn`. Starting Town, Serra Avenger; Spider-Man 2099 and Jace Reawakened read the same line.

**A permanent of each permanent type (2026-11-04).** `effect:keep-one-of-each-permanent-type`: Cataclysm's `choose-one-of-each` already was it, with all six types and `who: "each-opponent"` (Liliana, Dreadhorde General) or the nonland four (Cataclysmic Gearhulk); new is `chosenBy: "you"`, the caster answering for each player (Mythos of Snapdax).

**A target player's counters as an amount (2026-11-04).** `amount:target-player-counters`: `playerCounters`'s `forTarget` reads the player in a target slot. Vraska, Betrayal's Sting.

**"Put them onto the battlefield" (2026-11-04).** `effect:put-arrived-all-tapped`: `put-arrived-onto-battlefield`'s `count: "all"` takes every card a batched trigger fired on still there, with no choice. Hedge Shredder.

**A turn's triggered mana ability (2026-11-04).** `effect:turn-scoped-mana-trigger`: a `delayed-trigger` with `whenever: { on: "tapped-for-mana" }` and an `add-mana` effect is a triggered mana ability (rule 605.1b): `Game.tappedForManaExtras` reads it with the permanents', and the watchers leave it alone. High Tide, Bubbling Muck, Pyre Rhymer with Molten Tide.

**The greatest among cards discarded this way (2026-11-04).** `effect:greatest-among-discarded`, no engine code: `{ thisWay: "discarded", perPlayer: "greatest" }` (Jace's Archivist, Windfall's), `greatestOf: "mana-value"`, read as a reflexive ability triggers through `value` (Ill-Timed Explosion), and compared with through a `this-way` condition's filter, `manaValue: { op: "eq", n: { amount } }`, per player (Scythe Specter, its ties included). Not authored: Cait, Cage Brawler, whose "you and defending player each draw a card, then discard a card" wants those two discards simultaneous (no scope names the pair). Jace's Archivist, Scythe Specter, Ill-Timed Explosion.

**Gaining control of a spell (2026-11-04).** `effect:gain-control-spell`: the effect `gain-control-spell`, then `change-targets`' `"choose-new"`, whose targets are then judged from the new controller's side (rule 115.7d). The spell keeps who put it on the stack (`GameObject.putOnStackBy`, read through `spellCaster`) and each control effect (`controlEffects`): a spell countered (`castBy`), "if you cast it" (`entry.cast.by`), the `cast` filter on the stack, "the first spell you cast each turn", a turn's spell grants and rebound's "your hand" all read the caster; the permanent is the thief's with the caster its default controller (110.2b, 800.4a, 800.4c). A grant follows its wording (109.5): "spells you cast" (`castBy: "you"`, `firstEachTurn`, a turn's `next`) stays with the caster, "spells you control" goes with the spell. `{ seat }` slots count from the controller, so a stolen spell's targets, and another player's copy's, move to their players' new slots (`rebindSeatTargets`). No new decision. Not built: the exchange of Sudden Substitution, and the copy's random new targets of Chef's Kiss. Commandeer, Aethersnatch.

**A flicker back and forth between a card's faces (2026-11-04).** `effect:exile-transform-return`, no engine code: `flicker` of the source with `transformed` (rule 712.14a), and without it, front face up (712.14), which a Saga's final chapter does as it stops being a Saga, so it's never sacrificed (714.4). Printed "under its owner's control" is the default; a chapter's bare "return it to the battlefield" is `underYourControl` (110.2a), which only shows on a stolen Saga. The scaffolder reads "Exile ~, then return it to the battlefield transformed under its owner's control" and the "Activate only as a sorcery and only if …" of the two conditions. It doesn't read chapters. Urabrask // The Great Work, Sheoldred // The True Scriptures.

**One creature spell from your graveyard (2026-11-04).** `zone:graveyard-cast-permission-one-use`, no engine code: a `player-effect`'s `castFromGraveyard` with `once` (Whispersteel Dagger's), and the scaffolder's template for the line. Chainer, Nightmare Adept.

**Cards from hand taking {2} off (2026-11-04).** `cost:exile-cards-from-hand-cost-reduction`: `additionalCost.exileFromHandReduces`, named on the cast's `delve` from its offer (`delve.fromHand`), delve's shape with any number allowed past what lowers the cost. The client's delve popup words it. March of Swirling Mist, March of Wretched Sorrow, March of Otherworldly Light (March of Burgeoning Life and March of Reckless Joy wait on their own effects).

**The cards exiled with it, to their graveyards (2026-11-04).** `effect:exiled-with-source-to-graveyard`: `return-exiled-by-source`'s `linked: "graveyard"` now moves them as one event and reaches what it exiled "until it leaves" (`exiledUntil`, rule 610.3); `thisWay: "put-into-graveyard"` counts them. Valakut Exploration, Pinnacle Starcage, Gravegouger (Foreboding Steamboat still waits for its each-player choice's exile).

**A cast using this permanent's own mana (2026-11-04).** `stat:cast-using-mana-from-this-source`, no engine code: a `cast-spell` trigger's `manaFrom: { fromSource: true }` (Barracks of the Thousand's), `discover` of `manaValueOf: "trigger-object"`, and the scaffolder's template for the line. Brass's Tunnel-Grinder // Tecutlan, the Searing Rift.

**The token an ability just made (2026-11-04).** `effect:created-token-reference`, no engine code: `create-token-copy`'s `gains` and `sacrificeAtEndStep`, `of` a graveyard slot with `asCard`; or a `for-each-permanent` over `thisWay: "created"` after a `create-token`. Feldon of the Third Path, Case of the Stashed Skeleton.

**The top card, sometimes (2026-11-04).** `static:look-at-top-card-conditional`, no engine code: a `visibility: { looksAtOwnLibraryTop }` static with a `condition`, which `viewFor` already read. Case of the Locked Hothouse, Ranger Class.

**A target filter read against its object's controller (2026-11-04).** `effect:target-filter-relative-to-controller`: a `DynamicOperand`'s `forItsController`, the amount's `"each"` read as the matched object's controller. Drown in the Loch.

**A cost reduced by the most any one opponent controls (2026-11-04).** `cost:cost-reduction-greatest-opponent-count`: the `{ greatestOpponentCount: CardFilter }` `CostReductionAmount`. Cavern-Hoard Dragon.
**A spell's triggers doubled (2026-11-04).** `static:double-triggers-of-spells`: `doubleTriggersOf`'s `spells`, reaching a spell's own triggers ("when you cast this spell") beside a permanent's. Echoes of Eternity.

**One or more leaving the battlefield, once per event (2026-11-04).** `trigger:leaves-batch-trigger`: `leaves-battlefield`'s `batched`, off a `permanents-left` event once the whole event is done, a commander's deferred move (903.9b) waiting with it. Dour Port-Mage; Nefarious Imp. Aang, Airbending Master has it now and is `author-freed`'s if nothing else blocks it.

**Counters put on a player as a trigger (2026-11-04).** `trigger:counters-put-on-players`: `counters-put`'s `orPlayer`, a player's counters and energy too, once per player for a simultaneous placement (toxic's ruling), `by` now on `player-counters-changed` and `energy-changed`. All Will Be One.

**A spell being countered as a trigger (2026-11-04).** `trigger:counters-a-spell-trigger`: `counters-spell` (who controls what countered it, ward included) and `spell-countered` (who cast it, so never a copy); neither for an ability countered, a spell that can't be, or one that fizzles. Baral, Chief of Compliance; Lullmage Mentor; Multani's Presence.

**A static prevention's follow-up on its recipient (2026-11-04).** `replacement:prevention-follow-up-on-recipient`: a `would-deal-damage` `prevent`'s `then` reads the damage's recipient as `"trigger-recipient"`. Vigor.

**A loss of life replaced (2026-11-04).** `replacement:would-lose-life`: `LifeLossReplacement` (`who`, `multiplier`), asked of every loss in `Game.changeLife`, damage's before its floor. Bloodletter of Aclazotz.

**As this transforms (2026-11-04).** `replacement:as-transforms`: `asTransformsInto` on the face named, an `EffectSpec` applied inside `Game.transformPermanent` before the event (rule 712.20). Sephiroth. It can't ask anything yet: Ludevic and Curse of Leeches choose while transforming.

**One amount against another (2026-11-04).** `condition:intervening-if-amounts`: the `amounts` condition (`left`, `op`, `right`), answered by `ConditionOptions.amounts` from `Game.interveningIfMet` and a resolving `conditional`, read unclamped (`signedAmountValue`). Liberator, Urza's Battlethopter; ED-E, Lonesome Eyebot.

**A spell's own ceiling on X (2026-11-04).** `effect:x-max-player-count`: `CardDefinition.maxX`, a `CostReductionAmount` capping `Game.xPlanFor` as X is chosen (rule 601.2b), and the amount `{ playersInGame: true }`. Open the Way. Soul Immolation (`aggregate`) and Winter's Chill (`countOf`) can say their ceilings the same way, and wait on other things.

**The total power of what died together (2026-11-04).** `effect:batched-dies-total-power`: a batched `dies` trigger's `batchValue: "total-power"`, its `{ triggerValue: true }` their total power as they last existed (rule 603.10a), fixed as it triggers; a `create-token`'s `basePt` no longer clamps (rule 107.1b). The Skullspore Nexus.

**Blocking or blocked by a creature of a type (2026-11-04).** `static:blocking-subtype-condition`: a `controls` condition's filter is now read with its source, so `blockingRelation` works there; `anyController` and `of: "host"` make it Sting, the Glinting Dagger.

**Do this only once each turn (2026-11-04).** `stat:do-this-once-each-turn`, no engine code: `may`'s `oncePerTurn` was already it, and the scaffolder now reads the sentence. Ondu Spiritdancer. Evelyn, the Covetous and Jacob Hauken, Inspector wait on `static:play-from-exile-once-each-turn`, a different limit.

**A spell's target count from an amount (2026-11-04).** `decision:spell-target-count-amount`: `Game.effectiveTargetSpecs` reads a spell's `any-number` bounds other than `"x"` for that cast (`spellSpecsWithAmounts`), the kick count already known (rules 601.2b–c), so each multikicker offer has its own count. Comet Storm, Strength of the Tajuru, Aether Burst. Reap waits on `decision:target-count-from-other-target`.

**Copies of the chosen type's tokens that entered this turn (2026-11-04).** `effect:tokens-entered-this-turn-copies`: no engine code, `create-token-copy`'s `{ each }` over `token`, `enteredThisTurn` and `ofChosenType`. Renewed Solidarity, and a parser template for the line.

**A spell copied an additional time (2026-11-04).** `replacement:copy-spell-additional-time`: the `would-copy-spell` replacement over one instruction to copy, however many copies it makes (`Game.withSpellCopyEvent`, around the `copy-spell` effect, storm and Zada's ordered copies). Its additional copies take new targets, and go where their controller chooses among copies that couldn't (`choose-number`'s `placeCopy`). Twinning Staff.

**A count of each affected creature's own name (2026-11-04).** `static:pt-per-same-name-as-affected`: no engine code, `grantPtPerCount` with `relativeToAffected`, `excludeSelf` and `sameNameAsSource`. Mirror Box, and parser templates for its lines.

**Each nonland permanent all colors (2026-11-04).** `static:all-colors-static`: no engine code, a static's `setColors` over a filter. Leyline of the Guildpact, and parser templates for its lines.

**The cards just exiled, looked at and put back (2026-11-04).** `effect:exile-from-hand-any-number-swap`: `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "exiled-this-way"`, a face-down card picked itself rather than by its group (rule 406.3), in order (rule 401.4). Scroll Rack.

**Attack you and aren't blocked (2026-11-04).** `trigger:attacks-you-unblocked-batch`: `attacks-player` with `unblocked`, off a `player-attacked-unblocked` event per player attacked directly, as blockers are declared. Coveted Jewel.

**Each combat this turn (2026-11-04).** `effect:delayed-trigger-each-combat`, no engine code: `additional-combat` twice with `afterThisPhase`, in a `conditional` on `turn-structure`, and a `delayed-trigger` at a `whenever` of `step-begins`. Full Throttle.

**That extra turn's end step (2026-11-04).** `effect:delayed-trigger-extra-turn-end-step`, no engine code: `take-extra-turn`'s `thatTurn.atEndStep` was there already (Warrior's Oath), and the scaffolder reads the line. Final Fortune, proving it from an instant on another player's turn; Last Chance waits for `author-freed`.

**A graveyard to the bottom, in a random order (2026-11-04).** `effect:graveyard-to-library-bottom-random`: `put-on-libraries`' `what: { graveyardOf }` and `position: "bottom-random"`, with the placement `randomlyAmong` so a commander's deferred move lands at random too. Endurance.

**A discard cost of mana value X (2026-11-04).** `cost:discard-mana-value-x`: `cost.discard.manaValueX`, X announced as it's activated with no `{X}` needed, the ability offered once per X with a card to pay it. Kozilek, the Great Distortion; Knollspine Invocation.

**Annihilator X, and a ward of two sacrificed permanents (2026-11-04).** `keyword:annihilator-x`: `annihilator(amount, text)`. `decision:ward-blight`, no engine code: `ward`'s `sacrifice.count` was there. An entering permanent's "among cards in exile" sees itself coming from exile (`AggregateReads.stillInExile`, as the graveyard's). Ulamog, the Defiler.

**Magecraft and a colour of mana kept (2026-11-04).** `trigger:copy-spell-trigger` and `replacement:mana-persistence-color`, no engine code: `orCopy`, `keepsUnspentMana` and `resolved-this-turn` were there. Ashling, Flame Dancer. The Last Agni Kai's "until end of turn" keeping is still open.

**What a flicker does once it's back (2026-11-04).** `effect:flicker-return-then`: `flicker`'s `returnThen`, run by the delayed return and reading it through `this-way`'s `"put-onto-battlefield"`. Phelia, Exuberant Shepherd; Obzedat, Ghost Council; Hallowed Respite (a plain `sequence`).
**You may roll again (2026-11-04).** `effect:roll-again`: a `roll-dice` table row's `mayRollAgain` (rule 706.3c), a `may` of the same roll after the row, with the number of dice held; `effect-worth` reads a roll as its rows' average, so a bot rolls again for tokens. Delina, Wild Mage.

**A CDA over devotion (2026-11-04).** `bug:cda-devotion`, no engine code: `setBasePtFromCount` reads `{ devotionTo }` in every zone already (Tymaret's shape). Daxos, Blessed by the Sun.

**Each opponent chooses fame or fortune (2026-11-04).** `effect:fame-or-fortune`, no engine code: a `vote` with `choose` over named options, `for-each-vote` per option, and `choose-permanents` of `controlledByPlayer: "that-player"` for what isn't targeted. Seize the Spotlight.

**The chosen type in addition (2026-11-04).** `static:is-chosen-type`, no engine code: `addsChosenCreatureType` beside an `ofChosenType` lord (Metallic Mimic and Morophon's lines), both read by the scaffolder. Adaptive Automaton.
**An instruction, if that player leads each other player (2026-11-04).** `condition:compare-each-other-player`, no engine code: a `conditional` on `of-player` `that-player` over `player-compare` `than: "each-opponent"`, and the scaffolder reads the trailing "X if that player has more … than each other player." under a trigger that names the player. Battle Angels of Tyr.

**A planeswalker that's an Equipment, and "can't be attacked" (2026-11-04).** `mechanic:planeswalker-equipment`: the `cant-be-attacked` `CombatRestriction`, read by `whyCannotAttack` and `canBeAttacked` for the attackers offer, and not by `legalDefenders`, so one already attacked stays and one entering attacking may choose it (rule 508.4c). The rest was there: `attach` from a loyalty ability, a conditional `grantsTriggered`. The Aetherspark.

**A repeated search with one shuffle (2026-11-04).** `effect:repeated-search-one-shuffle`, no engine code: `search-library`'s `shufflesLater` on each, in a `may`, the second in a `conditional`, and a closing `shuffle-library` of `searchers`. Claim Jumper.

**Two target players search (2026-11-04).** `effect:two-players-search`, no engine code: `for-each-target` over `search-library` of `who: { controllerOfTarget: 0 }`, which orders target players by APNAP (rule 101.4). Scheming Symmetry.

**A counter on each creature target player controls (2026-11-04).** `effect:add-counter-all-target-player`, no engine code: `add-counter-all` with `controlledByTarget` (Requisition Raid's shape); the scaffolder now reads the line and "proliferate twice". Contagion Engine.

**A token copy handed to another player, and a trigger about the player its event named (2026-11-04).** `effect:opponent-token-copy`: `create-token-copy`'s `who` takes `"that-player"` under a `for-each-player`, with `goadedForGame`, and `for-each-player` an `except`, its `simultaneous` one token event; `condition:named-artifact-condition`: `whileCondition` reads `"that-player"`, and a draw names its player. Wedding Ring, Fractured Identity, Life of the Party.

**A target with mana value X on an activated ability (2026-11-04).** `bug:target-filter-x-activated`, no engine code: `manaValue: { op: "eq", n: "x" }` on a graveyard target, the ability offered once per X with a target (`targetSpecReadsX`, Ent-Draught Basin's shape); the scaffolder reads The Scarab God's "except it's a 4/4 black Zombie". Lazotep Quarry.

**A doubled trigger, by a land or Bird entering and by a draw (2026-11-04).** `static:trigger-doubling-filter`, no engine code: `doubleTriggers` with an `anyOf` filter (Panharmonicon's shape). `static:trigger-doubler-draw-cause`: `doubleTriggers`' new cause `"draws"`. The scaffolder reads these lines and "you may play lands and cast … spells from the top". Traveling Chocobo, Krang, the All-Powerful.
**Untapping permanents as a cost (2026-11-04).** `cost:untap-cost`: `AbilityCost.untapOthers`, chosen with `choose-permanents` as the cost is paid (rule 601.2h), so a creature tapped for the ability's own mana can be one (Halo Fountain's ruling); a stunned one can't (rule 122.1d), as for `{Q}`. Halo Fountain, Crackleburr.

**A manland with all creature types (2026-11-04).** `effect:all-creature-types-animate`, no engine code: `animate` with `addSubtypes: ["(every creature type)"]`, Faceless Haven's shape, now read by the scaffolder. Mutavault.

**A cost increase except on the caster's turn (2026-11-04).** `cost:cost-increase-not-your-turn`: `costModification.exceptOnCastersTurn`, read against the player casting the spell, so it reaches the Grid's own controller on another turn; reductions still apply to it (its ruling). Defense Grid.

**A hand card up to your land count (2026-11-04).** `condition:hand-filter-land-count`, no engine code: `look-and-choose` from the hand with `manaValue` against `{ amount: { countOf } }`, Loot's and Beseech the Queen's read, on a warped creature's leaves trigger. Anticausal Vestige.

**An optional life cost for a spell's reduction (2026-11-06).** `cost:optional-life-additional-cost-for-spells`: `costModification.optionalLife`, the Defilers' "you may pay 2 life … cost {G} less if you paid life this way" — an optional additional cost (rule 601.2b) announced as a `cast-spell` variant naming the permanents it pays for (`optionalLife`, every set of them its own offer, labelled by `optionalLifeText`), once a spell for each, the reduction only with it paid, payable only with the life (119.4). Defiler of Vigor, Defiler of Dreams.

**A static scoped by toxic (2026-11-06).** `static:static-scoped-by-has-toxic`: a `filter` scope with `hasToxic` waits with the keyword-scoped statics in the layer fold and reads the target's toxic so far (`TargetView.toxic`), so toxic another static, an Aura or an effect grants counts and a creature that lost its abilities has none (rule 613.8). Skrelv's Hive.

**Exiling a target spell plotted (2026-11-06).** `effect:exile-target-spell-plotted`, no engine code: an `exile` of a `spell` target then `become-plotted` of the same slot; the owner casts it free on a later turn, a cost increase still applying (rule 702.170d). Aven Interrupter.

**One play shared among cards (2026-11-07).** `zone:cast-one-from-milled-until-end-of-turn`, `zone:impulse-play-only-one`: `GameState.permissionGroups`, a count of plays shared among the cards one effect gave a permission, named by each card's own (`graveyardCastPermission.group`, `impulse.group`) and spent by any one played under it, whichever zone it's in (a milled card exiled instead, rule 701.17c) — `grant-graveyard-cast`'s `upTo` ("until end of turn, you may cast a spell from among those cards") and `impulse-exile`'s `upTo` ("you may play one of those cards"). Locke, Treasure Hunter; Unlucky Witness.

**A card cast from each player's milled cards (2026-11-07).** `effect:cast-one-per-player-from-milled`, no engine code: `cast-now` `from: "milled-this-way"` with `repeat` and `onePerGraveyard`, read by each card's owner — the player who milled it — the casts made as the trigger resolves (the ruling). The Ur-Sphinx.

**Playing the card milled, on an activated ability (2026-11-07).** `effect:play-milled-card-this-turn`, no engine code: B273's `grant-graveyard-cast` `cards: "milled-this-way"` with `play` (Tablet of Discovery), beside a batched `leaves-graveyard` trigger the play sets off. Ark of Hunger.

**Three cards asked for by name, on what was there (2026-11-10).** No engine code. `trigger:self-put-into-graveyard-from-library`: Narcomoeba's `fromGraveyard` trigger `{ on: "put-into-graveyard", who: "self", from: "library" }`, on Witness the Future's spell (Gaea's Blessing). `effect:trigger-spell-gains-keyword`: a `grant-keyword` to `"trigger-object"` reaches the spell on the stack and rides onto the permanent it becomes (a `castRider`, rule 400.7a — Judith, Carnage Connoisseur's), here from an emblem's cast trigger (Tyvar Kell). `effect:double-life-total`: `set-life-total` to `{ product: [{ lifeTotal: "you" }, 2] }` (rule 701.10d; a negative total doubles too), under `as-player` for another player — Beacon of Immortality's target, or `{ controllerOf: "trigger-object" }` for "its controller's" (Celestial Mantle). The scaffolder reads "Double your life total."
**A "can't" on each player by what that player did this turn (2026-11-10).** `static:prohibit-by-player-turn-history`: `TurnDid` (`{ stat: TurnStat, atLeast? }`) on a static prohibition's `who` (`{ who: "opponents", thisTurn }`, `PlayersWhoDid`) and on a filter (`controllerThisTurn`), each player asked for themselves from the journal, so what they did before the permanent arrived counts. Angelic Arbiter.

**One choice of where the chosen cards go (2026-11-10).** `effect:look-and-choose-destination-choice`, no engine code: `look-and-choose` with `destination: "stay"` and a `then` that is a one-mode `modal` over the chosen cards, each mode a simultaneous `for-each-target` (`put-onto-battlefield`, or `return-to-hand` from the library); "a creature card and/or a land card" is `together.oneEach`. Green Sun's Twilight.
**A Saga chapter that divides an amount (2026-11-10).** `effect:saga-chapter-divided`: `SagaChapter.divided`, a triggered ability's `divided` on a chapter — the chapter ability already went on the stack through the triggered-ability path (`Game.triggerDivided`, rule 603.3d), so the field was all that was missing. Vorinclex // The Grand Evolution (both faces), Revival of the Ancestors.

**A player effect for as long as you control its source (2026-11-10).** `effect:no-max-hand-size-while-you-control-source`: `PlayerDuration` takes `"while-you-control-source"` and `"while-source"`, read into `Expiry`'s `whileSource` as the effect resolves (`EffectContext.whileSourceHere`: nothing if the source has left or isn't yours, rule 611.2b) and ended for good by `Game.endPlayerWhileSource` as it leaves or changes hands — for `player-effect`, `timed-static`, `prohibit` and a game-wide `restrict` alike. Jin-Gitaxias // The Great Synthesis (both faces).

**Welcome to . . . // Jurassic Park (2026-11-10).** `bug:saga-exile-return-transformed`, no engine code: one optional slot per opponent seat (The True Scriptures' shape), each a `become` with `setSubtypes: ["Wall"]` (rule 205.1b: a "[creature type] artifact creature" keeps its other types and non-creature subtypes) lasting `"while-you-control-source"`; `create-token`'s `gainUntilEndOfTurn`; `flicker` `transformed` `underYourControl`; and on the land, Underworld Breach's `grantsToGraveyard` escape narrowed to Dinosaur cards.

**Casting a spell with cascade (2026-11-11).** `trigger:cast-spell-with-cascade`: `cast-spell`'s `spellHas: "cascade"`, read off the abilities the spell has on the stack as it's cast (`Game.spellHasCascade`, rule 702.85a), so a cascade a static or an effect gave it counts (The First Sliver) and a filter's printed-only `hasKeyword` wasn't enough. Aurora Phoenix, from the graveyard.

**A direction to attack in, until your next turn (2026-11-11).** `static:attack-direction-restriction`: the `attack-only-nearest` combat record (`{ direction }`), made by a `restrict` over the game and read by `nearestOpponentRule` beside Pramikon's static (rule 508.1c). Bound with the permanent that chose it, so "the last chosen direction" of one permanent replaces its earlier one while two permanents that disagree leave nobody to attack (the ruling). The rule now refuses a battle whoever protects it ("the nearest opponent … and planeswalkers controlled by that opponent"), Pramikon's too. Teyo, Geometric Tactician.

**Players a creature of a name has dealt combat damage this game (2026-11-11).** `stat:players-dealt-combat-damage-by-name-this-game`: the journal's whole-game `combatDamagedBy` (each player's list of the names of creatures that dealt them combat damage, as named then, whoever controlled them; kept past the round the entries last) and the `combat-damaged-this-game` condition (`byCreatureNamed`), asked of each opponent through `for-each-player` and `of-player`. Gollum, Obsessed Stalker.
**A loyalty ability as a target (2026-11-11).** `target:loyalty-ability`: the `{ kind: "ability" }` target takes `loyalty: true` — an activated ability with a loyalty symbol in its cost (rule 606.2), stamped `GameObject.loyaltyAbility` on the stack object as it is activated; with `sourceTypes: ["planeswalker"]` it is "target loyalty ability of a planeswalker" (Verdant Command, in `counter-abilities.test.ts`). The scaffolder reads the line.

**Attacking as though it had haste (2026-11-11).** `static:attack-as-though-haste`: `StaticAbility.attackAsThoughHaste` (`true`, or `"your-opponents"`), read through the static's `affects` by `attacksAsThoughHaste` in `combat/eligibility.ts`, the one place summoning sickness stops an attack (rules 302.6, 508.1a, 702.10b) — no haste is given, so {T} abilities stay shut (702.10c). Instill Energy and Frenzied Saddlebrute (`attack-as-though-haste.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads both lines. Chaos Lord's "unless it entered this turn" form is its own key still.

**An amount once for each listed ability (2026-11-11).** `static:per-keyword-pump`: `modify-pt-all` takes `perMatchOf: CardFilter[]` — each permanent gets the amount once for each listed filter it matches, all counted before any is changed (rule 611.2c) — with two filter clauses for what `keyword` could not ask: `keywordVariant: "hexproof" | "protection"` (the keyword in any variant, rules 702.11d and 702.16a, printed or granted) and `hasKeyword: "partner"` (partner or "partner with", rule 702.124f). Akroma, Vision of Ixidor (`per-keyword-pump.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the line.
**Warp with life in its cost, and from the graveyard (2026-11-11).** `keyword:warp-from-graveyard-and-life-cost`: `warp` takes `payLife` (life in the warp cost, paid as the spell is cast, rules 601.2h and 119.4) and `fromGraveyard` ("You may cast this card from your graveyard using its warp ability": a `via: "warp"` cast offered from its owner's graveyard, rule 702.185a's cost and exile unchanged, counted as a graveyard cast). The scaffolder now reads warp at all (`Warp {cost}`, `Warp—{cost}, Pay N life.`). Timeline Culler.

**The first targeting ability each turn costs less (2026-11-11).** `cost:first-targeting-ability-cost-reduction`: `abilityCostModification`'s `targeting` now takes `reduceGeneric` (assumed while the ability is only offered when a legal target earns it; an activation only the reduction pays for is offered with just those targets, rules 602.2b and 601.2c before 601.2f) and `firstEachTurn` — used up by an earlier activation of that player's this turn with such a target, which every targeted activation now journals (`activated`, `how: "targeting"`, the target as it was). `becomes-target`'s `abilityOnly: "activated"` is "the target of an activated ability"; the journal's `targeted` entries tell an activated ability from a triggered one. Professor Hojo.

**A target group with different controllers (2026-11-11).** `effect:target-different-controllers`: the `any-number` group's `differentControllers` — each member past the first an `other` slot with `differentController` (`concreteTargetSpecs`), narrowed as they're picked (`slotOptions`, rule 601.2c) and checked again as it resolves by the slot relation's own rule (608.2b). Cloud's Limit Break (tiered, as Thunder Magic).

**A look-and-choose's leftover exiled with counters (2026-11-11).** `effect:look-and-choose-leftover-exile-with-counters`: `look-and-choose` takes `leftoverCounters: { kind, amount }` with `leftover: "exile"` — each card left over gets the counters as it's exiled (kept on the `choose-from-zone` decision, a `secondPick`'s too; not where a `leftoverIf` sent the rest elsewhere). Karn, Scion of Urza (`look-and-choose-leftover-counters.test.ts`), whose −1 is the exile-zone look-and-choose with a `counters` filter; the scaffolder reads both lines.

**A subtype needs a card type it goes with (2026-11-11).** `bug:creature-subtype-needs-creature-type`: the layer-4 fold ends by dropping every subtype that goes with no card type the object has (rule 205.3d; `subtypesFitting` in `characteristics.ts`), so a creature type given for good (`become { addSubtypes, duration: "permanent" }`) is gone while the permanent isn't a creature and back when it is. Archangel Elspeth (`creature-subtype-needs-type.test.ts`); Guide of Souls and Valkyrie's Call, already in the pool, are right now too. The scaffolder reads "Put N +1/+1 counters on target creature. It becomes a [type] in addition to its other types [and gains keyword]."

**Piles for each opponent (2026-11-11).** `mechanic:piles-each-opponent-simultaneous`: `separate-into-piles` takes `each: PlayerScope` — every player of the scope separates their own `objects` (a `permanents`), in turn order; then `chooser` chooses a pile of each; then every chosen pile meets `chosen` in one instruction and every other `rest` (rule 608.2e: each action's choices for everyone, then the action at once). The same `separate-piles` and `choose-pile` decisions, asked once a player; a player with nothing to separate isn't asked, nor a choice between two empty piles (rule 700.3d). Make an Example (`piles-each-opponent.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the card.
**An ability target narrowed by its source (2026-11-11).** `target:ability-source-filter`: the `{ kind: "ability" }` target takes `sourceFilter` (a `CardFilter` on the ability's source, read as `sourceTypes` reads it: the object while it's still the one the ability came from, else its last-known information, rule 113.7a — `abilitySourceAs` in `targeting.ts`) and `otherSource` ("from another … source"). Abstruse Archaic, The Peregrine Dynamo (`ability-source-filter.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads "Copy target … ability you control [from a … source]".

**A cost reduction for each card type shared with the linked exile (2026-11-11).** `cost:reduction-per-type-shared-with-exiled`: the amount `{ cardTypesSharedWith: "exiled-with-source", of: "priced" }` — how many card types the spell being priced shares with the cards exiled with the static's source (rule 607.2a), each type once — as a `costModification`'s `reduceGeneric` (rule 601.2f). Cemetery Prowler (`card-types-shared-with-exiled.test.ts`).

**Copying each of the spells cast this way (2026-11-11).** `effect:copy-spells-cast-this-way`: `copy-all` takes `of` (an object set — only its members still on the stack, `{ thisWay: "cast" }` for the spells a `cast-now` earlier in the resolution cast) and `times` ("twice": each copy its own instruction and its own turn in the order chosen, rule 707.10). Finale of Promise (`copy-spells-cast-this-way.test.ts`): a `cast-now` of its two optional graveyard targets, then, at X 10 or more, the copies.

**A flashback cost that exiles cards from the graveyard (2026-11-11).** `cost:flashback-exile-x-cost`: `flashback.exileFromGraveyard: { count: number | "x", filter? }`, paid on the flashback cast alone through the same payment as `additionalCost.exileFromGraveyard` (never the spell itself; chosen as the cost is paid, rule 601.2h), `"x"` an X announced for that cast though the flashback's mana has none (rules 107.3a, 118.3). Summons of Saruman (`flashback-graveyard-exile-cost.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the line. Left in the same family: `cost:flashback-remove-x-loyalty-among` (Light Up the Night).

**Protection that spares what was already attached (2026-11-11).** `static:protection-spares-own-attachments`: `protection.doesntRemoveAttached` (rule 702.16p) — the Auras and Equipment the static's controller controls that became attached to the creature no later than the static's own Aura (timestamps, rule 613.7e) aren't removed by this instance; an opponent's, a later one, or one moved off and back is (702.16c-d), and another instance of the same quality removes them all. One more spared-set on the 702.16n machinery (`SparedAttachments`, read in `protectionBlocks`), which Equipment legality now names its attachment to — so "doesn't remove Auras" asks that the attachment is an Aura. Benevolent Blessing (`protection-already-attached.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the line.

**"Counter that spell or ability", nothing to pay (2026-11-11).** `effect:counter-targeting-spell-or-ability`: the `ward` effect takes `cost: null` — a becomes-target trigger's counter of whatever did the targeting (rule 701.6a), nobody asked; "for the first time each turn" is the trigger's `firstTimeEachTurn`, already counted per creature over every player's spells and abilities. Kira, Great Glass-Spinner (granted through `grantsTriggered`) and Shimmering Glasskite (`counter-targeting-spell-or-ability.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the effect. Diplomatic Escort's "counter target spell or ability that targets a creature" is another thing, still open under this key.
**"That many" of damage that can't be prevented, on a resolved shield (2026-11-11).** `amount:damage-that-would-be-dealt`: `thenCountsUnprevented` (Jared Carthalion's static) now reaches a `replacement-effect`'s record (`ActiveReplacement.then.countsUnprevented`, the `shield` step's `thenAmount`), so "if damage would be dealt to that creature this turn, prevent that damage and put that many +1/+1 counters on it" gives its counters for unpreventable damage too (rule 615.12, the ruling). Gatta and Luzzu.

**An odd or even number of counters (2026-11-11).** `condition:counter-parity-condition`: the `self-counters` condition takes `parity: "odd" | "even"` in place of `compare` (every kind together with no `counter`; last known once the source has left, as `compare` is), for a static's condition and a resolving "then if". Sab-Sunen, Luxa Embodied.

**"You may have this land enter tapped.
