If you do, …" (2026-11-11).** `replacement:enter-tapped-optional-rider`: an `enters-battlefield` replacement's `mayEnterTapped: { then? }` — asked before the land moves (`askEnterChoice`, rule 614.12) through the `choose-creature-type` short menu unleash uses ("Enter untapped" first, so a seat with no opinion declines), the answer on `enterChoice.enterTapped`; chosen, it is an "enters tapped" of its own (ordered against Spelunking's "enters untapped" by rule 616.1's question) and `then` happens as it enters, not as a trigger (rule 614.1c). No new decision kind, no client change. Mariposa Military Base.
**Only one colour of mana on an ability's X (2026-11-11).** `cost:x-spend-only-color`: `AbilityCost.xSpendOnly` (a colour) — "Spend only black mana on X". `Game.activatedAbilityManaCost` turns what is left of the X in the generic part, after every increase and reduction (rules 601.2f, 602.2b; a reduction takes the cost's other generic mana first), into that many symbols of the colour, so the offer's `maxX`, the activation and the payment price one cost. A colour word (`COLOR_WORD_KEYS`, rule 612.2). Crypt Rats, Crimson Hellkite. NOT built: the same line on a spell (`cost:x-spend-color-restriction` — Consume Spirit, Soul Burn's two colours).

**Attacking your opponents and/or their planeswalkers (2026-11-11).** `condition:filter-attacking-your-opponents`: the filter clause `attackingOpponent` takes `"or-their-planeswalkers"` — a creature attacking an opponent of the filtering player, or a planeswalker one controls (rule 506.2), whoever controls it; never a battle. Roar of Resistance (its trigger, `may` and pump were already there).

**The planar die outside Planechase (2026-11-11).** `mechanic:planar-die`: `roll-dice` takes `sides: "planar"` — "Roll the planar die". Only a Planechase game has a planar die (rule 901.3) and the engine plays none, so the instruction is ignored (rule 101.3; Fractured Powerstone's ruling: no effect in non-Planechase games): no die thrown, no `dice-rolled` event, no draw from the random stream. Fractured Powerstone. NOT built: the Planechase variant itself (`mechanic:planechase`); Ichor Elixir, the key's other card, isn't Commander-legal.
**An additional cost's act that reads X (2026-11-11).** `cost:blight-x-additional-cost`: a spell's `additionalCost.also.do` with an `"x"` amount makes it an X spell without an {X} in its mana cost (`Game.additionalCostActX`): X announced as it's cast (rules 107.3a, 601.2b), the offer's `xCost.maxX` the least of what the act could pay (`costActMaxX`) and the card's `maxX`, the act paid at it once the spell is on the stack (601.2h). A blight of 0 with nothing reading the blighted creature asks nothing. Soul Immolation ("blight X", X no more than the greatest toughness among creatures you control; castable only with a creature, 701.68b). `blight-x-cost.test.ts`.

**A search splitting its finds by count (2026-11-11).** `effect:search-split-count`: no engine change — `search-library.instead` with `pick: { exactly: n }` and no `chooser` is the searcher's own split, every find when there are no more than n (rule 609.3). Viewpoint Synchronization ("put two of them onto the battlefield tapped and the other into your hand": `destination: "hand"`, `instead: { destination: "battlefield", enterTapped: true, pick: { exactly: 2 } }`). The scaffolder reads the two sentences. `search-destination.test.ts`.

**A static that enlarges a surveil (2026-11-11).** `static:surveil-additional-cards`: `StaticAbility.surveilAdditional: N` — its controller's surveils of one or more look at N more cards, which are among those that may go to the graveyard or stay on top in any order (rule 701.25b); not a replacement, a surveil of 0 isn't one (701.25c), several add up; the "may" is always taken (every card looked at may stay where it was). Read in `Game.beginScry`. Enhanced Surveillance (with "Exile this enchantment: Shuffle your graveyard into your library", which was already expressible). The scaffolder reads the line. `surveil-additional.test.ts`.

**"X can't be 0" on a spell's own {X} (2026-11-11).** `cost:x-cant-be-zero`: `CardDefinition.minX` — the floor of the X offered (`castSpellActions`' `leastX`, rule 601.2b) and a refusal in `whyCannotCastSpell`, so a cast that pays no {X} (X is 0 there, rule 107.3b: free, cascade) isn't offered at all (Mind Grind's ruling). Mind Grind (`test/x-cant-be-zero.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the closing sentence and "each opponent reveals … until they reveal N land cards, then puts all cards revealed this way into their graveyard".

**"Target spell can't be countered" (2026-11-11).** `effect:target-spell-cant-be-countered`: the `spell-cant-be-countered` effect (`target`) sets `GameObject.uncounterable` on a spell on the stack — the mark Cavern of Souls' mana leaves, read by `Game.cantBeCountered` and cleared when the spell leaves the stack; an ability isn't a spell and takes none. A counterspell still targets it and does the rest (rules 701.6a, 101.2; Vexing Shusher's ruling). Vexing Shusher (`test/spell-cant-be-countered.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads "target spell can't be countered".

**Hexproof from each of its colors (2026-11-11).** `keyword:hexproof-from-own-colors`: `StaticAbility.hexproofFrom.ownColors` — in the layer-6 fold each affected permanent's own colours (layer 5's answer) join its `hexproofFrom.colors` (rules 702.11d, 702.11g), so "becomes all colors" widens it and a colourless permanent gets none (rule 105.2c); it goes with a loss of hexproof like any other (702.11e). Tam, Mindful First-Year (`test/hexproof-from-own-colors.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the static and "[target] becomes all colors until end of turn".

**A card exiled with it, put into a graveyard as a cost (2026-11-11).** `cost:cost-move-linked-exiled-card`: `AbilityCost.putFromExile` takes `{ count, linked: true, filter? }` beside the processors' number — the cards exiled with the source in its current stint (rule 607.2a), whoever owns them, chosen as the cost is paid with the same `choose-from-zone` (rules 602.2b, 601.2h) and gating activation on there being enough (118.3). No new decision kind, no client change. Shelob, Dread Weaver.

**A spell cast from the hand, the graveyard or the exiled cards you own (2026-11-11).** `effect:cast-now-from-hand-graveyard-or-owned-exile`: `cast-now`'s `from: { zones }` takes `"exile"` beside `"hand"` and `"graveyard"` — every card the effect's controller owns in exile, a face-down one only if they may look at it (rule 406.3b; the player who foretold a card may, 702.143a), read by `ResolutionContext.exiledOwnedSeen`. The same `cast-now` offer, no client change. Kaya the Inexorable.

**All commanders you own, from the command zone and the graveyard (2026-11-11).** `effect:put-all-commanders-into-hand`: `return-from-graveyard`'s `from: "command-zone-and-graveyard"` adds the commanders the controller owns in the command zone to their graveyard's cards, so `filter: { isCommander: true }`, `count: "all"`, `destination: "hand"` moves every one at once with no choice and no cap; a commander leaving the graveyard still gets its owner's rule 903.9b choice, and isn't shuffled in by what follows. No new decision kind, no client change. Campfire.

**A planeswalker's own loyalty abilities twice a turn (2026-11-11).** `static:loyalty-twice-self-only`: `loyaltyActivationsEachTurn` takes `{ times: 2, of: "self" }` beside Oath of Teferi's `2` — "You may activate the loyalty abilities of Urza twice each turn rather than only once" lifts rule 606.3 for the permanent that has it and no other planeswalker (`Game.loyaltyActivationsAllowed` reads the source's own static first). Urza, Lord Protector and its melded face Urza, Planeswalker (the meld pair's other card, The Mightstone and Weakstone, now `meldCard`); the first melded planeswalker, entering with its printed loyalty (306.5b).

**The damage dealt to it this turn, as an amount (2026-11-11).** `amount:damage-dealt-to-object-this-turn`: no engine code — it is Blazing Effigy's `{ damageDealtThisTurn: true, who: "each-player", to: "source" }` without its `by`: all the turn's damage to the source in the stint the ability refers to, read from the journal as a dies trigger resolves (603.10a). Tangled Colony; the scaffolder reads "create X [tokens], where X is the amount of damage dealt to it this turn".

**Revealing a face-down permanent, and what card it is (2026-11-11).** `condition:face-down-is-creature-card`: `look-at-face-down` takes `reveal: true` — "Reveal target face-down permanent": every player is shown it (rule 701.20a; `Game.revealFaceDown`, the reveal an instant or sorcery card already got under 701.40g) and it stays face down — and the `target` condition takes `cardTypes`, the types of the card itself as printed, whatever the permanent is on the battlefield (a face-down permanent is always a 2/2 creature, rule 708.2a; Hauntwoods Shrieker's ruling: only the printed card counts, so March of the Machines makes no artifact card a creature card; a token is no card, 111.6). Hauntwoods Shrieker: reveal, then a `may` `turn-face-up` under that condition. No new decision.
**Every player's hand spells exiled with the permanent (2026-11-11).** `replacement:all-hand-spells-exiled-linked`: `exile-spell-as-it-resolves` takes `anyOwner` ("into **a** graveyard": the spell's owner, whoever cast it — the mark's player, who makes the rule 616.1 choice) and `linked` (the card is exiled with the effect's source in the stint the ability refers to, rule 607.2a, counted by `exiledWithCount`). River Song's Diary (`test/river-songs-diary.test.ts`), whose upkeep half was already `exiled-with-source` + `choose-random-card` + `cast-now`; the scaffolder reads both lines.

**Life lost from radiation, replaced (2026-11-11).** `replacement:life-loss-from-radiation`: `LifeLossReplacement.from: "radiation"` (rule 728.1a — the loss the rad counters' own ability causes, told by its resolving source, a copy's included) and `instead: { effect }` for `would-lose-life` (the loss never happens, rule 614.6; `"x"` is the loss after any doubling, the order the player would take, rule 616.1; not read for damage's loss). Strong, the Brutish Thespian (`test/life-loss-from-radiation.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads the static and "you get N rad counters and put …".

**The defending player of an attacker that has left combat, for a target (2026-11-11).** `bug:defending-player-after-removal`: `targeting.ts`'s `defendingPlayerOf` — behind `"creature-defending-player-controls"` and the `whose: "defending-player"` specs — falls back, while the combat phase lasts, to the source's `attacked` journal entry of this combat (rule 508.5: "the player that creature was attacking before it was removed from combat"), as an effect's `defendingPlayerOf` already did for the whole turn. Armix, Filigree Thrasher (`test/defending-player-after-removal.test.ts`), whose reflexive target is chosen with Armix perhaps dead; the scaffolder reads its line.
**Dice rolled as a permanent enters (2026-11-11).** `replacement:roll-as-enters`: an `enters-battlefield` replacement's `rollAsEnters: { sides, count }` with `counters.amount: "rolled-as-enters"` — its controller rolls as it enters (rules 614.1c, 706), through the one `Game.rollDice`, so roll triggers and "roll an additional die" replacements see it; no dice, no roll. Neverwinter Hydra. Fixed beside it: what a permanent does as it enters (Thief of Blood's counter removal) ran once early, in the look `enterTapOrderAsked` takes before a cast permanent moves, so a cast Thief of Blood entered with no counters. The scaffolder reads the line. `roll-as-enters.test.ts`.

**What a resolution phased out (2026-11-11).** `effect:phased-out-this-way-count`: the `thisWay` kind `"phased-out"`, read off the `permanents-phased-out` event — each permanent as it last was on the battlefield (rule 702.26b: it's treated as though it doesn't exist now), counted by who controlled it then, a token stack as every token, what phased out indirectly (702.26g) included unless a filter leaves it out. Out of Time ("for each creature that phased out this way"; vanishing without a number, rule 702.63b). The scaffolder reads both sentences and a bare "Vanishing". `phased-out-this-way.test.ts`.

**A sacrifice cost with mana value X (2026-11-11).** `cost:sacrifice-cost-mv-x-plus-one`: an activated ability's one-permanent `sacrifice: { filter }` whose filter reads X (`sacrificeCostFilterReadsX`) — X announced though the mana cost has none (rules 602.2b, 601.2b), the ability offered once per X with a permanent to sacrifice (601.2h) and legal targets (601.2c), each offer naming its own `sacrifice.choices`; the named permanent is checked at the action's X. "Mana value X plus 1" on the target is a `{ sum: ["x", 1] }` operand, which already worked. Sidisi, Regent of the Mire. Client: an offer at one X with a sacrifice asks which permanent and sends both. The scaffolder reads the cost and the target. `sacrifice-mana-value-x.test.ts`; a manual check.

**Any player may pay half their life to counter it (2026-11-11).** `effect:any-player-may-pay-to-counter`: Dash Hopes' `each-player-may` of `who: "each-player"` with `ifDid` countering the source already ran the sentence; what was missing was the amount. An `each-player-may`'s `options` take `{ payLifeAmount, text }`, read for each player as they're asked (rule 101.4), a per-player amount (`lifeTotal: "each"`) being their own — "half their life, rounded up" (107.1a), offered only to a player who can pay it (119.4). Temporal Extortion; the scaffolder reads "Any player may pay N life / half their life, rounded up. If a player does, counter ~." No new decision.

**Up to twice X targets (2026-11-11).** `decision:twice-x-target-count`: an "any number of target …" group's `max` / `min` may be a whole multiple of the spell's own X, `{ product: ["x", 2] }`, read by one helper (`xMultiple` in `target.ts`) wherever a bare `"x"` was: it waits for the X announced before the targets (rule 601.2b–c; `groupReadsX`, not a cast-time amount), `specsAtX` fixes it at twice the X cast with (the client judges picks through the same function), the cast offer's `minXByTargetCount` is half the count rounded up (`Game.xGroupOffer`), and a driver of a modal spell fits its picks the same way (`fitModalXGroup`). Erebos's Intervention; the scaffolder reads "up to twice X target …" and "… target cards from graveyards" in an X group. No new decision.

**Damage that causes no loss of life (2026-11-11).** `replacement:damage-doesnt-cause-life-loss`: a `lose-life` prohibition takes `from: "damage"`, which stops rule 120.3a's result and nothing else (`playerLosesNoLifeToDamage`, asked at the one place damage to a player costs life): the damage is dealt in full, so lifelink, infect's poison, commander damage and the monarch's trigger see it, and a loss from an effect or a life payment still happens (`playerCantLoseLife` skips these records). Archon of Coronation, under `condition: { kind: "monarch", who: "you" }`.

**Exiling a face-down permanent to play its card (2026-11-11).** `cost:exile-face-down-permanent-face-up`: no engine code — "Exile a face-down permanent you control face up" is Food Chain's `sacrifice` + `exilesSacrifice` cost over `{ faceDown: true }` (no sacrifice, never turned face up, so neither is seen), and "you may play that card this turn" a `grant-play` of `cards: { paid: "sacrificed" }`, the paid member being the card in its exile stint: cast for its costs at its own timing, a land as the land play, nothing for a token. Primordial Mist; the scaffolder reads the cost's "face up" and the permission after an exile cost.

**The enchanted player draws a card (2026-11-11).** `trigger:enchanted-player-draws`: no engine code — a `draws` trigger's `who: "attached"` on an Aura that enchants a player already means the player it's attached to as the card was drawn (`matchesWhoPlayer`), once per card, and who drew is the `"trigger-controller"` scope. Curse of Fool's Wisdom; the scaffolder now reads the trigger and its "they lose N life and you gain N life".

**All colours, outside the colour identity (2026-11-11).** `static:all-colors-cda`: "is all colors" was already the card's `colors` (a characteristic-defining ability, rule 604.3 — Transguild Courier); what was missing is "this ability doesn't affect its color identity", now `CardDefinition.colorsOutsideIdentity`, read by `colorIdentityOf` alone (rule 903.4: the mana symbols still count). Fallaji Wayfarer, whose "multicolored spells you cast have convoke" is `grantsToSpells` over `{ multicolored: true }` (the scaffolder read it as a subtype).

**One of the permanents chosen, at random (2026-11-11).** `effect:random-choice-among-chosen`: no engine code — "for each opponent, choose a nonland permanent that player controls" is Ultimate Magic: Meteor's `vote` with `choose` and `about: "each-opponent"` (the controller's choices as it resolves, rule 608.2d, none a target), and "destroy one of them chosen at random" is Cinderheart Giant's `choose-permanents` with `random: true` over `filter: { thisWay: "chosen", notKeyword: "indestructible" }` — one seeded draw among what this resolution chose, an indestructible one left out of it while another can be destroyed (Chaos Defiler's ruling; with every one indestructible nothing is destroyed either way). Chaos Defiler; the scaffolder reads the two sentences and a "When ~ enters or dies" trigger as its two abilities.
**A permanent controlled by a player who was dealt combat damage by N or more of a kind (2026-11-11).** `target:player-dealt-combat-damage-by-n-filter`: `CardFilter.controllerTurnHistory` (`{ what: JournalWhat, filter?, atLeast?, window? }`) asks the permanent's controller's journal list through `journalCount`, and the `combat-damaged-by` view is now `oncePerList`: a source counts once per stint for each player it dealt combat damage, as it was then, so a double striker is one Pirate. No new decision kind, no client change. Admiral Beckett Brass.

**Exploring more than once for one (2026-11-11).** `replacement:explore-replacement`: the `would-explore` replacement (`ExploreReplacement`: `filter` for the explorer, `instead: { modify: { times } }`), read by `Game.exploreTimes` as the `explore` effect reaches one instruction for one permanent: it becomes that many whole explorations (rule 701.44a, each its own reveal, choice and `explored` event), flagged `replaced` so the replacement isn't applied to them again, and several multiply (rule 614.5). A permanent that has left is matched as it last was (701.44c). No new decision kind, no client change. Topography Tracker.

**A spell with devoid (2026-11-11).** `filter:has-devoid`: a `devoid` row in `CARD_KEYWORD_READS` (`hasKeyword: "devoid"`), read off the card's printed "Devoid" line as fading, modular and unearth are, since devoid is authored as `colors: []` alone (rule 702.114a) and nothing else tells a devoid spell from another colorless one. All 132 devoid cards in the Oracle snapshot print the line. No engine change beyond the row. Corrupted Crossroads.

**Combat damage to a player become that player's mill (2026-11-11).** `replacement:combat-damage-becomes-mill`: no engine code. A `would-deal-damage` replacement with `instead: { effect }` already binds the damaged player as `"trigger-player"` (`Game.dealDamage` → `runReplacedBy`), so Undead Alchemist is `combat: true`, `source: { subtype: "Zombie", controlledBy: "you" }`, `to: "player"` and a `mill` of `"x"` for `"trigger-player"` (rule 614.1a; the damage never happens, 614.6, so two Alchemists replace it once), and its second ability the `put-into-graveyard` trigger with `from: "library"` (`test/combat-damage-becomes-mill.test.ts`). The scaffolder reads both lines: the replacement, "whenever a [type] card is put into [an opponent's / your / a] graveyard from [anywhere / a library]" as a trigger, and that trigger's "exile that card [and create a token]".

**"Whenever this creature evolves" (2026-11-11).** `trigger:evolves-trigger`: the `evolves` trigger (rule 702.100b: a creature evolves when one or more +1/+1 counters are put on it as a result of its evolve ability resolving), built the way `trains` is — `add-counter` takes `evolve: true`, which `evolve()` writes (and the twelve pool cards that inline the ability now carry), `Game.addCounter` marks the `counter-added` event `evolved` when any counter was put, and the trigger reads that. Once however many counters a replacement makes of the one, never when none is put, never for another counter (Watchful Radstag and its rulings, `test/evolves-trigger.test.ts`). The scaffolder reads "whenever this creature evolves" and its "create a token that's a copy of it".

**A draw become a face-down exile (2026-11-11).** `replacement:draw-replacement-exile-face-down`: no engine code. `would-draw`'s `instead: { effect }` runs an `exile-from-library` with `faceDown: true` and `linked: true` as the permanent's own (rules 614.1a, 614.6, 406.3, 607.2a), one card per draw replaced, nothing with an empty library (614.11); "return all cards exiled with it to their owner's hand and you lose that much life" is `return-exiled-by-source { linked: "hand" }` then a `lose-life` of `{ thisWay: "returned-to-hand" }`. Asmodeus the Archfiend (`test/draw-becomes-exile-face-down.test.ts`). The scaffolder reads both: "if you would draw a card, [one sentence] instead." had lost the sentence's period, and the return line is a template.

**An Aura attached as it's turned face up (2026-11-11).** `replacement:as-turned-face-up-attach-aura`: `asTurnedFaceUp` takes `{ effect }` beside `{ counters }` — anything the card says happens as it's turned face up, done by its controller before a state-based action is checked (rules 708.8, 704.3): Gift of Doom's "you may attach it to a creature" is an `attach` of the source to a host chosen `among`, optional, not targeted (a hexproof creature may be chosen, one the Aura can't enchant may not). The effect waits in `GameState.pendingAsTurnedFaceUp` for the next look at priority, and an Aura listed there isn't put into a graveyard for being unattached (704.5m) until its choice is answered; unattached after that, it is. With it, a turn-face-up cost with a choice still open ("Morph—Sacrifice another creature") now leaves the permanent face down until the choice is answered (`pendingTurnFaceUp`; 702.37e: pay the cost, then turn it), for every morph card. Gift of Doom; the scaffolder reads both "As ~ is turned face up" lines.

**Any target that was dealt damage this turn (2026-11-11).** `effect:target-dealt-damage-this-turn`: a `permanent` target slot's `playerWith: { turnStat }` — what its `orPlayer` part asks of a player, as `filter` asks of a permanent: that turn stat of theirs above 0, read as the target is chosen and as it resolves (rule 608.2b). Needle Drop is `typesAnyOf` of creature, planeswalker and battle with `damagedThisTurnBy: {}`, `orPlayer: "any"` and `playerWith: { turnStat: "damage-taken" }` (the journal's damage total, combat or not). Needle Drop; the scaffolder reads the phrase.

**A copy's starting loyalty (2026-11-11).** `effect:copy-starting-loyalty`: `CopyExceptions.loyalty` — "except … its starting loyalty is 1": a copiable `PtModifier.startingLoyalty` (rule 707.9b), read through `startingLoyaltyOverride` as the copy enters in place of the printed loyalty its card's own enters-with-loyalty replacement says (306.5b), so anything entering as a copy of the copy has it too (Spark Double on the token: 1, plus its own). Jace, Mirror Mage, whose "0: Draw a card and reveal it. Remove … loyalty counters equal to that card's mana value" needed nothing new (`reveal-drawn`, `{ manaValueOf: { thisWay: "drawn" } }`); the scaffolder reads "create a token that's a copy of ~, except it's not legendary [and its starting loyalty is N]".
**A number a permanent notes (2026-11-11).** `condition:noted-life-total`: the `enters-battlefield` replacement's `notes: EffectAmount` ("As this enchantment enters, note your life total", rule 614.1c: read as it enters, no trigger) and the `note` effect ("Then note your life total"), both writing `GameObject.chosenOnEnter` for the stint, the number `{ chosenNumber: true }` already read; "if your life total is greater than or equal to the last noted life total" is the `amounts` condition over it. Sigarda's Splendor (the only card that notes a number).

**The same name as the chosen permanent (2026-11-11).** `condition:filter-same-name-as-chosen`: no engine change. "Choose a land you control. Then search your library for up to two basic land cards which have the same name as the chosen land" is a `choose-permanents` with `remember: true` (its pick is this resolution's `thisWay: "chosen"`, the source gone or not) and a `search-library` filter of `sameNameWith: { thisWay: "chosen" }` (rule 201.2a), behind a `may` with `cost` over `sacrifice-source` for "you may pay {3} and sacrifice it. If you do". Canoptek Wraith; the scaffolder now reads "When ~ deals combat damage to a player".

**A permission's own flash (2026-11-11).** `zone:cast-from-library-top-flash`: `PlayTerms.flash` ("If you cast a spell this way, you may cast it as though it had flash", rules 601.3c, 702.8a), the one timing word a play permission has, so it works from every zone a permission reaches. `Game.whyCannotCast` lets a permission cast begin any time when a permission that fits how it's paid says so (`permissionGivesFlash`), and a cast that leans on it is put under that permission; the same card under another permission (Bolas's Citadel's life payment) keeps a sorcery's timing. Elsha of the Infinite.
**Exchanging control of a spell and a permanent (2026-11-11).** `effect:exchange-control-permanent-and-spell`: the `exchange-control` effect takes a spell on the stack as either object (`Game.exchangeControl` hands it over through `gainControlOfSpell`, one timestamp for both sides, rules 701.12b, 112.2, 110.2b), all or nothing as before (701.12a); `change-targets`' `by: "its-controller"` asks the spell's controller, whoever that is by then, for "the spell's controller may choose new targets for it". Sudden Substitution.

**A meld that enters tapped and attacking (2026-11-11).** `effect:meld-enters-attacking`: the `meld` effect's `tapped` and `attacking` (an `EnterAttacking`, carried as a `MeldEntry` to `Game.meldByEffect`, which hands the melded permanent to `putIntoAttack` as it enters, rule 508.4: never declared, its controller choosing what it attacks). "If ~ and a creature named X are attacking" is the card's: `attacking: true` on `partner` and a `conditional` on the source around the `meld`. Mishra, Claimed by Gix, with Mishra, Lost to Phyrexia (its "choose three" a `modal` with `minModes: 3`); Phyrexian Dragon Engine gained its `meldCard`.

**Each instance of a step or phase skipped this turn (2026-11-11).** `effect:skip-steps-this-turn`: the `skip-step` effect's `eachThisTurn` (rules 500.11, 614.10) — `TurnState.skipsEachThisTurn` for draw and upkeep steps and main phases (`step: "main"`, rule 505.1: `enterStep` passes a skipped main phase with a `step-skipped` event and no count in `mainPhases`), `TurnState.noCombat` for combat phases; lasting the turn, so a phase added after it resolves is skipped too. The player's choice is `as-player` of `"active-player"` over a `modal`, Hall of Gemstone's shape: no new decision. Fatespinner.
